  'm': 'Random Monster Spawn'
};

const ItemIcons = {
  beartrap: function(ctx, x, y, size) {
    const r = size * 0.45;
    const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(x - r*0.3, y - r*0.3, r*0.1, x, y, r);
    g.addColorStop(0, '#bbb');
    g.addColorStop(0.5, '#777');
    g.addColorStop(1, '#333');
    ctx.fillStyle = g;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(x, y, r, 0, Math.PI*2);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.strokeStyle = '#555';
    ctx.lineWidth = size*0.06;
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(x, y, r*0.55, 0, Math.PI*2);
    ctx.strokeStyle = '#888';
    ctx.lineWidth = size*0.03;
    ctx.setLineDash([size*0.08, size*0.08]);
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.setLineDash([]);
    const g2 = ctx.createRadialGradient(x - r*0.2, y - r*0.2, 0, x, y, r*0.4);
    g2.addColorStop(0, '#ddd');
    g2.addColorStop(1, '#999');
    ctx.fillStyle = g2;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(x, y, r*0.4, 0, Math.PI*2);
    ctx.fill();
  },
  landmine: function(ctx, x, y, size) {
    const r = size * 0.44;
    const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(x - r*0.25, y - r*0.25, r*0.05, x, y, r);
    g.addColorStop(0, '#00cc33');
    g.addColorStop(0.6, '#006611');
    g.addColorStop(1, '#003308');
    ctx.fillStyle = g;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(x, y, r, 0, Math.PI*2);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.strokeStyle = '#008822';
    ctx.lineWidth = size*0.06;
    ctx.stroke();
    for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
      const a = (i / 8) * Math.PI * 2;
      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0,255,50,0.2)';
      ctx.lineWidth = size*0.02;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(x + Math.cos(a)*r*0.3, y + Math.sin(a)*r*0.3);
      ctx.lineTo(x + Math.cos(a)*r*0.85, y + Math.sin(a)*r*0.85);
      ctx.stroke();
    }
    ctx.fillStyle = '#222';
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(x, y, r*0.2, 0, Math.PI*2);
    ctx.fill();
  },
  crucifix: function(ctx, x, y, size) {
    const w = size * 0.12;
    const h = size * 0.45;
    const crossW = size * 0.32;
    ctx.fillStyle = '#7a4620';
    ctx.fillRect(x - w, y - size*0.08 - h*0.75, w*2, h);
    ctx.fillRect(x - crossW, y - size*0.08, crossW*2, w*2);
    const gv = ctx.createLinearGradient(x, y - size*0.08 - h*0.75, x + w*2, y - size*0.08 - h*0.75);
    gv.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,255,255,0.15)');
    gv.addColorStop(0.5, 'rgba(255,255,255,0)');
    gv.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,0.2)');
    ctx.fillStyle = gv;
    ctx.fillRect(x - w, y - size*0.08 - h*0.75, w*2, h);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#5a2d0c';
    ctx.fillRect(x - w + size*0.02, y - size*0.08 - h*0.72, w*1.5, h*0.9);
    ctx.fillRect(x - crossW + size*0.02, y - size*0.06, crossW*1.5, w*1.2);
  },
  bloxy_cola: function(ctx, x, y, size) {
    const w = size * 0.32;
    const h = size * 0.42;
    const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(x, y - h, x, y + h);
    g.addColorStop(0, '#d47a30');
    g.addColorStop(0.4, '#b05e20');
    g.addColorStop(0.6, '#8a3f10');
    g.addColorStop(1, '#d47a30');
    ctx.fillStyle = g;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(x - w, y - h*0.4);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x - w, y - h, x - w*0.5, y - h);
    ctx.lineTo(x + w*0.5, y - h);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x + w, y - h, x + w, y - h*0.4);
    ctx.lineTo(x + w, y + h*0.7);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x + w, y + h, x + w*0.3, y + h);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x, y + h*1.1, x - w*0.3, y + h);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x - w, y + h, x - w, y + h*0.7);
    ctx.closePath();
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.3)';
    ctx.lineWidth = size*0.04;
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.fillStyle = '#ddd';
    ctx.fillRect(x - w*0.55, y - h*0.9, w*1.1, h*0.18);
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.4)';
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(x + w*0.25, y + h*0.15, size*0.05, 0, Math.PI*2);
    ctx.fill();
  },
  cake: function(ctx, x, y, size) {
    const w = size * 0.38;
    const h = size * 0.42;
    ctx.fillStyle = '#f8d0e0';
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(x - w, y + h);
    ctx.lineTo(x - w, y - h*0.5);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x - w, y - h, x - w*0.5, y - h);
    ctx.lineTo(x + w*0.7, y - h);
    ctx.lineTo(x + w*0.7, y + h);
    ctx.closePath();
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.2)';
    ctx.lineWidth = size*0.03;
    ctx.stroke();
    const stripeH = h / 4;
    ctx.fillStyle = '#ff99cc';
    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
      if (i % 2 === 1) ctx.fillStyle = '#fff';
      else ctx.fillStyle = '#ff99cc';
      ctx.beginPath();
      const sy = y + h - (i + 1) * stripeH;
      ctx.moveTo(x - w, sy);
      ctx.lineTo(x - w, sy + stripeH);
      ctx.lineTo(x + w*0.7, sy + stripeH);
      ctx.lineTo(x + w*0.7, sy);
      ctx.closePath();
      ctx.fill();
    }
    ctx.fillStyle = '#ffdd44';
    ctx.fillRect(x - size*0.02, y - h - size*0.06, size*0.05, h*0.25);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#ff8800';
    ctx.fillRect(x - size*0.01, y - h - size*0.02, size*0.02, size*0.06);
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,100,0.7)';
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(x, y - h - size*0.02, size*0.04, 0, Math.PI*2);
    ctx.fill();
  },
  witches_brew: function(ctx, x, y, size) {
    const w = size * 0.3;
    const h = size * 0.4;
    ctx.fillStyle = '#1a4422';
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(x - w*0.3, y - h);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x, y - h*1.15, x + w*0.3, y - h);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x + w, y - h*0.5, x + w, y + h*0.3);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x + w*0.8, y + h, x, y + h);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x - w*0.8, y + h, x - w, y + h*0.3);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x - w, y - h*0.5, x - w*0.3, y - h);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.strokeStyle = '#0d3311';
    ctx.lineWidth = size*0.05;
    ctx.stroke();
    const lg = ctx.createLinearGradient(x, y - h*0.3, x, y + h*0.6);
    lg.addColorStop(0, '#44ff44');
    lg.addColorStop(0.6, '#228833');
    lg.addColorStop(1, '#0d3311');
    ctx.fillStyle = lg;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(x - w*0.7, y - h*0.1);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x - w*0.7, y + h*0.5, x, y + h*0.6);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x + w*0.7, y + h*0.5, x + w*0.7, y - h*0.1);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x + w*0.5, y - h*0.3, x, y - h*0.25);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x - w*0.5, y - h*0.3, x - w*0.7, y - h*0.1);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.25)';
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(x + w*0.3, y + h*0.05, size*0.04, 0, Math.PI*2);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle = '#8b6914';
    ctx.fillRect(x - w*0.35, y - h*1.1, w*0.7, h*0.15);
  },
  medkit: function(ctx, x, y, size) {
    const s = size * 0.38;
    ctx.fillStyle = '#fff';
    ctx.fillRect(x - s, y - s, s*2, s*2);
    ctx.strokeStyle = '#ff4444';
    ctx.lineWidth = size*0.08;
    ctx.strokeRect(x - s, y - s, s*2, s*2);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#ff4444';
    ctx.fillRect(x - size*0.08, y - s*0.6, size*0.16, s*1.2);
    ctx.fillRect(x - s*0.6, y - size*0.08, s*1.2, size*0.16);
  },
  apple: function(ctx, x, y, size) {
    const r = size * 0.38;
    const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(x - r*0.3, y - r*0.3, r*0.05, x, y, r);
    g.addColorStop(0, '#ff6655');
    g.addColorStop(0.6, '#cc2222');
    g.addColorStop(1, '#881111');
    ctx.fillStyle = g;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.ellipse(x, y, r, r*1.05, 0, 0, Math.PI*2);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(x, y - r*0.15, r*0.2, 0, Math.PI, true);
    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.3)';
    ctx.lineWidth = size*0.03;
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.strokeStyle = '#5a3a1a';
    ctx.lineWidth = size*0.05;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(x, y - r*1.05);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x + size*0.03, y - r*1.25, x + size*0.02, y - r*1.35);
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.fillStyle = '#338822';
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(x + size*0.02, y - r*1.3);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x + size*0.12, y - r*1.25, x + size*0.15, y - r*1.1);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x + size*0.1, y - r*1.1, x + size*0.02, y - r*1.2);
    ctx.fill();
  },
  noisemaker: function(ctx, x, y, size) {
    // Golf club pointing upward: shaft (brown), hosel (blue+red), face (white)
    const shaftW = Math.max(2, size * 0.08);
    const shaftH = size * 0.42;
    const faceW = Math.max(4, size * 0.28);
    const faceH = Math.max(2.5, size * 0.10);
    const hoselW = Math.max(2, size * 0.12);
    const hoselH = Math.max(2, size * 0.08);
    // Shaft: brown gradient
    const shaftGrad = ctx.createLinearGradient(x, y + shaftH * 0.35, x, y - shaftH * 0.65);
    shaftGrad.addColorStop(0, '#5a3a1a');
    shaftGrad.addColorStop(0.5, '#8b5e3c');
    shaftGrad.addColorStop(1, '#5a3a1a');
    ctx.fillStyle = shaftGrad;
    ctx.fillRect(x - shaftW / 2, y + shaftH * 0.35, shaftW, -shaftH);
    // Face (club head): white, at top of shaft, horizontal
    ctx.fillStyle = '#f0f0f0';
    ctx.strokeStyle = '#ccc';
    ctx.lineWidth = 0.8;
    ctx.fillRect(x - faceW / 2, y - shaftH * 0.65 - faceH, faceW, faceH);
    ctx.strokeRect(x - faceW / 2, y - shaftH * 0.65 - faceH, faceW, faceH);
    // Hosel: half blue half red
    ctx.fillStyle = '#4488ff';
    ctx.fillRect(x - hoselW / 2, y - shaftH * 0.65, hoselW, hoselH / 2);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#ff4444';
    ctx.fillRect(x - hoselW / 2, y - shaftH * 0.65 + hoselH / 2, hoselW, hoselH / 2);
  },
  totem: function(ctx, x, y, size) {
    var w = size * 0.22, h = size * 0.45;
    ctx.fillStyle = '#ffd700';
    ctx.fillRect(x - w/2, y - h/2 + size*0.05, w, h);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#44ff44';
    ctx.fillRect(x - w*0.6, y - h*0.3, w*0.5, h*0.15);
    ctx.fillRect(x + w*0.1, y - h*0.3, w*0.5, h*0.15);
    ctx.strokeStyle = '#cc9900';
    ctx.lineWidth = 0.5;
    ctx.strokeRect(x - w/2, y - h/2 + size*0.05, w, h);
  },
  shield_potion: function(ctx, x, y, size) {
    var w = size * 0.32, h = size * 0.42;
    var g = ctx.createLinearGradient(x, y - h, x, y + h);
    g.addColorStop(0, '#224488');
    g.addColorStop(0.4, '#4488ff');
    g.addColorStop(0.6, '#1a3366');
    g.addColorStop(1, '#224488');
    ctx.fillStyle = g;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(x - w, y - h*0.4);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x - w, y - h, x - w*0.5, y - h);
    ctx.lineTo(x + w*0.5, y - h);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x + w, y - h, x + w, y - h*0.4);
    ctx.lineTo(x + w, y + h*0.7);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x + w, y + h, x + w*0.3, y + h);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x, y + h*1.1, x - w*0.3, y + h);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x - w, y + h, x - w, y + h*0.7);
    ctx.closePath();
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle = '#8b6914';
    ctx.fillRect(x - w*0.4, y - h*0.95, w*0.8, h*0.16);
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(150,200,255,0.4)';
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(x + w*0.25, y + h*0.15, size*0.05, 0, Math.PI*2);
    ctx.fill();
  },
  smoke_bomb: function(ctx, x, y, size) {
    var r = size * 0.35;
    ctx.fillStyle = '#4a5a2a';
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.roundRect ? ctx.roundRect(x - r, y - r*0.8, r*2, r*1.6, 3) : ctx.fillRect(x - r, y - r*0.8, r*2, r*1.6);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.strokeStyle = '#2a3a0a';
    ctx.lineWidth = 0.8;
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.fillStyle = '#ddd';
    ctx.fillRect(x - size*0.03, y - r*0.85, size*0.06, r*0.5);
  },
  phase: function(ctx, x, y, size) {
    var r = size * 0.33;
    ctx.fillStyle = '#6633aa';
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(x, y - r*0.7);
    ctx.bezierCurveTo(x - r, y - r*0.5, x - r, y + r*0.3, x, y + r*0.8);
    ctx.bezierCurveTo(x + r, y + r*0.3, x + r, y - r*0.5, x, y - r*0.7);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.strokeStyle = '#aa66ff';
    ctx.lineWidth = 1;
    ctx.stroke();
    for (var ti = 0; ti < 3; ti++) {
      var tang = (ti / 3) * Math.PI * 2 + Math.sin(Date.now()*0.005) * 0.3;
      var tlx = x + Math.cos(tang) * r * 0.8;
      var tly = y + Math.sin(tang) * r * 0.6;
      ctx.strokeStyle = '#9955dd';
      ctx.lineWidth = 0.4;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(tlx, tly);
      ctx.lineTo(tlx + Math.cos(tang) * r * 0.4, tly + Math.sin(tang) * r * 0.4);
      ctx.stroke();
    }
  },
  lucky_charm: function(ctx, x, y, size) {
    var r = size * 0.15;
    ctx.fillStyle = '#ffd700';
    ctx.fillRect(x - r*1.2, y + r*0.6, r*2.4, r*1.6);
    ctx.strokeStyle = '#aa8800';
    ctx.lineWidth = 0.5;
    ctx.strokeRect(x - r*1.2, y + r*0.6, r*2.4, r*1.6);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#ffd700';
    ctx.fillRect(x - size*0.03, y - r*0.8, size*0.06, r*1.4);
    for (var ci = 0; ci < 3; ci++) {
      var ciy = y - r*0.6 + ci * r*0.6;
      ctx.fillRect(x - size*0.03, ciy, size*0.06, r*0.2);
    }
    ctx.fillStyle = '#ccaa00';
    ctx.fillRect(x - r*0.8, y + r*0.8, r*0.6, r*0.6);
    ctx.fillRect(x + r*0.2, y + r*0.8, r*0.6, r*0.6);
  },
  genes_hand: function(ctx, x, y, size) {
    var r = size * 0.28;
    ctx.fillStyle = '#cc88ff';
    // Palm
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(x, y + r*0.1, r*0.7, 0, Math.PI*2);
    ctx.fill();
    // Fingers
    for (var fi = 0; fi < 5; fi++) {
      var fx = x - r*0.5 + fi * r*0.25;
      var fy = y - r*0.5;
      ctx.fillStyle = '#cc88ff';
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.roundRect ? ctx.roundRect(fx - r*0.08, fy - r*0.3, r*0.16, r*0.5, r*0.04) : ctx.fillRect(fx - r*0.08, fy - r*0.3, r*0.16, r*0.5);
      ctx.fill();
    }
    ctx.strokeStyle = '#aa66dd';
    ctx.lineWidth = 0.5;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(x, y + r*0.1, r*0.7, 0, Math.PI*2);
    ctx.stroke();
  },
  healing_aura: function(ctx, x, y, size) {
    var r = size * 0.28;
    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(68,255,68,0.6)';
    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(x, y, r, 0, Math.PI*2);
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(68,255,68,0.2)';
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(x, y, r, 0, Math.PI*2);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle = '#44ff44';
    ctx.font = (size*0.35) + 'px monospace';
    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
    ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
    ctx.fillText('+', x - r*0.7, y - r*0.6);
    ctx.fillText('+', x + r*0.7, y - r*0.6);
    ctx.fillText('+', x - r*0.8, y + r*0.5);
    ctx.fillText('+', x + r*0.8, y + r*0.5);
    ctx.fillText('+', x, y - r);
    ctx.textAlign = 'start';
    ctx.textBaseline = 'alphabetic';
  }
};

const Rarities = {
  common:     { name: 'Common', dropChance: 0.55 },
  rare:       { name: 'Rare', dropChance: 0.25 },
  super_rare: { name: 'Super Rare', dropChance: 0.15 },
  epic:       { name: 'Epic', dropChance: 0.5 }
};

function weightedRandomRarity() {
  const entries = Object.entries(Rarities);
  const totalWeight = entries.reduce((sum, [, r]) => sum + r.dropChance, 0);
  let r = Math.random() * totalWeight;
  for (const [key, rarity] of entries) {
    r -= rarity.dropChance;
    if (r <= 0) return key;
  }
  return entries[entries.length - 1][0];
}

function getRandomItemByRarity(game) {
  var eligible = Object.entries(Items).filter(function(e) { return e[1].giveOnRoundEnd; });
  if (game.playerCount < 2) {
    eligible = eligible.filter(function(e) { return e[0] !== 'genes_hand' && e[0] !== 'healing_aura'; });
  }
  if (eligible.length === 0) return null;
  let rarity = weightedRandomRarity();
  let filtered = eligible.filter(([, it]) => it.rarity === rarity);
  if (filtered.length === 0) {
    if (rarity === 'super_rare') { rarity = 'rare'; filtered = eligible.filter(([, it]) => it.rarity === rarity); }
    if (filtered.length === 0 && rarity === 'rare') { rarity = 'common'; filtered = eligible.filter(([, it]) => it.rarity === rarity); }
  }
  if (filtered.length === 0) filtered = eligible;
  const idx = Math.floor(Math.random() * filtered.length);
  return filtered[idx][0];
}

function weightedRandom(types) {
  const entries = Object.entries(types);
  const totalWeight = entries.reduce((sum, [, t]) => sum + t.weight, 0);
  let r = Math.random() * totalWeight;
  for (const [key, t] of entries) {
    r -= t.weight;
    if (r <= 0) return key;
  }
  return entries[entries.length - 1][0];
}

// ============================================================
// MAP PARSER
// ============================================================
function parseMap(text) {
  const lines = text.split('\n').filter(l => l.length > 0);
  if (lines.length === 0) return null;
  let cleanLines = [];
  let cols = 0;
  for (const line of lines) {
    const clean = line.replace(/ /g, '');
    if (clean.length > 0) {
      cleanLines.push(clean);
      if (clean.length > cols) cols = clean.length;
    }
  }
  if (cleanLines.length === 0) return null;
  // Pad rows to same width
  for (let i = 0; i < cleanLines.length; i++) {
    while (cleanLines[i].length < cols) cleanLines[i] += 'G';
  }
   const validChars = new Set(['O','C','G','d','A','P','M','F','U','W','S','T','m']);
  const tiles = [];
  const doors = [];
   const artifacts = [];
   const waypoints = [];
   const randomSpawns = [];
   let altarPositions = [];
   let playerSpawn = null;
   let monsterSpawn = null;
   for (let y = 0; y < cleanLines.length; y++) {
     const row = [];
     for (let x = 0; x < cleanLines[y].length; x++) {
       const ch = cleanLines[y][x];
       if (!validChars.has(ch)) {
         console.warn('Invalid char:', ch, 'at', x, y);
         row.push('C');
         continue;
       }
       row.push(ch);
       if (ch === 'd') doors.push({ x, y });
       if (ch === 'F') artifacts.push({ x, y });
       if (ch === 'U') waypoints.push({ x, y });
        if (ch === 'A') altarPositions.push({ x, y });
       if (ch === 'P') playerSpawn = { x, y };
       if (ch === 'M') monsterSpawn = { x, y };
       if (ch === 'm') randomSpawns.push({ x, y });
     }
     tiles.push(row);
   }
   return {
      tiles, doors, artifacts, waypoints, randomSpawns,
      altarPositions, playerSpawn, monsterSpawn,
      width: cols, height: cleanLines.length
   };
}

// ============================================================
// PARTICLE SYSTEM
// ============================================================
class Particle {
  constructor(x, y, vx, vy, life, color) {
    this.x = x; this.y = y;
    this.vx = vx; this.vy = vy;
    this.life = life; this.maxLife = life;
    this.color = color;
  }
  update(dt) {
    this.x += this.vx * dt;
    this.y += this.vy * dt;
    this.life -= dt;
  }
  get alive() { return this.life > 0; }
  render(ctx, cx, cy) {
    const alpha = this.life / this.maxLife;
    ctx.globalAlpha = alpha;
    ctx.fillStyle = this.color;
    ctx.fillRect(this.x - cx, this.y - cy, 2, 2);
    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
  }
}

// ============================================================
// DOOR ENTITY
// ============================================================
class DoorEntity {
  constructor(x, y) {
    this.tileX = x; this.tileY = y;
    this.open = false;
    this.animating = false;
    this.animTimer = 0;
    this.animDuration = 0.4;
    this.debounceTimer = 0;
    this.animProgress = 0;
    this.cooldown = false;
    this.groupMembers = null;
    this.lockCooldown = 0;
  }
  hasGroup() { return this.groupMembers !== null && this.groupMembers.length > 0; }
  update(dt) {
    if (this.animating) {
      this.animTimer += dt;
      if (this.open) this.animProgress = Math.min(1, this.animTimer / this.animDuration);
      else this.animProgress = Math.max(0, 1 - this.animTimer / this.animDuration);
      if (this.animTimer >= this.animDuration) {
        this.animating = false;
        this.debounceTimer = 0.3;
        this.animProgress = this.open ? 1 : 0;
      }
    }
    if (this.debounceTimer > 0) {
      this.debounceTimer -= dt;
      if (this.debounceTimer <= 0) this.debounceTimer = 0;
    }
    if (this.lockCooldown > 0) { this.lockCooldown -= dt; if (this.lockCooldown <= 0) this.lockCooldown = 0; }
  }
  toggle(skipPropagation) {
    if (this.animating || this.debounceTimer > 0) return;
    if (typeof game !== 'undefined' && game.lockedDoor && game.lockedDoor.tiles.some(function(t) { return t.doorRef === this; })) return;
    if (!skipPropagation && this.groupMembers) {
      for (let i = 0; i < this.groupMembers.length; i++) {
        const member = this.groupMembers[i];
        if (member !== this) member.toggle(true);
      }
    }
    this.open = !this.open;
    this.animating = true;
    this.animTimer = 0;
  }
  forceOpen() {
    if (!this.open && !this.animating) {
    if (typeof game !== 'undefined' && game.lockedDoor && game.lockedDoor.tiles.some(function(t) { return t.doorRef === this; })) return;
      if (this.groupMembers) {
        for (let i = 0; i < this.groupMembers.length; i++) {
          const member = this.groupMembers[i];
          if (member !== this && !member.open && !member.animating) member.toggle(true);
        }
      }
      this.toggle(true);
    }
  }
  get solid() { return !this.open && !this.animating; }
}

// ============================================================
// ARTIFACT ENTITY
// ============================================================
class ArtifactEntity {
  constructor(x, y) {
    this.tileX = x; this.tileY = y;
    this.collected = false;
    this.glowPhase = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
    this.displayName = 'Artifact';
    this.isFake = false;
    this.decoyRef = null;
  }
  update(dt) { this.glowPhase += dt * 3; }
  render(ctx, tx, ty, ts, camX, camY) {
    if (this.collected) return;
    const cx = tx * ts + ts / 2 - camX;
    const cy = ty * ts + ts / 2 - camY;
    const glow = 0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(this.glowPhase);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#00cccc';
    ctx.globalAlpha = 0.3 + glow * 0.7;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(cx, cy, ts * 0.3, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
    ctx.fillStyle = '#00cccc';
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(cx, cy, ts * 0.12, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
  }
}

// ============================================================
// PLAYER
// ============================================================
class Player {
  constructor(tileX, tileY, playerIndex, name, playerClass) {
    this.playerIndex = playerIndex || 0;
    this.name = name || ('P' + (this.playerIndex + 1));
    this.tileX = tileX;
    this.tileY = tileY;
    this.px = tileX * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2;
    this.py = tileY * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2;
    this.speed = 140;
    this.baseSpeed = 140;
    this.hp = 100;
    this.maxHp = 100;
    this.alive = true;
    this.interacting = false;
    this.interactTimer = 0;
    this.interactTarget = null;
    this.interactType = null;
    this.invulnTimer = 0;
    this.playerClass = playerClass || 'oracle';
    this.abilityCooldown = 0;
    this.abilityActive = false;
    this.abilityTimer = 0;
    this.keybinds = preGameKeybinds[playerIndex]
      ? { ...preGameKeybinds[playerIndex] }
      : { ...DEFAULT_KEYBINDS[playerIndex] };
    this.inventory = [null, null, null, null, null];
    this.selectedSlot = 0;
    this.useTimer = 0;
    this.using = false;
    this.useTapPending = false;
    this.speedItemTimer = 0;
    this.speedItemMult = 1;
    this.commandYellMult = 0;
    this.commandYellTimer = 0;
    this.invisible = false;
    this.invisibleTimer = 0;
    this.timeTravelUsed = false;
    this.duplicatedSlots = new Set();
    this.lastDx = 0;
    this.lastDy = 0;
    this.initialSpawnDone = false;
    this.wiredGirlUsed = false;
    this.rerrolerUsed = false;
    this.menuOpen = false;
    this.menuSelectedIndex = 0;
    this.menuUseTimer = 0;
    this.menuUseTapPending = false;
    this.phasing = false;
    this.phasingTimer = 0;
    this.helpfulpriestUsed = false;
    this.shieldCharges = 0;
    this.luckyCharmActive = false;
    this.allyWarpUses = 0;
    this.stationaryTimer = 0;
    this.healingAuraSlow = false;
    this.poisonTimer = 0;
    this.poisonTick = 0;
  }

  update(dt, game) {
    if (this.invulnTimer > 0) this.invulnTimer -= dt;
    if (!this.alive) return;
    if (this.abilityCooldown > 0) this.abilityCooldown -= dt;
    if (this.abilityActive) {
      this.abilityTimer -= dt;
      if (this.abilityTimer <= 0) {
        this.abilityActive = false;
        if (this.playerClass === 'vex') { this.phasing = false; ejectPlayerFromWall(this, game); }
      }
    }
    if (this.phasing) {
      this.phasingTimer -= dt;
      if (this.phasingTimer <= 0) { this.phasing = false; ejectPlayerFromWall(this, game); }
    }
    if (this.speedItemTimer > 0) {
      this.speedItemTimer -= dt;
      if (this.speedItemTimer <= 0) {
        this.speedItemTimer = 0;
        this.speedItemMult = 1;
      }
    }
    if (this.commandYellTimer > 0) {
      this.commandYellTimer -= dt;
      if (this.commandYellTimer <= 0) { this.commandYellMult = 0; this.commandYellTimer = 0; }
    }
    if (this.invisibleTimer > 0) {
      this.invisibleTimer -= dt;
      if (this.invisibleTimer <= 0) this.invisible = false;
    }
    let finalSpeed = this.baseSpeed;
    if (this.abilityActive && this.playerClass === 'scout') finalSpeed *= 1.5;
    if (this.speedItemTimer > 0) finalSpeed = Math.max(finalSpeed, this.baseSpeed * this.speedItemMult);
    if (this.commandYellTimer > 0) finalSpeed += this.baseSpeed * this.commandYellMult;
    this.speed = finalSpeed;
    for (var zz = 0; zz < game.activeZones.length; zz++) {
      var zone = game.activeZones[zz];
      var dzx = this.px - zone.x, dzy = this.py - zone.y;
      if (Math.sqrt(dzx * dzx + dzy * dzy) <= zone.radius) { this.speed += zone.bonusSpeed; break; }
    }
    if (this.healingAuraSlow) {
      var inAura = false;
      for (var ai = 0; ai < game.activeAuras.length; ai++) {
        if (game.activeAuras[ai].player === this) { inAura = true; break; }
      }
      if (inAura) { this.speed = this.baseSpeed * 0.2; }
      else { this.healingAuraSlow = false; }
    }
    let dx = 0, dy = 0;
    const kb = this.keybinds;
    if (keys[kb.up]) dy -= 1;
    if (keys[kb.down]) dy += 1;
    if (keys[kb.left]) dx -= 1;
    if (keys[kb.right]) dx += 1;
    if (dx !== 0 && dy !== 0) { dx *= 0.707; dy *= 0.707; }
    const spd = this.speed;
    const nx = this.px + dx * spd * dt;
    const ny = this.py + dy * spd * dt;
    if (this.canMoveTo(nx, this.py, game)) this.px = nx;
    if (this.canMoveTo(this.px, ny, game)) this.py = ny;
    if (dx !== 0 || dy !== 0) { this.lastDx = dx; this.lastDy = dy; this.stationaryTimer = 0; }
    else { this.stationaryTimer += dt; }
    this.tileX = Math.floor(this.px / TILE_SIZE);
    this.tileY = Math.floor(this.py / TILE_SIZE);
    this.checkInteraction(game);
    if (keysJustPressed[kb.interact] && this.interactTarget && this.interactType === 'door') {
      this.interactTarget.toggle();
      keysJustPressed[kb.interact] = false;
    }
    if (this.interacting && this.interactType !== 'door') {
      this.interactTimer += this.playerClass === 'teevee' && this.interactType === 'artifact' ? dt * 1.5 : dt;
      game.updateInteraction(this);
    }
    if (!keys[kb.interact]) { this.interacting = false; this.interactTimer = 0; }
    // Hotbar: tap to cycle, hold to use
    if (keysJustPressed[kb.use]) {
      keysJustPressed[kb.use] = false;
      this.useTapPending = true;
      this.useTimer = 0;
    }

    if (keys[kb.use]) {
      if (this.useTapPending) {
        this.useTimer += dt;
        if (this.useTimer >= 0.3) {
          this.useTapPending = false;
          this.useTimer = 0;
        }
      } else if (this.inventory[this.selectedSlot]) {
        this.useTimer += dt;
        if (this.useTimer >= 1) {
          game.useItem(this);
          this.useTimer = 0;
        }
      }
    }

    if (!keys[kb.use]) {
      if (this.useTapPending) {
        let next = this.selectedSlot;
        let tries = 0;
        do {
          next = (next + 1) % 5;
          tries++;
        } while (tries < 5 && !this.inventory[next]);
        this.selectedSlot = next;
      }
      this.useTapPending = false;
      this.useTimer = 0;
      this.using = false;
    }
    if (this.playerClass === 'helpfulpriest') {
      if (!this.alive) { this.menuOpen = false; return; }
      if (this.helpfulpriestUsed) { this.menuOpen = false; return; }
      if (keysJustPressed[kb.ability]) {
        keysJustPressed[kb.ability] = false;
        if (!this.menuOpen) {
          this.menuOpen = true;
          this.menuSelectedIndex = 0;
          this.menuUseTapPending = false;
          this.menuUseTimer = 0;
        } else {
          this.menuUseTapPending = true;
          this.menuUseTimer = 0;
        }
      }
      if (keys[kb.ability]) {
        if (this.menuUseTapPending) {
          this.menuUseTimer += dt;
          if (this.menuUseTimer >= 0.3) { this.menuUseTapPending = false; this.menuUseTimer = 0; }
        } else {
          this.menuUseTimer += dt;
          if (this.menuUseTimer >= 1.0 && !this.helpfulpriestUsed) {
            applyPriestEffect(game, this);
            this.helpfulpriestUsed = true;
            this.menuOpen = false;
            this.menuUseTimer = 0;
          }
        }
      }
      if (!keys[kb.ability]) {
        if (this.menuUseTapPending) {
          if (this.menuSelectedIndex >= game.playerCount - 1) {
            this.menuOpen = false;
          } else {
            this.menuSelectedIndex++;
          }
        }
        this.menuUseTapPending = false;
        this.menuUseTimer = 0;
      }
    }
  }

  hasSpace() { return this.inventory.some(s => !s); }
  countEmptySlots() { return this.inventory.filter(s => !s).length; }
  addToInventory(item) {
    for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
      if (!this.inventory[i]) { this.inventory[i] = item; return true; }
    }
    return false;
  }
  getEquippedItem() { return this.inventory[this.selectedSlot]; }
  removeEquipped() { this.inventory[this.selectedSlot] = null; this.duplicatedSlots.delete(this.selectedSlot); }

  canMoveTo(px, py, game) {
    const margin = 4;
    const checks = [
      { x: px - margin, y: py - margin },
      { x: px + margin, y: py - margin },
      { x: px - margin, y: py + margin },
      { x: px + margin, y: py + margin }
    ];
    for (const c of checks) {
      const tx = Math.floor(c.x / TILE_SIZE);
      const ty = Math.floor(c.y / TILE_SIZE);
      if (tx < 0 || ty < 0 || tx >= game.mapWidth || ty >= game.mapHeight) return false;
      const tile = game.tiles[ty][tx];
      if (tile === 'O' || tile === 'W') {
        if (!(this.phasing && this.playerClass === 'vex')) {
          var isPhased = false;
          for (var pwi = 0; pwi < game.phasedWalls.length; pwi++) {
            if (game.phasedWalls[pwi].tileX === tx && game.phasedWalls[pwi].tileY === ty) { isPhased = true; break; }
          }
          if (!isPhased) return false;
        }
      }
      if (tile === 'd') {
        const door = game.doorEntities.find(d => d.tileX === tx && d.tileY === ty);
        if (door && door.solid) return false;
      }
    }
    return true;
  }

  checkInteraction(game) {
    const range = 1.5;
    this.interactTarget = null;
    let bestDist = this.playerClass === 'teevee' ? range * TILE_SIZE * 2 : range * TILE_SIZE;
    for (const door of game.doorEntities) {
      if (game.lockedDoor && game.lockedDoor.tiles.some(function(t) { return t.doorRef === door; })) continue;
      const dx = door.tileX + 0.5 - this.px / TILE_SIZE;
      const dy = door.tileY + 0.5 - this.py / TILE_SIZE;
      const dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy) * TILE_SIZE;
      if (dist < bestDist) {
        bestDist = dist;
        this.interactTarget = door;
        this.interactType = 'door';
      }
    }
    if (game.activeMonsters.every(m => !m.alive)) {
      const altarTargets = [];
      for (const g of game.altarGroups) {
        altarTargets.push({ centerX: g.centerX, centerY: g.centerY, isGroup: true, groupData: g });
      }
      for (const s of game.altarSingles) {
        altarTargets.push({ centerX: s.centerX, centerY: s.centerY, isGroup: false, x: s.x, y: s.y });
      }
      for (const at of altarTargets) {
        const dx = at.centerX / TILE_SIZE - this.px / TILE_SIZE;
        const dy = at.centerY / TILE_SIZE - this.py / TILE_SIZE;
        const dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy) * TILE_SIZE;
        if (dist < bestDist) {
          bestDist = dist;
          this.interactTarget = at;
          this.interactType = 'altar';
        }
      }
    }
    for (const art of game.artifactEntities) {
      if (art.collected) continue;
      const dx = art.tileX + 0.5 - this.px / TILE_SIZE;
      const dy = art.tileY + 0.5 - this.py / TILE_SIZE;
      const dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy) * TILE_SIZE;
      const artMax = this.playerClass === 'teevee' ? range * TILE_SIZE * 2 : bestDist;
      if (dist < artMax && dist < bestDist) {
        bestDist = dist;
        this.interactTarget = art;
        this.interactType = 'artifact';
      }
    }
    // Check shops (only when monster dead, or debugMode)
    if (game.activeMonsters.every(m => !m.alive) || game.debugMode) {
      for (const shop of game.shopItems) {
        if (shop.sold) continue;
        const dx = shop.x + 0.5 - this.px / TILE_SIZE;
        const dy = shop.y + 0.5 - this.py / TILE_SIZE;
        const dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy) * TILE_SIZE;
        if (dist < bestDist) {
          bestDist = dist;
          this.interactTarget = shop;
          this.interactType = 'shop';
        }
      }
    }
    // Check trash bins (only when monster dead, or debugMode)
    if (game.activeMonsters.every(m => !m.alive) || game.debugMode) {
      for (let y = 0; y < game.mapHeight; y++) {
        for (let x = 0; x < game.mapWidth; x++) {
          if (game.tiles[y][x] !== 'T') continue;
          const dx = x + 0.5 - this.px / TILE_SIZE;
          const dy = y + 0.5 - this.py / TILE_SIZE;
          const dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy) * TILE_SIZE;
          if (dist < bestDist) {
            bestDist = dist;
            this.interactTarget = { x, y };
            this.interactType = 'trash';
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }

  takeDamage(dmg, game) {
    if (this.invulnTimer > 0) return;
    if (this.shieldCharges > 0) {
      this.shieldCharges--;
      this.invulnTimer = 0.5;
      for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        game.particles.push(new Particle(
          this.px, this.py,
          (Math.random() - 0.5) * 60, (Math.random() - 0.5) * 60,
          0.3, '#4488ff'
        ));
      }
      return;
    }
    this.hp -= dmg;
    this.invulnTimer = 0.8;
    for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
      game.particles.push(new Particle(
        this.px, this.py,
        (Math.random() - 0.5) * 80, (Math.random() - 0.5) * 80,
        0.4, '#ff4444'
      ));
    }
    if (this.hp <= 0) {
      if (this.inventory[this.selectedSlot] === 'totem') {
        this.hp = 50;
        this.alive = true;
        this.px = game.playerSpawn.x * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2;
        this.py = game.playerSpawn.y * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2;
        this.tileX = game.playerSpawn.x;
        this.tileY = game.playerSpawn.y;
        this.invulnTimer = 5;
        this.invisible = true;
        this.invisibleTimer = 5;
        this.inventory[this.selectedSlot] = null;
        for (let i = 0; i < 12; i++) {
          game.particles.push(new Particle(this.px, this.py, (Math.random()-0.5)*100, (Math.random()-0.5)*100, 0.6, '#ffd700'));
        }
        game.showMessage(this.name + ' was saved by the Totem!');
        if (game.lockedDoor && game.lockedDoor.lockedByPlayerIndex === this.playerIndex) {
          game.lockedDoor = null;
        }
        return;
      }
      this.hp = 0; this.alive = false;
      if (game.lockedDoor && game.lockedDoor.lockedByPlayerIndex === this.playerIndex) {
        game.lockedDoor = null;
      }
      this.inventory = [null, null, null, null, null];
      this.selectedSlot = 0;
      this.duplicatedSlots = new Set();
      this.useTimer = 0;
      this.using = false;
      this.useTapPending = false;
      game.showMessage(this.name + ' died!');
    }
  }

  render(ctx, game) {
    if (!this.alive) return;
    if (this.invulnTimer > 0 && Math.floor(this.invulnTimer * 10) % 2 === 0) return;
    if (!game.debugMode && !game.isPointVisible(this.px, this.py)) return;
    const sx = this.px - game.camX;
    const sy = this.py - game.camY;
    if (this.invisible && !game.debugMode) ctx.globalAlpha = 0.5;
    if (this.phasing && this.playerClass === 'vex') ctx.globalAlpha = 0.4;
    const playerColors = ['#4488ff', '#ff8844', '#44ff44', '#ff44ff'];
    const playerStrokes = ['#2266dd', '#dd6622', '#22dd22', '#dd22dd'];
    const ci = this.playerIndex % playerColors.length;
    ctx.fillStyle = playerColors[ci];
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(sx, sy, TILE_SIZE * 0.35, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.strokeStyle = playerStrokes[ci];
    ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.fillStyle = '#fff';
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(sx - 3, sy - 2, 3, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.arc(sx + 3, sy - 2, 3, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle = '#111';
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(sx - 3, sy - 2, 1.5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.arc(sx + 3, sy - 2, 1.5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
    // Name label
    ctx.font = '10px "Courier New", monospace';
    ctx.fillStyle = '#fff';
    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
    ctx.fillText(this.name, sx, sy - TILE_SIZE * 0.80);

    // HP Bar
    var barWidth = TILE_SIZE;
    var barHeight = Math.max(4, Math.floor(TILE_SIZE * 0.2));
    var barX = sx - barWidth / 2;
    var barY = sy - TILE_SIZE * 0.63;
    var hpRatio = Math.max(0, this.hp / this.maxHp);

    // Fundo escuro da barra
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.7)';
    if (ctx.roundRect) {
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.roundRect(barX - 1, barY - 1, barWidth + 2, barHeight + 2, 2);
      ctx.fill();
    } else {
      ctx.fillRect(barX - 1, barY - 1, barWidth + 2, barHeight + 2);
    }

    // Borda
    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.3)';
    ctx.lineWidth = 1;
    if (ctx.roundRect) {
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.roundRect(barX - 1, barY - 1, barWidth + 2, barHeight + 2, 2);
      ctx.stroke();
    } else {
      ctx.strokeRect(barX - 1, barY - 1, barWidth + 2, barHeight + 2);
    }

    // Barra de HP com gradiente verde -> vermelho (top half)
    var hpHalfH = barHeight / 2;
    var hpGrad = ctx.createLinearGradient(barX, 0, barX + barWidth, 0);
    if (hpRatio > 0.5) {
      hpGrad.addColorStop(0, '#00ff00');
      hpGrad.addColorStop(1, '#ffff00');
    } else {
      hpGrad.addColorStop(0, '#ffff00');
      hpGrad.addColorStop(1, '#ff0000');
    }
    ctx.fillStyle = hpGrad;
    if (ctx.roundRect) {
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.roundRect(barX, barY, barWidth * hpRatio, hpHalfH, 1.5);
      ctx.fill();
    } else {
      ctx.fillRect(barX, barY, barWidth * hpRatio, hpHalfH);
    }
    // Shield bar overlay (bottom half) - blue gradient
    if (this.shieldCharges > 0) {
      var shieldRatio = this.shieldCharges / 2;
      var shGrad = ctx.createLinearGradient(barX, 0, barX + barWidth, 0);
      shGrad.addColorStop(0, '#0044ff');
      shGrad.addColorStop(0.5, '#0088ff');
      shGrad.addColorStop(1, '#00ffff');
      ctx.fillStyle = shGrad;
      if (ctx.roundRect) {
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.roundRect(barX, barY + hpHalfH, barWidth * shieldRatio, hpHalfH, 1.5);
        ctx.fill();
      } else {
        ctx.fillRect(barX, barY + hpHalfH, barWidth * shieldRatio, hpHalfH);
      }
    }

    ctx.textAlign = 'start';
    if (this.phasing && this.playerClass === 'vex') {
      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(136,204,255,0.6)';
      ctx.lineWidth = 2;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(sx, sy, TILE_SIZE * 0.42, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.stroke();
    }
    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
    if (this.commandYellTimer > 0 && !game.debugMode) {
      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,0,0.5)';
      ctx.lineWidth = 2;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(sx, sy, TILE_SIZE * 0.4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.stroke();
    }
  }
}

// ============================================================
// MONSTER
// ============================================================
class MonsterEntity {
  constructor(tileX, tileY, game, monsterType) {
    this.tileX = tileX;
    this.tileY = tileY;
    this.px = tileX * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2;
    this.py = tileY * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2;
    this.speed = 70;
    this.alive = false;
    this.state = 'patrol';
    this.currentWaypoint = 0;
    this.waypointOrder = [];
    this.facingAngle = 0;
    this.chaseTimer = 0;
    this.searchTimer = 0;
    this.lastKnownPlayerPos = null;
    this.attackCooldown = 0;
    this.game = game;
    this.path = null;
    this.pathTarget = null;
    this.pathRecalc = 0;
    this.type = monsterType || 'normal';
    const mt = MonsterTypes[this.type];
    this.chaseLoseTime = mt.chaseLoseTime;
    this.searchDuration = mt.searchDuration;
    this.bodyColor = mt.color;
    this.bodyStroke = mt.strokeColor;
    this.name = mt.name;
    this.stunTimer = 0;
    this.tauntTimer = 0;
    this.tauntTarget = null;
    this.preTauntState = 'patrol';
    this.trapSeeking = false;
    this.frozen = false;
    this.isTransformed = false;
    this.ghostTimer = 0;
    this.ghostVisible = true;
    this.rushSpeedBonus = 0;
    this.decoySpeedBoostTimer = 0;
    this.jesterPathRecalc = 0;
    this.stolenItems = [];
    this.speedScaleTimer = 0;
    this.speedScaleBonus = 0;
    this.berserkerBonus = 0;
    this.shieldActive = true;
    this.bombTimer = 0;
    this.bombTimer = 0;
    this.wandererBounceCount = 0;
    this.wandererUsingWaypoint = false;
  }

  spawn() {
    this.alive = true;
    this.state = 'patrol';
    this.currentWaypoint = 0;
    this.generateWaypointOrder();
    if (this.waypointOrder.length > 0) this.currentWaypoint = 0;
    this.px = this.tileX * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2;
    this.py = this.tileY * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2;
    this.path = null;
    this.pathTarget = null;
    this.speedScaleTimer = 0;
    this.speedScaleBonus = 0;
    this.berserkerBonus = 0;
    this.stunTimer = 0;
    this.shieldActive = true;
    this.bombTimer = 0;
    this.stolenItems = [];
    this.copiedPlayer = null;
    if (this.type === 'jester') {
      this.trapSeeking = this.game.traps.length > 0;
    }
    if (this.type === 'mithrix') {
      for (var mi = 0; mi < this.game.players.length; mi++) {
        var pl = this.game.players[mi];
        if (!pl.alive) continue;
        var occupiedSlots = [];
        for (var si = 0; si < pl.inventory.length; si++) {
          if (pl.inventory[si]) occupiedSlots.push(si);
        }
        var stealCount = Math.ceil(occupiedSlots.length / 2);
        for (var sc = 0; sc < stealCount; sc++) {
          if (occupiedSlots.length === 0) break;
          var randIdx = Math.floor(Math.random() * occupiedSlots.length);
          var slotIdx = occupiedSlots[randIdx];
          occupiedSlots.splice(randIdx, 1);
          this.stolenItems.push({ playerIndex: mi, itemKey: pl.inventory[slotIdx], slotIndex: slotIdx });
          pl.inventory[slotIdx] = null;
        }
      }
      if (this.stolenItems.length > 0) this.game.showMessage('Mithrix stole ' + this.stolenItems.length + ' items!');
    }
  }

  die() {
    this.alive = false;
    this.state = 'patrol';
    this.path = null;
    this.pathTarget = null;
    this.tauntTimer = 0;
    this.tauntTarget = null;
    if (this.type === 'mithrix') {
      for (var si = 0; si < this.stolenItems.length; si++) {
        var sd = this.stolenItems[si];
        if (sd.playerIndex < this.game.players.length) {
          var pl = this.game.players[sd.playerIndex];
          if (pl) {
            if (!pl.inventory[sd.slotIndex]) {
              pl.inventory[sd.slotIndex] = sd.itemKey;
            } else {
              var restored = false;
              for (var ei = 0; ei < pl.inventory.length; ei++) {
                if (!pl.inventory[ei]) { pl.inventory[ei] = sd.itemKey; restored = true; break; }
              }
            }
          }
        }
      }
      if (this.stolenItems.length > 0) this.game.showMessage('Mithrix returned ' + this.stolenItems.length + ' items!');
      this.stolenItems = [];
    }
    this.speedScaleBonus = 0;
    this.speedScaleTimer = 0;
    this.berserkerBonus = 0;
    this.shieldActive = true;
    this.bombTimer = 0;
  }

  generateWaypointOrder() {
    const wp = this.game.waypoints;
    if (wp.length === 0) { this.waypointOrder = []; return; }
    const indices = [...Array(wp.length).keys()];
    for (let i = indices.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
      const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
      [indices[i], indices[j]] = [indices[j], indices[i]];
    }
    this.waypointOrder = indices;
    this.currentWaypoint = 0;
  }

  findNearestPlayer() {
    let nearest = null;
    let nearestDist = Infinity;
    for (const p of this.game.players) {
      if (!p.alive) continue;
      if (p.invisible) continue;
      // Mighty Tinkerer zone: players inside zone are invisible to monster
      var playerInZone = false;
      for (var zi = 0; zi < this.game.activeZones.length; zi++) {
        var zone = this.game.activeZones[zi];
        var zdx = p.px - zone.x;
        var zdy = p.py - zone.y;
        if (Math.sqrt(zdx * zdx + zdy * zdy) <= zone.radius) {
          playerInZone = true;
          break;
        }
      }
      if (playerInZone) continue;
      const dx = p.px - this.px;
      const dy = p.py - this.py;
      const dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
      if (dist < nearestDist) { nearestDist = dist; nearest = p; }
    }
    return nearest;
  }

  canSeePlayer() {
    const player = this.findNearestPlayer();
    if (!player) return false;
    if (player.invisible) return false;
    if (this.type === 'blind' && player.stationaryTimer >= 2.0) return false;
    const dx = player.px - this.px;
    const dy = player.py - this.py;
    const dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
    if (this.type === 'sentinel') {
      if (dist > 400) return false;
      return this.hasLineOfSight(this.px, this.py, player.px, player.py);
    }
    if (dist > 250) return false;
    const angle = Math.atan2(dy, dx);
    let diff = angle - this.facingAngle;
    while (diff > Math.PI) diff -= Math.PI * 2;
    while (diff < -Math.PI) diff += Math.PI * 2;
    if (Math.abs(diff) > (Math.PI / 3) * 0.85 && dist > 50) return false;
    return this.hasLineOfSight(this.px, this.py, player.px, player.py);
  }

  canDetectBehind() {
    if (this.type === 'blind') return false;
    const player = this.findNearestPlayer();
    if (!player) return false;
    if (player.invisible) return false;
    const dx = player.px - this.px;
    const dy = player.py - this.py;
    const dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
    if (dist > 45) return false;
    const angleToPlayer = Math.atan2(dy, dx);
    let diff = angleToPlayer - this.facingAngle;
    while (diff > Math.PI) diff -= Math.PI * 2;
    while (diff < -Math.PI) diff += Math.PI * 2;
    if (Math.abs(diff) > Math.PI * 0.65) return true;
    return false;
  }

  hasLineOfSight(x1, y1, x2, y2) {
    const tiles = this.game.tiles;
    const tx1 = Math.floor(x1 / TILE_SIZE);
    const ty1 = Math.floor(y1 / TILE_SIZE);
    const tx2 = Math.floor(x2 / TILE_SIZE);
    const ty2 = Math.floor(y2 / TILE_SIZE);
    const dx = Math.abs(tx2 - tx1);
    const dy = Math.abs(ty2 - ty1);
    const sx = tx1 < tx2 ? 1 : -1;
    const sy = ty1 < ty2 ? 1 : -1;
    let err = dx - dy;
    let cx = tx1, cy = ty1;
    const steps = dx + dy;
    for (let i = 0; i < steps; i++) {
      if (cx === tx2 && cy === ty2) break;
      const e2 = err * 2;
      if (e2 > -dy) { err -= dy; cx += sx; }
      if (e2 < dx) { err += dx; cy += sy; }
      if (cx >= 0 && cx < this.game.mapWidth && cy >= 0 && cy < this.game.mapHeight) {
        if (tiles[cy][cx] === 'O') return false;
        if (tiles[cy][cx] === 'W' && this.type !== 'spider') return false;
        if (tiles[cy][cx] === 'd') {
          const door = this.game.doorEntities.find(d => d.tileX === cx && d.tileY === cy);
          if (!door || door.solid) return false;
        }
        if (this.game.isWallTile(cx, cy)) return false;
      }
    }
    return true;
  }

  canMoveTo(px, py) {
    const margin = 3;
    const checks = [
      { x: px - margin, y: py - margin },
      { x: px + margin, y: py - margin },
      { x: px - margin, y: py + margin },
      { x: px + margin, y: py + margin }
    ];
    for (const c of checks) {
      const tx = Math.floor(c.x / TILE_SIZE);
      const ty = Math.floor(c.y / TILE_SIZE);
      if (tx < 0 || ty < 0 || tx >= this.game.mapWidth || ty >= this.game.mapHeight) return false;
      const tile = this.game.tiles[ty][tx];
      if (tile === 'O' || (tile === 'W' && this.type !== 'spider')) return false;
      if (tile === 'd') {
        const door = this.game.doorEntities.find(d => d.tileX === tx && d.tileY === ty);
        if (door && door.solid) return false;
      }
      if (this.game.isWallTile(tx, ty)) return false;
    }
    if (this.type === 'twins') {
      for (var tmi = 0; tmi < this.game.activeMonsters.length; tmi++) {
        var other = this.game.activeMonsters[tmi];
        if (other === this || !other.alive || other.type !== 'twins') continue;
        var odx = px - other.px;
        var ody = py - other.py;
        if (Math.sqrt(odx * odx + ody * ody) < TILE_SIZE * 0.76) return false;
      }
    }
    return true;
  }

  update(dt) {
    if (!this.alive) return;
    if (this.stunTimer > 0) {
      this.stunTimer -= dt;
      if (this.stunTimer <= 0) this.stunTimer = 0;
      return;
    }
    if (this.attackCooldown > 0) this.attackCooldown -= dt;
    const mt = MonsterTypes[this.type];
    this.speed = (mt.speedMod || 1) * this.game.getMonsterSpeed() + this.rushSpeedBonus;
    if (this.type === 'desperation') {
      this.speed = (mt.speedMod || 1) * this.game.getMonsterSpeed() + this.game.collectedArtifacts * 15;
    }
    if (this.type === 'hoarder' && !this.shieldActive) {
      this.speed *= 1.2;
    }
    if (this.type === 'alien') {
      this.isTransformed = (this.state === 'chase' || this.state === 'search');
      const baseSpd = this.game.getMonsterSpeed() * (mt.speedMod || 1);
      if (this.state === 'chase') {
        this.speed = baseSpd * (4 / 3);
      } else if (this.state === 'search') {
        this.speed = baseSpd;
      } else {
        this.speed = baseSpd / 3;
      }
    }
    if (this.type === 'decoy' && this.decoySpeedBoostTimer > 0) {
      this.decoySpeedBoostTimer -= dt;
      if (this.decoySpeedBoostTimer <= 0) this.decoySpeedBoostTimer = 0;
      this.speed = this.speed * 2.0;
    }
    if (this.type === 'ghost') {
      this.ghostTimer += dt;
      if (this.ghostTimer >= 2.0) {
        this.ghostVisible = !this.ghostVisible;
        this.ghostTimer -= 2.0;
      }
    }
    // Speedscale: +2 speed every 7 seconds
    if (this.type === 'speedscale') {
      this.speedScaleTimer += dt;
      while (this.speedScaleTimer >= 7) { this.speedScaleTimer -= 7; this.speedScaleBonus += this.speed * 0.05; }
      this.speed += this.speedScaleBonus;
    }
    // Berserker: speed bonus applied
    if (this.type === 'berserker') {
      this.speed += this.berserkerBonus;
    }
    // Vampire: speed based on tile type
    if (this.type === 'vampire') {
      var vtile = this.game.tiles[this.tileY] ? this.game.tiles[this.tileY][this.tileX] : 'G';
      var veff = this.game.baseTileResolved[this.tileY] ? this.game.baseTileResolved[this.tileY][this.tileX] : vtile;
      if (veff === 'C' || veff === 'A' || veff === 'd') { this.speed *= 0.6; }
      else if (veff === 'G') { this.speed *= 1.6; }
      else if (vtile === 'F' || vtile === 'U' || vtile === 'm') {
        if (veff === 'G') this.speed *= 1.6; else this.speed *= 0.6;
      }
    }
    if (this.type === 'blind' && this.state === 'patrol') {
      this.speed *= 1.25;
    }
    if (this.type === 'spider') {
      var stile = this.game.tiles[this.tileY] ? this.game.tiles[this.tileY][this.tileX] : '';
      if (stile === 'W') this.speed *= 1.35;
    }
    // Bomber: drop bombs every 2 seconds
    if (this.type === 'bomber') {
      this.bombTimer += dt;
      while (this.bombTimer >= 2) {
        this.bombTimer -= 2;
        this.game.activeBombs.push({ tileX: this.tileX, tileY: this.tileY, timer: 15, ownerRef: this });
      }
    }
    // Acrid: contaminate current tile
    if (this.type === 'acrid') {
      var found = false;
      for (var cti = 0; cti < this.game.contaminatedTiles.length; cti++) {
        var ct = this.game.contaminatedTiles[cti];
        if (ct.tileX === this.tileX && ct.tileY === this.tileY) { ct.timer = 10; found = true; break; }
      }
      if (!found) {
        this.game.contaminatedTiles.push({ tileX: this.tileX, tileY: this.tileY, timer: 10 });
      }
    }
    if (this.type === 'jester' && this.trapSeeking) {
      if (this.game.traps.length === 0) {
        this.trapSeeking = false;
        this.state = 'patrol';
        this.generateWaypointOrder();
      } else {
        this.updateJester(dt);
        this.tileX = Math.floor(this.px / TILE_SIZE);
        this.tileY = Math.floor(this.py / TILE_SIZE);
        return;
      }
    }
    // Mighty Tinkerer zone: force monster speed
    for (var zi = 0; zi < this.game.activeZones.length; zi++) {
      var zone = this.game.activeZones[zi];
      var zdx = this.px - zone.x;
      var zdy = this.py - zone.y;
      if (Math.sqrt(zdx * zdx + zdy * zdy) <= zone.radius) {
        this.speed = zone.monsterSpeed;
        break;
      }
    }
    if (this.type === 'ballerina') {
      this.frozen = this.isBeingWatched();
      if (this.frozen) {
        this.tileX = Math.floor(this.px / TILE_SIZE);
        this.tileY = Math.floor(this.py / TILE_SIZE);
      }
    }
    this.checkNearbyDoors(dt);
    if (!(this.type === 'ballerina' && this.frozen)) {
      switch (this.state) {
        case 'patrol': this.updatePatrol(dt); break;
        case 'chase': this.updateChase(dt); break;
        case 'search': this.updateSearch(dt); break;
        case 'taunted': this.updateTaunted(dt); break;
      }
    }
    this.tileX = Math.floor(this.px / TILE_SIZE);
    this.tileY = Math.floor(this.py / TILE_SIZE);
    if (this.type === 'twins') {
      for (var tmi = 0; tmi < this.game.activeMonsters.length; tmi++) {
        var other = this.game.activeMonsters[tmi];
        if (other === this || !other.alive || other.type !== 'twins') continue;
        var tdx = this.px - other.px;
        var tdy = this.py - other.py;
        var tdist = Math.sqrt(tdx * tdx + tdy * tdy);
        var minDist = TILE_SIZE * 0.76;
        if (tdist < minDist && tdist > 0.001) {
          var overlap = minDist - tdist;
          var tnx = tdx / tdist;
          var tny = tdy / tdist;
          this.px += tnx * overlap * 0.5;
          this.py += tny * overlap * 0.5;
          other.px -= tnx * overlap * 0.5;
          other.py -= tny * overlap * 0.5;
        }
      }
    }
    if (this.alive && !(this.type === 'jester' && this.trapSeeking)) {
      const target = this.findNearestPlayer();
      if (target) {
        const dx = target.px - this.px;
        const dy = target.py - this.py;
        const dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
        if (dist < TILE_SIZE * 0.8 && this.attackCooldown <= 0) {
          // Mighty Tinkerer zone: players inside zone take no damage
          var targetInZone = false;
          for (var zi = 0; zi < this.game.activeZones.length; zi++) {
            var zone = this.game.activeZones[zi];
            var zdx = target.px - zone.x;
            var zdy = target.py - zone.y;
            if (Math.sqrt(zdx * zdx + zdy * zdy) <= zone.radius) {
              targetInZone = true;
              break;
            }
          }
            if (!targetInZone) {
              target.takeDamage(this.type === 'wanderer' ? 50 : 20, this.game);
              this.attackCooldown = 1.0;
            }
        }
      }
    }
  }

  checkNearbyDoors(dt) {
    const tx = Math.floor(this.px / TILE_SIZE);
    const ty = Math.floor(this.py / TILE_SIZE);
    for (let dy = -1; dy <= 1; dy++) {
      for (let dx = -1; dx <= 1; dx++) {
        const cx = tx + dx, cy = ty + dy;
        if (cx < 0 || cy < 0 || cx >= this.game.mapWidth || cy >= this.game.mapHeight) continue;
        if (this.game.tiles[cy][cx] === 'd') {
          const door = this.game.doorEntities.find(d => d.tileX === cx && d.tileY === cy);
          if (door && !door.open && !(this.game.lockedDoor && this.game.lockedDoor.tiles.some(function(t) { return t.doorRef === door; }))) door.forceOpen();
        }
      }
    }
  }

  followPath(dt) {
    if (!this.path || this.path.length === 0) return false;
    const next = this.path[0];
    const tx = next.x * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2;
    const ty = next.y * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2;
    const dx = tx - this.px;
    const dy = ty - this.py;
    const dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
    if (dist < TILE_SIZE * 0.25) {
      this.path.shift();
      if (this.path.length === 0) return false;
      return this.followPath(dt);
    }
    const ndx = dx / dist, ndy = dy / dist;
    this.facingAngle = Math.atan2(ndy, ndx);
    const spd = (this.state === 'chase' || this.state === 'taunted') ? this.speed * 1.4 : this.speed;
    const nx = this.px + ndx * spd * dt;
    const ny = this.py + ndy * spd * dt;
    if (this.canMoveTo(nx, this.py)) this.px = nx;
    if (this.canMoveTo(this.px, ny)) this.py = ny;
    return true;
  }

  updatePatrol(dt) {
    if (this.type === 'sentinel') return;
    if (this.type === 'wanderer') {
      this.updateWanderer(dt);
      return;
    }
    if (this.canDetectBehind()) {
      this.state = 'chase'; this.chaseTimer = 0; this.path = null; return;
    }
    if (this.canSeePlayer()) {
      this.state = 'chase'; this.chaseTimer = 0; this.path = null; return;
    }
    if (this.waypointOrder.length === 0) return;
    const wpIdx = this.waypointOrder[this.currentWaypoint];
    if (wpIdx >= this.game.waypoints.length) {
      this.generateWaypointOrder(); return;
    }
    const wp = this.game.waypoints[wpIdx];
    this.pathRecalc -= dt;
    if (!this.path || this.pathTarget !== wpIdx || this.pathRecalc <= 0) {
      const stx = Math.floor(this.px / TILE_SIZE);
      const sty = Math.floor(this.py / TILE_SIZE);
      const found = this.game.findPath(stx, sty, wp.x, wp.y, this.type === 'spider');
      if (found) {
        this.path = found;
        this.pathTarget = wpIdx;
        this.pathRecalc = 0.5;
      } else {
        this.path = null;
        this.pathTarget = null;
      }
    }
    if (this.path && this.path.length > 0) {
      if (!this.followPath(dt)) {
        this.path = null;
        this.currentWaypoint++;
        if (this.currentWaypoint >= this.waypointOrder.length) {
          this.generateWaypointOrder();
        }
      }
    } else {
      this.currentWaypoint++;
      if (this.currentWaypoint >= this.waypointOrder.length) {
        this.generateWaypointOrder();
      }
    }
  }

  updateWanderer(dt) {
    this.speed = 750;
    this.wandererBounceCount = this.wandererBounceCount || 0;
    if (this.wandererUsingWaypoint) {
      if (!this.path || this.path.length === 0) {
        this.wandererUsingWaypoint = false;
        this.wandererBounceCount = 0;
        this.facingAngle = [0, Math.PI/2, Math.PI, Math.PI*3/2][Math.floor(Math.random() * 4)];
        return;
      }
      if (!this.followPath(dt)) {
        this.path = null;
        this.wandererUsingWaypoint = false;
        this.wandererBounceCount = 0;
        this.facingAngle = [0, Math.PI/2, Math.PI, Math.PI*3/2][Math.floor(Math.random() * 4)];
      }
      return;
    }
    var ndx = Math.cos(this.facingAngle);
    var ndy = Math.sin(this.facingAngle);
    var nx = this.px + ndx * this.speed * dt;
    var ny = this.py + ndy * this.speed * dt;
    var hitWall = false;
    if (!this.canMoveTo(nx, this.py)) { nx = this.px; hitWall = true; }
    if (!this.canMoveTo(this.px, ny)) { ny = this.py; hitWall = true; }
    this.px = nx;
    this.py = ny;
    if (hitWall) {
      this.wandererBounceCount++;
      var dirs = [0, Math.PI/2, Math.PI, Math.PI*3/2];
      var cur = this.facingAngle;
      var curIdx = dirs.findIndex(function(d){return Math.abs(d - cur) < 0.1;});
      var nextIdx = (curIdx >= 0 ? (curIdx + (Math.random() < 0.5 ? 1 : 3)) % 4 : Math.floor(Math.random() * 4));
      this.facingAngle = dirs[nextIdx];
      if (this.wandererBounceCount >= 8 && this.game.waypoints.length > 0) {
        this.wandererBounceCount = 0;
        this.wandererUsingWaypoint = true;
        var rwp = this.game.waypoints[Math.floor(Math.random() * this.game.waypoints.length)];
        var stx = Math.floor(this.px / TILE_SIZE);
        var sty = Math.floor(this.py / TILE_SIZE);
        var found = this.game.findPath(stx, sty, rwp.x, rwp.y, this.type === 'spider');
        if (found) {
          this.path = found;
        } else {
          this.wandererUsingWaypoint = false;
        }
      }
    }
  }

  updateChase(dt) {
    const target = this.findNearestPlayer();
    if (!target) { this.state = 'patrol'; this.path = null; return; }
    if (!this.canSeePlayer() && !this.canDetectBehind()) {
      this.chaseTimer += dt;
      if (this.chaseTimer > this.chaseLoseTime) {
        this.lastKnownPlayerPos = {
          x: target.px, y: target.py
        };
        this.state = this.type === 'sentinel' ? 'patrol' : 'search';
        this.searchTimer = 0;
        this.path = null;
        return;
      }
    } else {
      this.chaseTimer = 0;
    }
    this.lastKnownPlayerPos = {
      x: target.px, y: target.py
    };
    const stx = Math.floor(this.px / TILE_SIZE);
    const sty = Math.floor(this.py / TILE_SIZE);
    const gtx = Math.floor(target.px / TILE_SIZE);
    const gty = Math.floor(target.py / TILE_SIZE);
    const found = this.game.findPath(stx, sty, gtx, gty, this.type === 'spider');
    if (found && found.length > 0) {
      this.path = found;
      this.followPath(dt);
    }
  }

  updateSearch(dt) {
    if (this.canSeePlayer() || this.canDetectBehind()) {
      this.state = 'chase'; this.chaseTimer = 0; this.path = null; return;
    }
    this.searchTimer += dt;
    if (this.searchTimer > this.searchDuration || !this.lastKnownPlayerPos) {
      this.state = 'patrol'; this.path = null; return;
    }
    const stx = Math.floor(this.px / TILE_SIZE);
    const sty = Math.floor(this.py / TILE_SIZE);
    const gtx = Math.floor(this.lastKnownPlayerPos.x / TILE_SIZE);
    const gty = Math.floor(this.lastKnownPlayerPos.y / TILE_SIZE);
    if (!this.path) {
      const found = this.game.findPath(stx, sty, gtx, gty, this.type === 'spider');
      if (found && found.length > 0) {
        this.path = found;
      } else {
        this.state = 'patrol'; this.path = null; return;
      }
    }
    if (!this.followPath(dt)) {
      this.state = 'patrol'; this.path = null;
    }
  }

  updateTaunted(dt) {
    this.tauntTimer -= dt;
    if (this.tauntTimer <= 0) {
      this.tauntTimer = 0;
      this.tauntTarget = null;
      this.state = this.preTauntState || 'patrol';
      this.path = null;
      return;
    }
    if (!this.tauntTarget) { this.state = 'patrol'; this.path = null; return; }
    const attackTarget = this.findNearestPlayer();
    if (attackTarget) {
      const adx = attackTarget.px - this.px;
      const ady = attackTarget.py - this.py;
      const adist = Math.sqrt(adx * adx + ady * ady);
      if (adist < TILE_SIZE * 0.8 && this.attackCooldown <= 0) {
        // Mighty Tinkerer zone: players inside zone take no damage
        var targetInZone = false;
        for (var zi = 0; zi < this.game.activeZones.length; zi++) {
          var zone = this.game.activeZones[zi];
          var zdx = attackTarget.px - zone.x;
          var zdy = attackTarget.py - zone.y;
          if (Math.sqrt(zdx * zdx + zdy * zdy) <= zone.radius) {
            targetInZone = true;
            break;
          }
        }
        if (!targetInZone) {
          attackTarget.takeDamage(20, this.game);
          this.attackCooldown = 1.0;
        }
      }
    }
    const stx = Math.floor(this.px / TILE_SIZE);
    const sty = Math.floor(this.py / TILE_SIZE);
    const gtx = Math.floor(this.tauntTarget.x / TILE_SIZE);
    const gty = Math.floor(this.tauntTarget.y / TILE_SIZE);
    if (!this.path) {
      const found = this.game.findPath(stx, sty, gtx, gty, this.type === 'spider');
      if (found && found.length > 0) {
        this.path = found;
      } else {
        this.path = null;
      }
    }
    if (this.path && this.path.length > 0) {
      this.followPath(dt);
    }
  }

  isPlayerLooking(player) {
    if (player.lastDx === 0 && player.lastDy === 0) return false;
    const angleToMe = Math.atan2(this.py - player.py, this.px - player.px);
    const playerFacing = Math.atan2(player.lastDy, player.lastDx);
    let diff = angleToMe - playerFacing;
    while (diff > Math.PI) diff -= Math.PI * 2;
    while (diff < -Math.PI) diff += Math.PI * 2;
    return Math.abs(diff) < Math.PI / 3;
  }

  isBeingWatched() {
    for (const p of this.game.players) {
      if (!p.alive) continue;
      if (!this.isPlayerLooking(p)) continue;
      const vp = this.game.visibilityPolygons[p.playerIndex];
      if (!vp) continue;
      let inside = false;
      const pts = vp.points;
      for (let i = 0, j = pts.length - 1; i < pts.length; j = i++) {
        const xi = pts[i].x, yi = pts[i].y;
        const xj = pts[j].x, yj = pts[j].y;
        if (((yi > this.py) !== (yj > this.py)) && (this.px < (xj - xi) * (this.py - yi) / (yj - yi) + xi)) {
          inside = !inside;
        }
      }
      if (inside) return true;
    }
    return false;
  }

  updateJester(dt) {
    if (this.stunTimer > 0) { this.stunTimer -= dt; if (this.stunTimer <= 0) this.stunTimer = 0; return; }
    let nearest = null; let nearestDist = Infinity;
    for (const t of this.game.traps) {
      const dx = t.x - this.px; const dy = t.y - this.py;
      const dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
      if (dist < nearestDist) { nearestDist = dist; nearest = t; }
    }
    if (!nearest) { this.trapSeeking = false; return; }
    if (nearestDist < TILE_SIZE * 0.2) {
      this.stunTimer = nearest.stun || 0;
      const idx = this.game.traps.indexOf(nearest);
      if (idx >= 0) this.game.traps.splice(idx, 1);
      this.path = null;
      return;
    }
    this.jesterPathRecalc -= dt;
    const stx = Math.floor(this.px / TILE_SIZE);
    const sty = Math.floor(this.py / TILE_SIZE);
    const gtx = Math.floor(nearest.x / TILE_SIZE);
    const gty = Math.floor(nearest.y / TILE_SIZE);
    if (!this.path || this.jesterPathRecalc <= 0) {
      this.path = this.game.findPath(stx, sty, gtx, gty);
      this.jesterPathRecalc = 0.5;
    }
    if (this.path && this.path.length > 0) {
      this.followPath(dt);
    } else {
      this.updatePatrol(dt);
    }
  }

  render(ctx, game) {
    if (!this.alive) return;
    if (this.type === 'ghost' && !this.ghostVisible && !game.debugMode) return;
    const oracleReveal = game.players.some(p => p.playerClass === 'oracle' && p.abilityActive);
    if (!game.debugMode && !oracleReveal && !game.isPointVisible(this.px, this.py)) return;
    if (this.type === 'ghost' && !this.ghostVisible && oracleReveal) return;
    const rx = this.px - game.camX;
    const ry = this.py - game.camY;
    if (oracleReveal) {
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,0,0.25)';
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(rx, ry, TILE_SIZE * 0.7, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
    }
    const baseRadius = TILE_SIZE * 0.38;
    let radius = baseRadius;
    if (this.type === 'monstrosity') radius = baseRadius * 1.2;
    if (this.type === 'alien' && !this.isTransformed) radius = baseRadius * 0.5;
    if (this.type === 'desperation') {
      const intensity = Math.min(1, game.collectedArtifacts / Math.max(1, game.artifactCount));
      radius = baseRadius * (1.0 + intensity * 0.15);
    }
    if (this.type === 'ghost') {
      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.7;
    }
    if (this.type === 'desperation') {
      const intensity = Math.min(1, game.collectedArtifacts / Math.max(1, game.artifactCount));
      ctx.shadowColor = 'rgba(255,0,0,' + (0.3 + intensity * 0.5) + ')';
      ctx.shadowBlur = 5 + intensity * 10;
    }
    if (this.type === 'rush' && this.rushSpeedBonus > 0) {
      const glowIntensity = Math.min(1, this.rushSpeedBonus / 200);
      ctx.shadowColor = 'rgba(255,170,0,' + (0.3 + glowIntensity * 0.5) + ')';
      ctx.shadowBlur = 5 + glowIntensity * 10;
    }
    if (this.type === 'decoy' && this.decoySpeedBoostTimer > 0) {
      ctx.shadowColor = 'rgba(153,102,255,0.5)';
      ctx.shadowBlur = 10;
    }
    if (this.type === 'ballerina' && this.frozen) {
      ctx.shadowColor = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.4)';
      ctx.shadowBlur = 8;
    }
    ctx.fillStyle = this.bodyColor;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(rx, ry, radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.strokeStyle = this.bodyStroke;
    ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
    ctx.stroke();
    const scale = radius / baseRadius;
    const ex = rx + Math.cos(this.facingAngle) * radius * 1.32;
    const ey = ry + Math.sin(this.facingAngle) * radius * 1.32;
    ctx.fillStyle = this.type === 'jester' && this.trapSeeking ? '#ff0' : '#ff0';
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(ex, ey, 3 * scale, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle = '#ffcc00';
    ctx.fillRect(rx - radius * 0.4, ry - radius * 1.18, radius * 0.8, radius * 0.32);
    ctx.fillRect(rx - radius * 0.21, ry - radius * 1.58, radius * 0.42, radius * 0.47);
    if (this.type === 'ratking') {
      ctx.fillStyle = '#ffcc00';
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(rx - radius * 0.35, ry - radius * 1.25);
      ctx.lineTo(rx - radius * 0.55, ry - radius * 1.7);
      ctx.lineTo(rx - radius * 0.15, ry - radius * 1.5);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(rx + radius * 0.35, ry - radius * 1.25);
      ctx.lineTo(rx + radius * 0.55, ry - radius * 1.7);
      ctx.lineTo(rx + radius * 0.15, ry - radius * 1.5);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(rx, ry - radius * 1.58);
      ctx.lineTo(rx - radius * 0.05, ry - radius * 1.85);
      ctx.lineTo(rx + radius * 0.05, ry - radius * 1.85);
      ctx.fill();
    }
    ctx.fillStyle = '#fff';
    ctx.beginPath();
    if (this.type === 'jester' && this.trapSeeking) {
      ctx.fillStyle = '#ff0';
      ctx.arc(rx - 3 * scale, ry - 2 * scale, 3 * scale, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.arc(rx + 3 * scale, ry - 2 * scale, 3 * scale, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle = '#800';
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(rx - 3 * scale, ry - 2 * scale, 1.5 * scale, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.arc(rx + 3 * scale, ry - 2 * scale, 1.5 * scale, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
    } else {
      ctx.arc(rx - 3 * scale, ry - 2 * scale, 3 * scale, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.arc(rx + 3 * scale, ry - 2 * scale, 3 * scale, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle = '#f00';
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(rx - 3 * scale, ry - 2 * scale, 1.5 * scale, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.arc(rx + 3 * scale, ry - 2 * scale, 1.5 * scale, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
    }
    if (this.type === 'jester' && this.trapSeeking) {
      ctx.strokeStyle = '#ff0';
      ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(rx, ry - radius * 0.65, 5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.stroke();
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(rx - 7, ry - radius * 0.65);
      ctx.lineTo(rx + 7, ry - radius * 0.65);
      ctx.moveTo(rx, ry - radius * 0.65 - 7);
      ctx.lineTo(rx, ry - radius * 0.65 + 7);
      ctx.stroke();
    }
    if (this.state === 'taunted') {
      const bounce = Math.sin(Date.now() / 1000 * 8 + (this.px + this.py) * 0.1) * 2;
      ctx.font = 'bold 12px "Courier New", monospace';
      ctx.textAlign = 'center';
      ctx.fillStyle = '#ff0000';
      ctx.fillText('!!!', rx, ry - radius * 1.3 + bounce);
      ctx.textAlign = 'start';
    }
    // Hoarder shield visual
    if (this.type === 'hoarder' && this.shieldActive) {
      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,215,0,0.6)';
      ctx.lineWidth = 2;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(rx, ry, TILE_SIZE * 0.65, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.stroke();
      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,215,0,0.3)';
      ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(rx, ry, TILE_SIZE * 0.55, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.stroke();
    }
    // Sentinel statue rendering
    if (this.type === 'sentinel' && this.state === 'patrol') {
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
      ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
      ctx.fillStyle = '#555566';
      ctx.fillRect(rx - radius * 0.7, ry - radius * 1.2, radius * 1.4, radius * 2.4);
      ctx.strokeStyle = '#333344';
      ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      ctx.strokeRect(rx - radius * 0.7, ry - radius * 1.2, radius * 1.4, radius * 2.4);
      ctx.fillStyle = '#666677';
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(rx, ry - radius * 0.8, radius * 0.5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
    }
    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
    ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
    ctx.shadowColor = 'transparent';
  }

  renderVisionCone(ctx, game) {
    if (!this.alive || !game.debugMode) return;
    const rx = this.px - game.camX;
    const ry = this.py - game.camY;
    const r = 195;
    // Front vision cone
    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,0,0,0.3)';
    ctx.lineWidth = 1;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(rx, ry, r, this.facingAngle - Math.PI / 3, this.facingAngle + Math.PI / 3);
    ctx.lineTo(rx, ry);
    ctx.closePath();
    ctx.stroke();
    // Rear detection zone (45px range, 126 deg sector behind monster)
    const rearR = 45;
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255, 165, 0, 0.15)';
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(rx, ry, rearR, this.facingAngle + Math.PI * 0.65, this.facingAngle - Math.PI * 0.65);
    ctx.lineTo(rx, ry);
    ctx.closePath();
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255, 165, 0, 0.5)';
    ctx.lineWidth = 1;
    ctx.stroke();
  }
}

// ============================================================
// PRE-GAME HOTKEY STORAGE
// ============================================================
let preGameKeybinds = {};

// ============================================================
// GAME CLASS
// ============================================================
class Game {
  constructor() {
    this.canvas = document.getElementById('game');
    this.ctx = this.canvas.getContext('2d');
    this.tiles = [];
    this.mapWidth = 0;
    this.mapHeight = 0;
    this.doorEntities = [];
    this.artifactEntities = [];
    this.waypoints = [];
    this.altarGroups = [];
    this.altarSingles = [];
    this.playerSpawn = { x: 0, y: 0 };
    this.monsterSpawn = { x: 0, y: 0 };
    this.players = [];
    this.playerCount = 1;
    this.playerNames = ['P1', 'P2', 'P3', 'P4'];
    this.playerClasses = ['oracle', 'oracle', 'oracle', 'oracle'];
    this.activeMonsters = [];
    this.monsterSpawnType = 'normal';
    this.summonPhase = '';
    this.summonPhaseTimer = 0;
    this.summonPhaseMessage = '';
    this.nameMessage = '';
    this.monsterDesc = '';
    this.particles = [];
    this.camX = 0; this.camY = 0;
    this.camScale = 1;
    this.summonCount = 0;
    this.roundNumber = 0;
    this.difficulty = 'easy';
    this.baseMonsterSpeed = 50;
    this.artifactFPoints = [];
    this.currency = 0;
    this.traps = [];
    this.shopItems = [];
    this.randomMonsterSpawnPoints = [];
    this.nightTransition = 0;
    this.isNight = false;
    this.roofAlpha = 1;
    this.targetRoofAlpha = 1;
    this.debugMode = false;
    this.paused = false;
    this.visibilityGrid = [];
    this.visibilityAge = [];
    this.lastTime = 0;
    this.artifactCount = 3;
    this.collectedArtifacts = 0;
    this.summoning = false;
    this.summonTimer = 0;
    this.summonDelay = 0;
    this.messageText = '';
    this.messageTimer = 0;
    this.interactDisplay = null;
    this.interactDuration = 0;
    this.lightmapCanvas = null;
    this.visibilityPolygons = [];
    this.playerVisionRange = [];
    this.tileVariation = [];
    this.tileEdgeMask = [];
    this.baseTileResolved = [];
    this.hotbarSize = 100;
    this.hotbarLayout = 'horizontal';
    this.hotbarGap = 30;
    this.activeClones = [];
    this.activeWalls = [];
    this.lockedDoor = null;
    this.debugSelectedMonster = null;
    this.selectedMonsterType = null;
    this.pendingMonsterType = null;
    this.ratEntities = [];
    this.chests = [];
    this.chestSpawnTimer = 0;
    this.chestFirstSpawned = false;
    this.contaminatedTiles = [];
    this.contaminatedParticles = [];
    this.activeBombs = [];
    this.smokeClouds = [];
    this.phasedWalls = [];
    this.activeAuras = [];
    this.zones = [];
    this.lightmapCanvas = null;
    this.chestPendingSpawn = false;
    this.chestPendingSpawnTimer = 0;
  }

  static tileHash(x, y) {
    let h = (x * 374761393 + y * 668265263) ^ 0x5bf03635;
    h = ((h >> 16) ^ h) * 0x45d9f3b;
    return ((h >>> 0) % 1000) / 1000;
  }

  static progressColor(t) {
    const r = Math.min(255, Math.max(0, Math.round(t < 0.5 ? 255 : 255 * (1 - t) * 2)));
    const g = Math.min(255, Math.max(0, Math.round(t < 0.5 ? 255 * t * 2 : 255)));
    const b = 0;
    return 'rgb(' + r + ',' + g + ',' + b + ')';
  }

  resolveBaseTile(x, y) {
    const tile = this.tiles[y][x];
    if (tile !== 'F' && tile !== 'U' && tile !== 'm') return tile;
    let floorCount = 0;
    let grassCount = 0;
    for (let dy = -1; dy <= 1; dy++) {
      for (let dx = -1; dx <= 1; dx++) {
        if (dx === 0 && dy === 0) continue;
        const nx = x + dx, ny = y + dy;
        if (nx < 0 || nx >= this.mapWidth || ny < 0 || ny >= this.mapHeight) continue;
        const neighborTile = this.tiles[ny][nx];
        if (neighborTile === 'C') floorCount++;
        else if (neighborTile === 'G') grassCount++;
        else if (neighborTile === 'd') floorCount++;
      }
    }
    return floorCount >= grassCount ? 'C' : 'G';
  }

  getEffectiveTile(x, y) {
    if (this.baseTileResolved[y] && this.baseTileResolved[y][x] !== undefined) {
      return this.baseTileResolved[y][x];
    }
    return this.tiles[y][x];
  }

  loadMap(mapData) {
    if (!mapData) return;
    this.tiles = mapData.tiles;
    this.mapWidth = mapData.width;
    this.mapHeight = mapData.height;
    this.zones = DEFAULT_ZONES.map(function(row) { return row.split(''); });
    this.doorEntities = mapData.doors.map(d => new DoorEntity(d.x, d.y));
    let doorByPos = {};
    for (let di = 0; di < this.doorEntities.length; di++) {
      let d = this.doorEntities[di];
      doorByPos[d.tileX + ',' + d.tileY] = d;
    }
    let visited = {};
    for (let di2 = 0; di2 < this.doorEntities.length; di2++) {
      let startDoor = this.doorEntities[di2];
      let key = startDoor.tileX + ',' + startDoor.tileY;
      if (visited[key]) continue;
      let component = [];
      let queue = [startDoor];
      visited[key] = true;
      while (queue.length > 0) {
        let current = queue.shift();
        component.push(current);
        let dirs = [[0, -1], [0, 1], [-1, 0], [1, 0]];
        for (let di3 = 0; di3 < dirs.length; di3++) {
          let nx = current.tileX + dirs[di3][0];
          let ny = current.tileY + dirs[di3][1];
          let nkey = nx + ',' + ny;
          let neighbor = doorByPos[nkey];
          if (neighbor && !visited[nkey]) {
            visited[nkey] = true;
            queue.push(neighbor);
          }
        }
      }
      if (component.length >= 2) {
        for (let ci = 0; ci < component.length; ci++) {
          component[ci].groupMembers = component;
        }
      }
    }
    this.artifactEntities = [];
    this.waypoints = mapData.waypoints;
    this.altarGroups = [];
    this.altarSingles = [];
    const altarPts = mapData.altarPositions || [];
    const altarUsed = new Set();
    for (const pos of altarPts) {
      const k = pos.x + ',' + pos.y;
      if (altarUsed.has(k)) continue;
      if (altarPts.some(p => p.x === pos.x + 1 && p.y === pos.y) &&
          altarPts.some(p => p.x === pos.x && p.y === pos.y + 1) &&
          altarPts.some(p => p.x === pos.x + 1 && p.y === pos.y + 1)) {
        this.altarGroups.push({
          minX: pos.x, minY: pos.y, maxX: pos.x + 1, maxY: pos.y + 1,
          centerX: (pos.x + 1) * TILE_SIZE,
          centerY: (pos.y + 1) * TILE_SIZE
        });
        altarUsed.add(k);
        altarUsed.add((pos.x + 1) + ',' + pos.y);
        altarUsed.add(pos.x + ',' + (pos.y + 1));
        altarUsed.add((pos.x + 1) + ',' + (pos.y + 1));
      }
    }
    for (const pos of altarPts) {
      const k = pos.x + ',' + pos.y;
      if (!altarUsed.has(k)) {
        this.altarSingles.push({
          x: pos.x, y: pos.y,
          centerX: (pos.x + 0.5) * TILE_SIZE,
