          centerY: (pos.y + 0.5) * TILE_SIZE
        });
      }
    }
    this.playerSpawn = mapData.playerSpawn || { x: 1, y: 1 };
    this.monsterSpawn = mapData.monsterSpawn || (altarPts.length > 0 ? altarPts[0] : null) || { x: 2, y: 2 };
    this.artifactEntities = [];
    this.randomMonsterSpawnPoints = mapData.randomSpawns || [];
    this.artifactFPoints = [];
    for (let y = 0; y < this.mapHeight; y++) {
      for (let x = 0; x < this.mapWidth; x++) {
        if (this.tiles[y][x] === 'F') this.artifactFPoints.push({ x, y });
      }
    }
    this.artifactCount = this.difficulty === 'easy' ? 3 : (this.difficulty === 'medium' ? 4 : 5);
    if (this.artifactFPoints.length < this.artifactCount) this.artifactCount = this.artifactFPoints.length;
    if (this.artifactCount === 0) this.artifactCount = 3;
    this.visibilityGrid = [];
    this.visibilityAge = [];
    for (let y = 0; y < this.mapHeight; y++) {
      this.visibilityGrid.push(new Array(this.mapWidth).fill(0));
      this.visibilityAge.push(new Array(this.mapWidth).fill(0));
    }
    this.canvas.width = this.mapWidth * TILE_SIZE;
    this.canvas.height = this.mapHeight * TILE_SIZE;
    this.players = [];
    const count = this.playerCount;
    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      const spawnPos = this.findSpawnPosition(this.playerSpawn.x, this.playerSpawn.y, i);
      const sx = spawnPos.x;
      const sy = spawnPos.y;
      const p = new Player(sx, sy, i, this.playerNames[i] || ('P' + (i + 1)), this.playerClasses[i] || 'oracle');
      if (i === 0) {
        p.px = this.playerSpawn.x * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2;
        p.py = this.playerSpawn.y * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2;
        p.tileX = this.playerSpawn.x;
        p.tileY = this.playerSpawn.y;
      }
      p.alive = true;
      p.hp = p.maxHp;
      p.interacting = false;
      p.interactTimer = 0;
      p.invulnTimer = 0;
      p.abilityCooldown = 0;
      p.abilityActive = false;
      p.abilityTimer = 0;
      if (p.playerClass === 'scout') p.speed = p.baseSpeed;
      p.inventory = [null, null, null, null, null];
      p.selectedSlot = 0;
      p.useTimer = 0;
      p.using = false;
      p.commandYellMult = 0;
      p.commandYellTimer = 0;
      p.invisible = false;
      p.invisibleTimer = 0;
      p.timeTravelUsed = false;
      p.duplicatedSlots = new Set();
      this.players.push(p);
    }
    for (var ui = 0; ui < this.players.length; ui++) {
      var up = this.players[ui];
      if (up.playerClass === 'utilityplayer' && !up.initialSpawnDone) {
        var itemKeys = Object.keys(Items);
        for (var si = 0; si < 5; si++) {
          var randItem = itemKeys[Math.floor(Math.random() * itemKeys.length)];
          up.addToInventory(randItem);
        }
        up.initialSpawnDone = true;
        this.showMessage(up.name + ' received 5 random items!');
      }
    }
    this.activeZones = [];
    this.lockedDoor = null;
    this.ratEntities = [];
    this.pendingMonsterType = null;
    // Clear monsters
    this.activeMonsters = [];
    this.monsterSpawnType = 'normal';
    this.summonPhase = '';
    this.summonPhaseTimer = 0;
    this.summonPhaseMessage = '';
    this.nameMessage = '';
    this.monsterDesc = '';
    // Reset state
    this.summonCount = 0;
    this.roundNumber = 0;
    this.collectedArtifacts = 0;
    this.isNight = false;
    this.nightTransition = 0;
    this.roofAlpha = 1;
    this.targetRoofAlpha = 1;
    this.summoning = false;
    this.summonTimer = 0;
    this.summonDelay = 0;
    this.particles = [];
    this.traps = [];
    this.activeClones = [];
    this.activeWalls = [];
    // Resolve base tile type for special markers (F, U, m)
    this.baseTileResolved = new Array(this.mapHeight);
    for (let y = 0; y < this.mapHeight; y++) {
      this.baseTileResolved[y] = new Array(this.mapWidth);
      for (let x = 0; x < this.mapWidth; x++) {
        this.baseTileResolved[y][x] = this.resolveBaseTile(x, y);
      }
    }
    this.buildAutotileCache();
    this.initShops();
    this.setDifficulty();
    updateMapEditor(this);
    this.showMessage('');
    this.hotbarSize = 100;
    this.hotbarLayout = 'horizontal';
    this.hotbarGap = 30;
    this.expandedHotkeyPlayers = new Set();
    preGameKeybinds = {};
  }

  selectArtifactsForRound() {
    // Select N unique random F points for this round
    const needed = this.artifactCount;
    const pool = [...this.artifactFPoints];
    const selected = [];
    while (selected.length < needed && pool.length > 0) {
      const idx = Math.floor(Math.random() * pool.length);
      selected.push(pool[idx]);
      pool.splice(idx, 1);
    }
    // Reset all existing entities
    for (const art of this.artifactEntities) art.collected = false;
    // Ensure we have exactly needed entities at the right positions
    this.artifactEntities = [];
    for (const sp of selected) {
      this.artifactEntities.push(new ArtifactEntity(sp.x, sp.y));
    }
  }

  getMonsterSpeed() {
    let bonus = 0;
    if (this.difficulty === 'easy') bonus = 5;
    else if (this.difficulty === 'medium') bonus = 8;
    else bonus = 12;
    return this.baseMonsterSpeed * (1 + bonus * this.roundNumber / 100);
  }

  getSpeedBonusPercent() {
    if (this.difficulty === 'easy') return this.roundNumber * 5;
    else if (this.difficulty === 'medium') return this.roundNumber * 8;
    else return this.roundNumber * 12;
  }

  setDifficulty() {
    if (this.artifactCount <= 3) this.difficulty = 'easy';
    else if (this.artifactCount <= 4) this.difficulty = 'medium';
    else this.difficulty = 'hard';
  }

  buildAutotileCache() {
    const h = this.mapHeight, w = this.mapWidth;
    this.tileVariation = new Array(h);
    this.tileVariation2 = new Array(h);
    this.tileVariation3 = new Array(h);
    this.tileEdgeMask = new Array(h);
    for (let y = 0; y < h; y++) {
      this.tileVariation[y] = new Float32Array(w);
      this.tileVariation2[y] = new Float32Array(w);
      this.tileVariation3[y] = new Float32Array(w);
      this.tileEdgeMask[y] = new Int8Array(w);
      for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) {
        this.tileVariation[y][x] = Game.tileHash(x, y);
        this.tileVariation2[y][x] = Game.tileHash(x * 7 + 13, y * 11 + 5);
        this.tileVariation3[y][x] = Game.tileHash(x * 3 + 7, y * 17 + 3);
        const resolved = this.baseTileResolved[y] ? this.baseTileResolved[y][x] : this.tiles[y][x];
        if (resolved !== 'C' && resolved !== 'G') {
          this.tileEdgeMask[y][x] = 0;
          continue;
        }
        let mask = 0;
        if (y > 0) {
          const up = this.baseTileResolved[y - 1] ? this.baseTileResolved[y - 1][x] : this.tiles[y - 1][x];
          if (up !== resolved) mask |= 1;
        }
        if (y < h - 1) {
          const down = this.baseTileResolved[y + 1] ? this.baseTileResolved[y + 1][x] : this.tiles[y + 1][x];
          if (down !== resolved) mask |= 2;
        }
        if (x > 0) {
          const left = this.baseTileResolved[y] ? this.baseTileResolved[y][x - 1] : this.tiles[y][x - 1];
          if (left !== resolved) mask |= 4;
        }
        if (x < w - 1) {
          const right = this.baseTileResolved[y] ? this.baseTileResolved[y][x + 1] : this.tiles[y][x + 1];
          if (right !== resolved) mask |= 8;
        }
        this.tileEdgeMask[y][x] = mask;
      }
    }
  }

  restart() {
    this.summoning = false;
    this.activeMonsters = [];
    this.traps = [];
    this.activeClones = [];
    this.activeWalls = [];
    this.ratEntities = [];
    this.currency = 0;
    this.hotbarSize = 100;
    this.hotbarLayout = 'horizontal';
    this.hotbarGap = 30;
    this.expandedHotkeyPlayers = new Set();
    this.loadMap(parseMap(DEFAULT_MAP.join('\n')));
    this.showClassSelection();
  }

  isWalkableTile(tx, ty) {
    if (tx < 0 || ty < 0 || tx >= this.mapWidth || ty >= this.mapHeight) return false;
    const tile = this.tiles[ty][tx];
    if (tile === 'O' || tile === 'W') return false;
    if (tile === 'd') {
      const door = this.doorEntities.find(d => d.tileX === tx && d.tileY === ty);
      if (door && door.solid) return false;
    }
    return true;
  }

  getZoneName(tileX, tileY) {
    if (!this.zones || tileX < 0 || tileY < 0 || tileY >= this.zones.length || tileX >= (this.zones[0] || []).length) return 'Unknown';
    var zc = this.zones[tileY][tileX];
    return ZONE_NAMES[zc] || 'Unknown';
  }

  spawnChest() {
    var freeTiles = [];
    for (var y = 0; y < this.mapHeight; y++) {
      for (var x = 0; x < this.mapWidth; x++) {
        var tile = this.tiles[y][x];
        if (tile !== 'C' && tile !== 'G') continue;
        if (tile === 'd' || tile === 'A' || tile === 'F' || tile === 'S') continue;
        var occupied = false;
        for (const ch of this.chests) { if (ch.tileX === x && ch.tileY === y) { occupied = true; break; } }
        if (occupied) continue;
        for (const pl of this.players) { if (pl.alive && pl.tileX === x && pl.tileY === y) { occupied = true; break; } }
        if (occupied) continue;
        freeTiles.push({ x: x, y: y });
      }
    }
    if (freeTiles.length === 0) return;
    var pos = freeTiles[Math.floor(Math.random() * freeTiles.length)];
    var variants = ['classic', 'patati', 'patata', 'clown', 'red', 'random'];
    var variant = variants[Math.floor(Math.random() * variants.length)];
    this.chests.push({ tileX: pos.x, tileY: pos.y, opened: false, colorVariant: variant, itemKey: null });
    this.showMessage('Um bau spawnou.');
  }

  findSpawnPosition(baseX, baseY, playerIndex) {
    const offsetsX = [0, 1, -1, 2];
    const offsetsY = [0, 0, 0, 0];
    let idx = playerIndex;
    if (idx >= offsetsX.length) idx = 0;
    let tx = baseX + offsetsX[idx];
    let ty = baseY + offsetsY[idx];
    if (this.isWalkableTile(tx, ty)) return { x: tx, y: ty };
    for (let radius = 1; radius <= 5; radius++) {
      for (let dx = -radius; dx <= radius; dx++) {
        const checkX = baseX + dx;
        const checkY = baseY;
        if (this.isWalkableTile(checkX, checkY)) return { x: checkX, y: checkY };
      }
      for (let dy = -radius; dy <= radius; dy++) {
        if (dy === 0) continue;
        for (let dx = -radius; dx <= radius; dx++) {
          const checkX = baseX + dx;
          const checkY = baseY + dy;
          if (this.isWalkableTile(checkX, checkY)) return { x: checkX, y: checkY };
        }
      }
    }
    return { x: baseX, y: baseY };
  }

  start() {
    this.loadMap(parseMap(DEFAULT_MAP.join('\n')));
    this.lastTime = performance.now();
    this.showClassSelection();
  }

  showClassSelection() {
    const sel = document.getElementById('class-select');
    const container = document.getElementById('player-setup-container');
    const descEl = document.getElementById('player-setup-desc');
    const label = document.getElementById('player-count-label');
    const minusBtn = document.getElementById('btn-player-minus');
    const plusBtn = document.getElementById('btn-player-plus');

    label.textContent = this.playerCount + ' Player' + (this.playerCount > 1 ? 's' : '');
    minusBtn.disabled = (this.playerCount <= 1);
    plusBtn.disabled = (this.playerCount >= MAX_PLAYERS);

    // Difficulty selector
    const diffBtns = document.querySelectorAll('#difficulty-selector button');
    diffBtns.forEach(btn => {
      btn.classList.toggle('selected', btn.dataset.diff === this.difficulty);
      btn.onclick = () => {
        this.difficulty = btn.dataset.diff;
        diffBtns.forEach(b => b.classList.remove('selected'));
        btn.classList.add('selected');
      };
    });

    const buildPanels = () => {
      container.innerHTML = '';
      for (let i = 0; i < this.playerCount; i++) {
        const panel = document.createElement('div');
        panel.className = 'player-setup-panel';
        panel.dataset.playerIndex = i;
        panel.innerHTML = '<h3>Player ' + (i + 1) + '</h3>';

        // Name input
        const nameInput = document.createElement('input');
        nameInput.type = 'text';
        nameInput.maxLength = 12;
        nameInput.placeholder = 'Name';
        nameInput.value = this.playerNames[i] || '';
        nameInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
          this.playerNames[i] = nameInput.value || ('P' + (i + 1));
        });
        panel.appendChild(nameInput);

        // Class icons
        const classIcons = document.createElement('div');
        classIcons.className = 'class-icons';
        for (const [key, cls] of Object.entries(PlayerClasses)) {
          const div = document.createElement('div');
          div.textContent = '';
          div.classList.add('class-icon-' + key);
          div.title = cls.name;
          if (this.playerClasses[i] === key) div.classList.add('selected');
          div.addEventListener('click', () => {
            classIcons.querySelectorAll('div').forEach(d => d.classList.remove('selected'));
            div.classList.add('selected');
            this.playerClasses[i] = key;
            descEl.textContent = (i + 1) + ': ' + cls.name + ' - ' + cls.desc;
          });
          div.addEventListener('mouseenter', () => {
            descEl.textContent = (i + 1) + ': ' + cls.name + ' - ' + cls.desc;
          });
          div.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => {
            const cur = this.playerClasses[i];
            if (PlayerClasses[cur]) {
              descEl.textContent = (i + 1) + ': ' + PlayerClasses[cur].name + ' - ' + PlayerClasses[cur].desc;
            } else {
              descEl.textContent = '';
            }
          });
          classIcons.appendChild(div);
        }
        panel.appendChild(classIcons);

        // Hotkey config toggle button
        const toggleBtn = document.createElement('button');
        toggleBtn.className = 'toggle-hotkey-btn';
        toggleBtn.textContent = 'Configure Hotkeys';
        toggleBtn.dataset.playerIndex = i;
        panel.appendChild(toggleBtn);

        toggleBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
          const isExpanded = toggleBtn.textContent === 'Hide Hotkeys';
          if (isExpanded) {
            toggleBtn.textContent = 'Configure Hotkeys';
            const inline = panel.querySelector('.hotkey-config-inline');
            if (inline) inline.remove();
          } else {
            toggleBtn.textContent = 'Hide Hotkeys';
            const inlineDiv = document.createElement('div');
            inlineDiv.className = 'hotkey-config-inline';
            const pi = i;
            const kb = preGameKeybinds[pi] || { ...DEFAULT_KEYBINDS[pi] };
            const actions = [
              { key: 'up', label: 'Move Up' },
              { key: 'down', label: 'Move Down' },
              { key: 'left', label: 'Move Left' },
              { key: 'right', label: 'Move Right' },
              { key: 'interact', label: 'Interact' },
              { key: 'ability', label: 'Ability' },
              { key: 'use', label: 'Use/Cycle Item' }
            ];
            let html = '';
            for (const act of actions) {
              const keyName = (kb[act.key] || '').toString();
              html += '<div class="hotkey-row">';
              html += '<span class="action">' + act.label + '</span>';
              html += '<button class="key-btn" data-player="' + pi + '" data-action="' + act.key + '">' + keyName.toUpperCase() + '</button>';
              html += '</div>';
            }
            inlineDiv.innerHTML = html;
            panel.appendChild(inlineDiv);
            inlineDiv.querySelectorAll('.key-btn').forEach(btn => {
              btn.addEventListener('click', function() {
                const bpi = parseInt(this.dataset.player);
                const act = this.dataset.action;
                rebindingPlayer = bpi;
                rebindingAction = act;
                document.querySelectorAll('.key-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('waiting'));
                this.classList.add('waiting');
                this.textContent = '...';
              });
            });
            checkKeyConflicts();
          }
        });

        container.appendChild(panel);
      }
    };

    minusBtn.onclick = () => { if (this.playerCount > 1) { this.playerCount--; label.textContent = this.playerCount + ' Player' + (this.playerCount > 1 ? 's' : ''); minusBtn.disabled = (this.playerCount <= 1); plusBtn.disabled = (this.playerCount >= MAX_PLAYERS); buildPanels(); } };
    plusBtn.onclick = () => { if (this.playerCount < MAX_PLAYERS) { this.playerCount++; label.textContent = this.playerCount + ' Player' + (this.playerCount > 1 ? 's' : ''); minusBtn.disabled = (this.playerCount <= 1); plusBtn.disabled = (this.playerCount >= MAX_PLAYERS); buildPanels(); } };

    buildPanels();
    descEl.textContent = '';

    document.getElementById('btn-start-game').onclick = () => {
      sel.style.display = 'none';
      this.loadMap(parseMap(DEFAULT_MAP.join('\n')));
      this.lastTime = performance.now();
      this.loop(this.lastTime);
    };
    sel.style.display = 'flex';
  }

  loop(now) {
    const dt = Math.min((now - this.lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
    this.lastTime = now;
    this.update(dt);
    this.render();
    requestAnimationFrame(t => this.loop(t));
  }

  update(dt) {
    if (this.paused) return;
    // Update doors
    for (const door of this.doorEntities) door.update(dt);
    // Push entities out of solid doors
    this.pushEntitiesFromDoors();
    // Update players
    for (const p of this.players) p.update(dt, this);
    // Update monsters
    for (const m of this.activeMonsters) m.update(dt);
    this.updateTraps(dt);
    // Update rat entities (Rat King)
    this.updateRatEntities(dt);
    // Update clones
    for (let i = this.activeClones.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const c = this.activeClones[i];
      if (!c.alive) { this.activeClones.splice(i, 1); continue; }
      c.timer -= dt;
      if (c.timer <= 0 || !c.targetMonster || !c.targetMonster.alive) {
        this.activateCloneCooldown(c.playerIndex);
        this.activeClones.splice(i, 1); continue;
      }
      const mtx = Math.floor(c.targetMonster.px / TILE_SIZE);
      const mty = Math.floor(c.targetMonster.py / TILE_SIZE);
      if (!c.path || c.path.length === 0) c.path = this.findClonePath(c.tileX, c.tileY, mtx, mty);
      if (c.path && c.path.length > 0) {
        const target = c.path[0];
        const tcx = target.x * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2;
        const tcy = target.y * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2;
        const dx = tcx - c.px, dy = tcy - c.py;
        const dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
        const cloneSpeed = 200;
        if (dist < 4) {
          c.path.shift();
          if (c.path.length === 0) { c.tileX = target.x; c.tileY = target.y; }
        } else if (dist > 0.001) {
          c.px += (dx / dist) * cloneSpeed * dt;
          c.py += (dy / dist) * cloneSpeed * dt;
        }
      }
      c.tileX = Math.floor(c.px / TILE_SIZE);
      c.tileY = Math.floor(c.py / TILE_SIZE);
      const mdx = c.targetMonster.px - c.px;
      const mdy = c.targetMonster.py - c.py;
      const mdist = Math.sqrt(mdx * mdx + mdy * mdy);
      if (mdist < TILE_SIZE * 0.8) {
        const stunVal = c.targetMonster.type === 'monstrosity' ? 3 * 0.65 : 3;
        c.targetMonster.stunTimer = Math.max(c.targetMonster.stunTimer || 0, stunVal);
        for (let j = 0; j < 8; j++) {
          const pa = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
          const pspd = 30 + Math.random() * 50;
          this.particles.push(new Particle(c.px, c.py, Math.cos(pa) * pspd, Math.sin(pa) * pspd, 0.4, '#ff4444'));
        }
        this.activateCloneCooldown(c.playerIndex);
        this.activeClones.splice(i, 1);
      }
    }
    // Update walls
    for (let i = this.activeWalls.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const w = this.activeWalls[i];
      w.timer -= dt;
      if (w.timer <= 0) this.activeWalls.splice(i, 1);
    }
    // Update zones
    for (let i = this.activeZones.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const z = this.activeZones[i];
      z.timer -= dt;
      if (z.timer <= 0) this.activeZones.splice(i, 1);
    }
    // Update contaminated tiles (Acrid)
    for (let i = this.contaminatedTiles.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      var ct = this.contaminatedTiles[i];
      ct.timer -= dt;
      if (ct.timer <= 0) this.contaminatedTiles.splice(i, 1);
    }
    // Acrid damage: apply poison debuff on contact
    for (const p of this.players) {
      if (!p.alive) continue;
      for (const ct of this.contaminatedTiles) {
        if (p.tileX === ct.tileX && p.tileY === ct.tileY) {
          if (p.poisonTimer <= 0) {
            p.poisonTimer = 5;
          }
        }
      }
    }
    // Apply poison damage
    for (const p of this.players) {
      if (!p.alive) continue;
      if (p.poisonTimer > 0) {
        p.poisonTimer -= dt;
        p.poisonTick += dt;
        if (p.poisonTick >= 1.0) {
          p.poisonTick -= 1.0;
          p.hp -= 3;
          if (p.hp <= 0) p.takeDamage(1, this);
        }
      } else {
        p.poisonTick = 0;
      }
    }
    // Update contaminated particles
    for (let i = this.contaminatedParticles.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      var cp = this.contaminatedParticles[i];
      cp.life -= dt;
      cp.y -= cp.vy * dt;
      if (cp.life <= 0) this.contaminatedParticles.splice(i, 1);
    }
    // Update smoke clouds
    for (let i = this.smokeClouds.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      var sc = this.smokeClouds[i];
      sc.timer -= dt;
      if (sc.timer <= 0) this.smokeClouds.splice(i, 1);
    }
    for (const p of this.players) {
      if (!p.alive) continue;
      if (p.invisibleTimer <= 0) p.invisible = false;
      for (const sc of this.smokeClouds) {
        var sdx = p.px - sc.x;
        var sdy = p.py - sc.y;
        if (Math.sqrt(sdx*sdx + sdy*sdy) <= sc.radius) { p.invisible = true; break; }
      }
      for (const pw of this.phasedWalls) {
        if (p.tileX === pw.tileX && p.tileY === pw.tileY) { p.invisible = true; break; }
      }
    }
    // Update phased walls
    for (let i = this.phasedWalls.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      var pw = this.phasedWalls[i];
      pw.timer -= dt;
      if (pw.timer <= 0) {
        for (const pl of this.players) {
          if (pl.alive && pl.tileX === pw.tileX && pl.tileY === pw.tileY) {
            var closestWalkable = null, closestDist = Infinity;
            for (var ey = -1; ey <= 1; ey++) {
              for (var ex = -1; ex <= 1; ex++) {
                if (ex === 0 && ey === 0) continue;
                var nx = pw.tileX + ex, ny = pw.tileY + ey;
                if (nx >= 0 && nx < this.mapWidth && ny >= 0 && ny < this.mapHeight) {
                  var nt = this.tiles[ny][nx];
                  var neff = this.baseTileResolved[ny] ? this.baseTileResolved[ny][nx] : nt;
                  if (nt !== 'O' && nt !== 'W' && (neff === 'C' || neff === 'G')) {
                    var ndist = Math.abs(ex) + Math.abs(ey);
                    if (ndist < closestDist) { closestDist = ndist; closestWalkable = { x: nx, y: ny }; }
                  }
                }
              }
            }
            if (closestWalkable) {
              pl.px = closestWalkable.x * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE/2;
              pl.py = closestWalkable.y * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE/2;
              pl.tileX = closestWalkable.x;
              pl.tileY = closestWalkable.y;
            }
            pl.phasing = false;
          }
        }
        this.phasedWalls.splice(i, 1);
      }
    }
    // Update active bombs
    for (let i = this.activeBombs.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      var b = this.activeBombs[i];
      b.timer -= dt;
      if (b.timer <= 0) { this.activeBombs.splice(i, 1); continue; }
      for (const pl of this.players) {
        if (!pl.alive) continue;
        if (pl.tileX === b.tileX && pl.tileY === b.tileY) {
          pl.takeDamage(15, this);
          this.activeBombs.splice(i, 1);
          this.showMessage(pl.name + ' stepped on a bomb!');
          break;
        }
      }
    }
    // Update healing auras
    for (let i = this.activeAuras.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      var au = this.activeAuras[i];
      au.timer -= dt;
      if (au.timer <= 0) {
        if (au.player) au.player.healingAuraSlow = false;
        this.activeAuras.splice(i, 1);
        continue;
      }
      for (const pl of this.players) {
        if (!pl.alive) continue;
        var adx = pl.px - au.player.px;
        var ady = pl.py - au.player.py;
        if (Math.sqrt(adx*adx + ady*ady) <= au.radius) {
          pl.hp = Math.min(pl.maxHp, pl.hp + au.healRate * dt);
        }
      }
    }
    // Update chests
    if (this.chestPendingSpawn) {
      this.chestPendingSpawnTimer -= dt;
      if (this.chestPendingSpawnTimer <= 0) {
        this.chestPendingSpawn = false;
        this.chestFirstSpawned = true;
        this.spawnChest();
      }
    }
    if (this.activeMonsters.some(function(m) { return m.alive; })) {
      this.chestSpawnTimer += dt;
      if (this.chestSpawnTimer >= 60 && this.chests.length < 3) {
        this.chestSpawnTimer = 0;
        this.spawnChest();
      }
    }
    for (const p of this.players) {
      if (!p.alive) continue;
      for (const ch of this.chests) {
        if (ch.opened) continue;
        var cdx = (ch.tileX + 0.5) * TILE_SIZE - p.px;
        var cdy = (ch.tileY + 0.5) * TILE_SIZE - p.py;
        if (Math.sqrt(cdx*cdx + cdy*cdy) < TILE_SIZE * 0.8) {
          if (!p.hasSpace()) { this.showMessage(p.name + "'s inventory is full!"); continue; }
          var keys = Object.keys(Items);
          if (this.playerCount < 2) {
            keys = keys.filter(function(k) { return k !== 'genes_hand' && k !== 'healing_aura'; });
          }
          var randKey = keys[Math.floor(Math.random() * keys.length)];
          p.addToInventory(randKey);
          ch.opened = true;
          ch.itemKey = randKey;
          this.showMessage(p.name + ' opened a chest! Got ' + Items[randKey].name + '!');
          for (var cpi = 0; cpi < 8; cpi++) {
            this.particles.push(new Particle((ch.tileX+0.5)*TILE_SIZE, (ch.tileY+0.5)*TILE_SIZE, (Math.random()-0.5)*60, (Math.random()-0.5)*60, 0.5, '#ffd700'));
          }
          break;
        }
      }
    }
    // Periodic locked door check
    if (this.lockedDoor) {
      this.lockedDoorCheckTimer = (this.lockedDoorCheckTimer || 0) + dt;
      if (this.lockedDoor.timer !== undefined) {
        this.lockedDoor.timer -= dt;
        if (this.lockedDoor.timer <= 0) {
          for (var ldi2 = 0; ldi2 < this.lockedDoor.tiles.length; ldi2++) {
            var lt2 = this.lockedDoor.tiles[ldi2];
            if (lt2.doorRef) lt2.doorRef.lockCooldown = 1.0;
          }
          this.lockedDoor = null;
        }
      }
      if (this.lockedDoor && this.lockedDoorCheckTimer >= 1.0) {
        this.lockedDoorCheckTimer -= 1.0;
        for (var lcti = 0; lcti < this.lockedDoor.tiles.length; lcti++) {
          var lct = this.lockedDoor.tiles[lcti];
          var door = lct.doorRef;
          if (door && door.open) {
            door.open = false;
            door.animating = false;
            door.animTimer = 0;
            door.animProgress = 0;
          }
        }
      }
    }
    // Update artifacts
    for (const art of this.artifactEntities) art.update(dt);
    // Update particles
    for (let i = this.particles.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      this.particles[i].update(dt);
      if (!this.particles[i].alive) this.particles.splice(i, 1);
    }
    // Update message
    if (this.messageTimer > 0) { this.messageTimer -= dt; if (this.messageTimer <= 0) this.showMessage(''); }
    // Day/night transition
    const anyMonsterAlive = this.activeMonsters.some(m => m.alive);
    this.targetRoofAlpha = this.computeRoofAlpha();
    this.roofAlpha += (this.targetRoofAlpha - this.roofAlpha) * Math.min(dt * 4, 1);
    if (anyMonsterAlive && !this.isNight) {
      this.nightTransition = Math.min(1, this.nightTransition + dt * 0.8);
      if (this.nightTransition >= 1) this.isNight = true;
    } else if (!anyMonsterAlive && this.isNight) {
      this.nightTransition = Math.max(0, this.nightTransition - dt * 1.2);
      if (this.nightTransition <= 0) this.isNight = false;
    } else if (!anyMonsterAlive && !this.isNight && this.nightTransition > 0) {
      this.nightTransition = Math.max(0, this.nightTransition - dt * 1.2);
    }
    // Summon delay
    if (this.summonDelay > 0) {
      this.summonDelay -= dt;
      if (this.summonDelay <= 0) {
        this.spawnMonster();
      }
    }
    // Check game state
    this.checkGameState();
    // Update player vision ranges (indoor/outdoor)
    this.updateVisionRanges(dt);
    // Camera
    this.updateCamera();
    // Update HUD
    updateHUD(this);
    updateAbilityHUD(this);
    updateHotbarHUD(this);
    // Consume justPressed
    for (const k in keysJustPressed) keysJustPressed[k] = false;
  }

  computeRoofAlpha() {
    if (this.debugMode) return 0;
    // If any player is inside, no roof
    for (const pl of this.players) {
      if (!pl.alive) continue;
      const pt = pl.tileX;
      const pty = pl.tileY;
      const tile = (pty >= 0 && pty < this.mapHeight && pt >= 0 && pt < this.mapWidth)
        ? this.tiles[pty][pt] : 'G';
      const effectiveTile = (pty >= 0 && pty < this.mapHeight && pt >= 0 && pt < this.mapWidth)
        ? (this.baseTileResolved[pty] ? this.baseTileResolved[pty][pt] : tile) : 'G';
      if (effectiveTile !== 'G' && effectiveTile !== 'O') return 0;
    }
    // All alive players outside - check near open doors
    for (const pl of this.players) {
      if (!pl.alive) continue;
      const pt = pl.tileX;
      const pty = pl.tileY;
      for (let di = 0; di < this.doorEntities.length; di++) {
        const door = this.doorEntities[di];
        if (!door.open && !door.animating) continue;
        if (door.hasGroup()) {
          // For grouped doors, check distance to group bounding box
          const members = door.groupMembers;
          let gMinX = Infinity, gMaxX = -Infinity, gMinY = Infinity, gMaxY = -Infinity;
          for (let gi = 0; gi < members.length; gi++) {
            if (members[gi].tileX < gMinX) gMinX = members[gi].tileX;
            if (members[gi].tileX > gMaxX) gMaxX = members[gi].tileX;
            if (members[gi].tileY < gMinY) gMinY = members[gi].tileY;
            if (members[gi].tileY > gMaxY) gMaxY = members[gi].tileY;
          }
          // Manhattan distance to nearest edge of bounding box
          const ddx = pt < gMinX ? gMinX - pt : (pt > gMaxX ? pt - gMaxX : 0);
          const ddy = pty < gMinY ? gMinY - pty : (pty > gMaxY ? pty - gMaxY : 0);
          const dist = Math.sqrt(ddx * ddx + ddy * ddy);
          if (dist < 3) return Math.max(0, (dist - 1) / 2);
        } else {
          const dx = door.tileX - pt;
          const dy = door.tileY - pty;
          const dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
          if (dist < 3) return Math.max(0, (dist - 1) / 2);
        }
      }
    }
    return 1;
  }

  pushEntitiesFromDoors() {
    for (const door of this.doorEntities) {
      if (!door.solid) continue;
      for (const p of this.players) {
        if (!p.alive) continue;
        this.pushOut(p, door.tileX, door.tileY);
      }
      for (const m of this.activeMonsters) {
        if (!m.alive) continue;
        this.pushOut(m, door.tileX, door.tileY);
      }
    }
  }

  pushOut(entity, doorTX, doorTY) {
    const et = Math.floor(entity.px / TILE_SIZE);
    const ety = Math.floor(entity.py / TILE_SIZE);
    if (et !== doorTX || ety !== doorTY) return;
    const dirs = [[0, -1], [0, 1], [-1, 0], [1, 0]];
    for (const [dx, dy] of dirs) {
      const nx = doorTX + dx, ny = doorTY + dy;
      if (nx < 0 || ny < 0 || nx >= this.mapWidth || ny >= this.mapHeight) continue;
         const tile = this.tiles[ny][nx];
         if (tile === 'O') continue;
         if (tile === 'W' && !walkOverW) continue;
      if (tile === 'd') {
        const d = this.doorEntities.find(dr => dr.tileX === nx && dr.tileY === ny);
        if (d && d.solid) continue;
      }
      entity.px = nx * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2;
      entity.py = ny * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2;
      return;
    }
  }

  updateCamera() {
    const alive = this.players.filter(p => p.alive);
    if (alive.length === 0) return;
    const mapW = this.mapWidth * TILE_SIZE;
    const mapH = this.mapHeight * TILE_SIZE;
    if (alive.length === 1) {
      // Single player: follow player, full map visible
      const p = alive[0];
      const targetCamX = p.px - mapW / 2;
      const targetCamY = p.py - mapH / 2;
      this.camX += (targetCamX - this.camX) * 0.15;
      this.camY += (targetCamY - this.camY) * 0.15;
      this.camScale += (1 - this.camScale) * 0.15;
    } else {
      // Multiplayer: show entire map
      this.camX += (0 - this.camX) * 0.1;
      this.camY += (0 - this.camY) * 0.1;
      this.camScale += (1 - this.camScale) * 0.1;
    }
  }

  updateVisibility() {
    const vRadius = this.isNight ? 5 : 12;
    const fogDecay = this.isNight ? 0.15 : 0.4;
    if (this.debugMode) {
      for (let y = 0; y < this.mapHeight; y++)
        for (let x = 0; x < this.mapWidth; x++)
          this.visibilityGrid[y][x] = 2;
      return;
    }
    // Age all tiles
    for (let y = 0; y < this.mapHeight; y++) {
      for (let x = 0; x < this.mapWidth; x++) {
        if (this.visibilityAge[y][x] > 0) {
          this.visibilityAge[y][x] -= fogDecay * 0.02;
          if (this.visibilityAge[y][x] <= 0) {
            this.visibilityAge[y][x] = 0;
            this.visibilityGrid[y][x] = 0;
          } else if (this.visibilityGrid[y][x] === 2) {
            this.visibilityGrid[y][x] = 1;
          }
        } else if (this.visibilityGrid[y][x] > 0) {
          this.visibilityGrid[y][x] = 0;
        }
      }
    }
    // Check visible tiles for each alive player (union)
    for (const pl of this.players) {
      if (!pl.alive) continue;
      const pt = pl.tileX;
      const pty = pl.tileY;
      for (let dy = -vRadius; dy <= vRadius; dy++) {
        for (let dx = -vRadius; dx <= vRadius; dx++) {
          const tx = pt + dx, ty = pty + dy;
          if (tx < 0 || ty < 0 || tx >= this.mapWidth || ty >= this.mapHeight) continue;
          if (dx * dx + dy * dy <= vRadius * vRadius) {
            if (this.hasLineOfSight(pt, pty, tx, ty)) {
              this.visibilityGrid[ty][tx] = 2;
              this.visibilityAge[ty][tx] = 1;
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }

  hasLineOfSight(x1, y1, x2, y2) {
    const dx = Math.abs(x2 - x1), dy = Math.abs(y2 - y1);
    const sx = x1 < x2 ? 1 : -1, sy = y1 < y2 ? 1 : -1;
    let err = dx - dy, cx = x1, cy = y1;
    const steps = dx + dy;
    for (let i = 0; i < steps; i++) {
      if (cx === x2 && cy === y2) break;
      const e2 = err * 2;
      if (e2 > -dy) { err -= dy; cx += sx; }
      if (e2 < dx) { err += dx; cy += sy; }
      if (cx >= 0 && cx < this.mapWidth && cy >= 0 && cy < this.mapHeight) {
        if (this.tiles[cy][cx] === 'O') return false;
        if (this.tiles[cy][cx] === 'd') {
          const door = this.doorEntities.find(d => d.tileX === cx && d.tileY === cy);
          if (!door || door.solid) return false;
        }
      }
    }
    return true;
  }

  findPath(sx, sy, gx, gy, walkOverW) {
    if (sx === gx && sy === gy) return [{ x: gx, y: gy }];
    const w = this.mapWidth, h = this.mapHeight;
    if (sx < 0 || sy < 0 || sx >= w || sy >= h ||
        gx < 0 || gy < 0 || gx >= w || gy >= h) return null;
    const visited = new Array(h);
    const parent = new Array(h);
    for (let i = 0; i < h; i++) {
      visited[i] = new Array(w).fill(false);
      parent[i] = new Array(w).fill(null);
    }
    const queue = [{ x: sx, y: sy }];
    visited[sy][sx] = true;
    let head = 0;
    const dirs = [[0, -1], [0, 1], [-1, 0], [1, 0]];
    while (head < queue.length) {
      const { x: cx, y: cy } = queue[head++];
      if (cx === gx && cy === gy) {
        const path = [];
        let bx = cx, by = cy;
        while (bx !== sx || by !== sy) {
          path.unshift({ x: bx, y: by });
          const p = parent[by][bx];
          if (!p) break;
          bx = p.x; by = p.y;
        }
        return path;
      }
      for (const [dx, dy] of dirs) {
        const nx = cx + dx, ny = cy + dy;
        if (nx < 0 || ny < 0 || nx >= w || ny >= h || visited[ny][nx]) continue;
         const tile = this.tiles[ny][nx];
        if (tile === 'O' || tile === 'W') continue;
        if (tile === 'd' && this.lockedDoor && this.lockedDoor.tiles.some(function(t) { return t.tileX === nx && t.tileY === ny; })) continue;
        if (this.isWallTile(nx, ny)) continue;
        visited[ny][nx] = true;
        parent[ny][nx] = { x: cx, y: cy };
        queue.push({ x: nx, y: ny });
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  isWallTile(tx, ty) {
    for (const w of (this.activeWalls || [])) {
      for (const t of w.tiles) {
        if (t.x === tx && t.y === ty) return true;
      }
    }
    return false;
  }

  findClonePath(sx, sy, gx, gy) {
    if (sx === gx && sy === gy) return [{ x: gx, y: gy }];
    const w = this.mapWidth, h = this.mapHeight;
    if (sx < 0 || sy < 0 || sx >= w || sy >= h ||
        gx < 0 || gy < 0 || gx >= w || gy >= h) return null;
    const visited = new Array(h);
    const parent = new Array(h);
    for (let i = 0; i < h; i++) {
      visited[i] = new Array(w).fill(false);
      parent[i] = new Array(w).fill(null);
    }
    const queue = [{ x: sx, y: sy }];
    visited[sy][sx] = true;
    let head = 0;
    const dirs = [[0, -1], [0, 1], [-1, 0], [1, 0]];
    while (head < queue.length) {
      const { x: cx, y: cy } = queue[head++];
      if (cx === gx && cy === gy) {
        const path = [];
        let bx = cx, by = cy;
        while (bx !== sx || by !== sy) {
          path.unshift({ x: bx, y: by });
          const p = parent[by][bx];
          if (!p) break;
          bx = p.x; by = p.y;
        }
        return path;
      }
      for (const [dx, dy] of dirs) {
        const nx = cx + dx, ny = cy + dy;
        if (nx < 0 || ny < 0 || nx >= w || ny >= h || visited[ny][nx]) continue;
        const tile = this.tiles[ny][nx];
        if (tile === 'O' || tile === 'W') continue;
        if (tile === 'd' && this.lockedDoor && this.lockedDoor.tiles.some(function(t) { return t.tileX === nx && t.tileY === ny; })) continue;
        visited[ny][nx] = true;
        parent[ny][nx] = { x: cx, y: cy };
        queue.push({ x: nx, y: ny });
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  activateCloneCooldown(playerIndex) {
    for (const p of this.players) {
      if (p.playerIndex === playerIndex && p.playerClass === 'redhacker') {
        p.abilityCooldown = PlayerClasses.redhacker.cooldown;
        break;
      }
    }
  }

  // ============================================================
  // RAYCAST LIGHTING SYSTEM
  // ============================================================
  castRay(px, py, dx, dy, maxDist) {
    const step = 3;
    let x = px, y = py;
    let dist = 0;
    while (dist < maxDist) {
      dist += step;
      x += dx * step;
      y += dy * step;
      const tx = Math.floor(x / TILE_SIZE);
      const ty = Math.floor(y / TILE_SIZE);
      if (tx < 0 || ty < 0 || tx >= this.mapWidth || ty >= this.mapHeight) break;
      const tile = this.tiles[ty][tx];
      if (tile === 'O') {
        x -= dx * step;
        y -= dy * step;
        break;
      }
      if (tile === 'd') {
        const door = this.doorEntities.find(d => d.tileX === tx && d.tileY === ty);
        if (!door || door.solid) {
          x -= dx * step;
          y -= dy * step;
          break;
        }
      }
    }
    return { x, y };
  }

  updateVisionRanges(dt) {
    const indoorRange = Math.sqrt(this.mapWidth * this.mapWidth + this.mapHeight * this.mapHeight) * TILE_SIZE;
    const outdoorRange = 160; // 5 tiles
    const lerpSpeed = 3;
    for (let i = 0; i < this.players.length; i++) {
      const p = this.players[i];
      if (!p.alive) { this.playerVisionRange[i] = outdoorRange; continue; }
      const tx = p.tileX, ty = p.tileY;
      let target;
      if (tx < 0 || ty < 0 || tx >= this.mapWidth || ty >= this.mapHeight) {
        target = outdoorRange;
      } else {
        const rawTile = this.tiles[ty][tx];
        const effectiveTile = this.baseTileResolved[ty] ? this.baseTileResolved[ty][tx] : rawTile;
        target = (effectiveTile === 'G' || effectiveTile === 'O') ? outdoorRange : indoorRange;
      }
      if (this.playerVisionRange[i] === undefined) this.playerVisionRange[i] = target;
      this.playerVisionRange[i] += (target - this.playerVisionRange[i]) * Math.min(dt * lerpSpeed, 1);
    }
  }

  buildVisibilityPolygon(player) {
    const baseRange = this.isNight ? 160 : 384;
    const playerRange = this.playerVisionRange[player.playerIndex] || baseRange;
    const maxDist = playerRange;
    const numRays = this.isNight ? 360 : 220;
    const points = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < numRays; i++) {
      const angle = (i / numRays) * Math.PI * 2;
      const dx = Math.cos(angle);
      const dy = Math.sin(angle);
      const hit = this.castRay(player.px, player.py, dx, dy, maxDist);
      points.push(hit);
    }
    for (let i = 0; i < points.length; i++) {
      const a = (i / numRays) * Math.PI * 2;
      const jitter = 2 + Math.sin(i * 7.3) * 1.5;
      points[i] = {
        x: points[i].x + Math.cos(a) * jitter,
        y: points[i].y + Math.sin(a) * jitter
      };
    }
    return points;
  }

  buildLightmap() {
    const W = this.canvas.width;
    const H = this.canvas.height;
    if (!this.lightmapCanvas || this.lightmapCanvas.width !== W || this.lightmapCanvas.height !== H) {
      this.lightmapCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
      this.lightmapCanvas.width = W;
      this.lightmapCanvas.height = H;
    }
    const lctx = this.lightmapCanvas.getContext('2d');
    const nightAlpha = 0.55 + this.nightTransition * 0.15;
    lctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);
    // Fill entire map with darkness
    lctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,' + nightAlpha + ')';
    lctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);

    this.visibilityPolygons = [];
    for (const pl of this.players) {
      if (!pl.alive) continue;
      const points = this.buildVisibilityPolygon(pl);
      if (points.length < 3) continue;
      this.visibilityPolygons.push({ player: pl, points: points });
      const sx = pl.px - this.camX;
      const sy = pl.py - this.camY;
      lctx.save();
      lctx.beginPath();
      lctx.moveTo(points[0].x - this.camX, points[0].y - this.camY);
      for (let i = 1; i < points.length; i++) {
        lctx.lineTo(points[i].x - this.camX, points[i].y - this.camY);
      }
      lctx.closePath();
      lctx.clip();
      // Erase darkness inside polygon using destination-out
      lctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-out';
      const maxR = this.playerVisionRange[pl.playerIndex] || (this.isNight ? 160 : 384);
      const grad = lctx.createRadialGradient(sx, sy, 0, sx, sy, maxR);
      grad.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(0,0,0,1)');
      grad.addColorStop(0.25, 'rgba(0,0,0,0.85)');
      grad.addColorStop(0.5, 'rgba(0,0,0,0.55)');
      grad.addColorStop(0.75, 'rgba(0,0,0,0.2)');
      grad.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,0.0)');
      lctx.fillStyle = grad;
      lctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
      lctx.restore();
    }
    return this.lightmapCanvas;
  }

  isPointVisible(px, py) {
    if (this.debugMode) return true;
    for (const vp of this.visibilityPolygons) {
      const pts = vp.points;
      let inside = false;
      for (let i = 0, j = pts.length - 1; i < pts.length; j = i++) {
        const xi = pts[i].x, yi = pts[i].y;
        const xj = pts[j].x, yj = pts[j].y;
        if (((yi > py) !== (yj > py)) && (px < (xj - xi) * (py - yi) / (yj - yi) + xi)) {
          inside = !inside;
        }
      }
      if (inside) return true;
    }
    return false;
  }

  checkGameState() {
    if (this.activeMonsters.length === 0 || this.activeMonsters.every(m => !m.alive)) return;
    let allCollected = true;
    for (const art of this.artifactEntities) {
      if (!art.isFake && !art.collected) { allCollected = false; break; }
    }
    if (allCollected) {
      this.artifactEntities = this.artifactEntities.filter(art => !art.isFake);
      for (const m of this.activeMonsters) m.die();
      this.activeMonsters = [];
      this.summoning = false;
      this.collectedArtifacts = 0;
      this.isNight = false;
      for (const p of this.players) {
        if (!p.alive) {
          p.alive = true;
          p.hp = p.maxHp;
          p.px = this.playerSpawn.x * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2;
          p.py = this.playerSpawn.y * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2;
          p.tileX = this.playerSpawn.x;
          p.tileY = this.playerSpawn.y;
          p.invulnTimer = 1.5;
        }
      }
      this.showMessage('Round ' + this.roundNumber + ' complete! Monster defeated!');
      // Round coins reward
      var coinReward = 0;
      if (this.difficulty === 'easy') coinReward = 5;
      else if (this.difficulty === 'medium') coinReward = 10;
      else if (this.difficulty === 'hard') coinReward = 15;
      if (this.currency < 30) {
        this.currency += coinReward;
        this.showMessage('Round ' + this.roundNumber + ' complete! +' + coinReward + ' coins!');
      } else {
        this.showMessage('Round ' + this.roundNumber + ' complete! Max coins reached (30).');
      }
      // Clean item effects
      for (const p of this.players) {
        p.speedItemTimer = 0;
        p.speedItemMult = 1;
        p.commandYellTimer = 0;
        p.commandYellMult = 0;
        p.timeTravelUsed = false;
        let spd = p.baseSpeed;
        if (p.abilityActive && p.playerClass === 'scout') spd *= 1.5;
        p.speed = spd;
      }
    this.activeClones = [];
    this.activeWalls = [];
    this.activeZones = [];
    this.lockedDoor = null;
    this.lockedDoorCheckTimer = 0;
    this.ratEntities = [];
    this.chests = [];
    this.chestSpawnTimer = 0;
    this.chestFirstSpawned = false;
    this.contaminatedTiles = [];
    this.contaminatedParticles = [];
    this.activeBombs = [];
    this.smokeClouds = [];
    this.phasedWalls = [];
    this.activeAuras = [];
    for (const p of this.players) {
      p.shieldCharges = 0;
      p.luckyCharmActive = false;
      p.healingAuraSlow = false;
      p.stationaryTimer = 0;
    }
    for (var ri = 0; ri < this.players.length; ri++) {
      var rp = this.players[ri];
          if (rp.playerClass === 'wiredgirl') { rp.wiredGirlUsed = false; rp.abilityCooldown = 0; }
          if (rp.playerClass === 'rerroler') { rp.rerrolerUsed = false; rp.abilityCooldown = 0; }
          if (rp.playerClass === 'helpfulpriest') { rp.helpfulpriestUsed = false; }
          if (rp.playerClass === 'timetraveler') { rp.timeTravelUsed = false; rp.abilityCooldown = 0; }
          if (rp.playerClass === 'allywarp') { rp.allyWarpUses = 2; }
    }
    this.expandedHotkeyPlayers = new Set();
      // Round reward: random item by rarity
      for (const p of this.players) {
        if (!p.alive) continue;
        if (p.hasSpace()) {
          const key = getRandomItemByRarity(this);
          if (key) {
            p.addToInventory(key);
            const rarityName = Rarities[Items[key].rarity] ? Rarities[Items[key].rarity].name : '';
            this.showMessage(p.name + ' received ' + Items[key].name + ' (' + rarityName + ')!');
          }
        }
      }
      this.restockShops();
    }
  }

  respawnArtifacts() {
    // Find all artifact spawn points
    const spawnPoints = [];
    for (let y = 0; y < this.mapHeight; y++) {
      for (let x = 0; x < this.mapWidth; x++) {
        if (this.tiles[y][x] === 'F') spawnPoints.push({ x, y });
      }
    }
    // Reset existing artifacts
    for (const art of this.artifactEntities) art.collected = false;
    // If not enough entities, create more
    while (this.artifactEntities.length < this.artifactCount && spawnPoints.length > 0) {
      const sp = spawnPoints[Math.floor(Math.random() * spawnPoints.length)];
      this.artifactEntities.push(new ArtifactEntity(sp.x, sp.y));
    }
  }

  spawnMonster() {
    this.summonCount++;
    this.roundNumber++;
    const typeKey = this.pendingMonsterType || ((this.debugMode && this.debugSelectedMonster) ? this.debugSelectedMonster : weightedRandom(MonsterTypes));
    this.pendingMonsterType = null;
    const mt = MonsterTypes[typeKey];
    this.monsterSpawnType = typeKey;
    this.nameMessage = '';
    this.showMessage(mt.desc, 5.5);
    this.chestPendingSpawn = true;
    this.chestPendingSpawnTimer = 5.5;
    const spd = this.getMonsterSpeed() * (mt.speedMod || 1);
    this.selectArtifactsForRound();
    let spawnX = this.monsterSpawn.x;
    let spawnY = this.monsterSpawn.y;
    if (mt.randomSpawn && this.randomMonsterSpawnPoints.length > 0) {
      const rp = this.randomMonsterSpawnPoints[Math.floor(Math.random() * this.randomMonsterSpawnPoints.length)];
      spawnX = rp.x;
      spawnY = rp.y;
    }
    for (let i = 0; i < mt.spawnCount; i++) {
      var stx = spawnX;
      var sty = spawnY;
      if (i > 0 && typeKey === 'twins') {
        var foundPos = false;
        for (var tries = 0; tries < 20; tries++) {
          var tox = spawnX + Math.floor((Math.random() - 0.5) * 6);
          var toy = spawnY + Math.floor((Math.random() - 0.5) * 6);
          if (tox >= 0 && tox < this.mapWidth && toy >= 0 && toy < this.mapHeight) {
            var tt = this.tiles[toy][tox];
            if (tt !== 'O' && tt !== 'W') { stx = tox; sty = toy; foundPos = true; break; }
          }
        }
        if (!foundPos) { stx = Math.round(spawnX + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2); sty = Math.round(spawnY + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2); }
      } else if (i > 0) {
        stx = Math.round(spawnX + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2);
        sty = Math.round(spawnY + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2);
      }
      const m = new MonsterEntity(
        Math.max(0, Math.min(this.mapWidth - 1, stx)),
        Math.max(0, Math.min(this.mapHeight - 1, sty)),
        this, typeKey
      );
      m.speed = spd;
      m.spawn();
      this.activeMonsters.push(m);
    }
    if (typeKey === 'ratking') {
      const king = this.activeMonsters[this.activeMonsters.length - 1];
      this.ratEntities = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
        this.ratEntities.push({
          px: king.px + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 20,
          py: king.py + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 20,
          tileX: king.tileX, tileY: king.tileY,
          targetTrap: null, devourTimer: 0,
          path: null, pathTarget: null, alive: true,
          kingRef: king
        });
      }
    }
    if (typeKey === 'decoy') {
      const decoyM = this.activeMonsters[this.activeMonsters.length - 1];
      const fakeNames = ['artifact', 'Artefact', 'Articfact', 'Artfact'];
      const fakeDisplayName = fakeNames[Math.floor(Math.random() * fakeNames.length)];
      let attempts = 0;
      let fx, fy;
      do {
        fx = Math.floor(Math.random() * this.mapWidth);
        fy = Math.floor(Math.random() * this.mapHeight);
        attempts++;
      } while (attempts < 200 &&
        (this.tiles[fy][fx] === 'O' || this.tiles[fy][fx] === 'W' ||
         this.tiles[fy][fx] === 'd'));
      const fakeArt = new ArtifactEntity(fx, fy);
      fakeArt.displayName = fakeDisplayName;
      fakeArt.isFake = true;
      fakeArt.decoyRef = decoyM;
      this.artifactEntities.push(fakeArt);
    }
  }

  collectArtifact(art) {
    if (art.collected) return;
    if (art.isFake) {
      art.collected = true;
      this.showMessage('WARNING: It was a fake artifact! The Decoy is coming!');
      const decoy = art.decoyRef;
      if (decoy && decoy.alive) {
        for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
          this.particles.push(new Particle(
            decoy.px, decoy.py,
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 100, (Math.random() - 0.5) * 100,
            0.5, '#9966ff'
          ));
        }
        decoy.px = art.tileX * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2;
        decoy.py = art.tileY * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2;
        decoy.tileX = art.tileX;
        decoy.tileY = art.tileY;
        decoy.path = null;
        decoy.decoySpeedBoostTimer = 10;
        for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
          this.particles.push(new Particle(
            decoy.px, decoy.py,
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 100, (Math.random() - 0.5) * 100,
            0.5, '#9966ff'
          ));
        }
        decoy.state = 'chase';
        decoy.chaseTimer = 0;
        decoy.path = null;
      }
      return;
    }
    art.collected = true;
    this.collectedArtifacts++;
    this.showMessage('Artifact collected! (' + this.collectedArtifacts + '/' + this.artifactCount + ')');
    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
      this.particles.push(new Particle(
        art.tileX * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2,
        art.tileY * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2,
        (Math.random() - 0.5) * 100, (Math.random() - 0.5) * 100,
        0.5, '#00cccc'
      ));
    }
    var hasArchaeologist = this.players.some(function(p) { return p.playerClass === 'archaeologist'; });
    if (hasArchaeologist) { this.currency += 1; this.showMessage('Archaeologist bonus: +1 coin!'); }
  }

  updateInteraction(player) {
    const target = player.interactTarget;
    const type = player.interactType;
    if (!target || !type) return;
    let duration = 0;
    if (type === 'door') duration = 0.3;
    else if (type === 'altar') duration = 2;
    else if (type === 'artifact') duration = 1.5 + 2.5 * Math.max(0, this.playerCount - 1);
    this.interactDisplay = { type, duration, target };
    this.interactDuration = duration;
    if (player.interactTimer >= duration) {
      player.interacting = false;
      player.interactTimer = 0;
      if (type === 'door') target.toggle();
      else if (type === 'altar' && !this.summoning) {
        this.summoning = true;
        this.summonDelay = 5;
        for (var ri = 0; ri < this.players.length; ri++) {
          var rp = this.players[ri];
          if (!rp.alive) continue;
          rp.abilityCooldown = 0;
          rp.abilityActive = false;
          rp.abilityTimer = 0;
          if (rp.playerClass === 'wiredgirl') { rp.wiredGirlUsed = false; }
          if (rp.playerClass === 'rerroler') { rp.rerrolerUsed = false; }
          if (rp.playerClass === 'helpfulpriest') { rp.helpfulpriestUsed = false; }
          if (rp.playerClass === 'timetraveler') { rp.timeTravelUsed = false; }
          if (rp.playerClass === 'allywarp') { rp.allyWarpUses = 2; }
        }
        const typeKey = this.selectedMonsterType || weightedRandom(MonsterTypes);
        this.pendingMonsterType = typeKey;
        this.nameMessage = MonsterTypes[typeKey].name + ' ouviu voce. Corra.';
        this.showMessage(this.nameMessage, this.summonDelay);
      }
      else if (type === 'artifact') this.collectArtifact(target);
      else if (type === 'shop') {
        this.buyFromShop(player, target);
      }
      else if (type === 'trash') {
        this.sellItem(player);
      }
    }
  }

  useItem(player) {
    const item = player.getEquippedItem();
    if (!item) return;
    const it = Items[item];
    if (!it) return;
    if (it.type === 'trap') {
      if (!it.stun && it.stun !== 0 && !it.tauntDuration) return;
      let bonusRadius = 0, bonusStun = 0;
      if (player.playerClass === 'boringtrapper' && item === 'beartrap') {
        bonusRadius = it.trapRadius;
        bonusStun = 1;
      }
      const trapObj = { x: player.px, y: player.py, item: item, active: true,
        stun: (it.stun || 0) + bonusStun, trapColor: it.trapColor, trapRadius: it.trapRadius + bonusRadius,
        tauntDuration: it.tauntDuration || 0, tauntTimer: it.tauntDuration || 0, tauntedMonster: null };
      if (trapObj.tauntDuration) {
        let nearest = null, nearestDist = Infinity;
        for (const m of this.activeMonsters) {
          if (!m.alive || m.state === 'taunted') continue;
          const dx = m.px - trapObj.x;
          const dy = m.py - trapObj.y;
          const dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
          if (dist < nearestDist) { nearestDist = dist; nearest = m; }
        }
        if (nearest) {
          nearest.preTauntState = nearest.state;
          nearest.state = 'taunted';
          nearest.tauntTarget = { x: trapObj.x, y: trapObj.y };
          nearest.tauntTimer = trapObj.tauntDuration;
          nearest.path = null;
          trapObj.tauntedMonster = nearest;
          this.showMessage(player.name + ' placed ' + it.name + ' - ' + nearest.name + ' taunted!');
        } else {
          this.showMessage(player.name + ' placed ' + it.name + ' (no monster to taunt)');
        }
      } else {
        this.showMessage(player.name + ' placed ' + it.name);
      }
      this.traps.push(trapObj);
      player.removeEquipped();
    } else if (it.type === 'consumable') {
      this.applyItemEffect(player, item);
      player.removeEquipped();
      this.showMessage(player.name + ' used ' + it.name);
    } else if (it.type === 'passive') {
      if (item === 'lucky_charm') {
        if (player.luckyCharmActive) { this.showMessage(player.name + ' already has a Lucky Charm active!'); return; }
        player.luckyCharmActive = true;
        player.removeEquipped();
        this.showMessage(player.name + ' activated Lucky Charm!');
      } else {
        this.showMessage(player.name + ' cannot use ' + it.name + ' directly.');
      }
    }
  }

  applyItemEffect(player, itemKey) {
    const it = Items[itemKey];
    if (!it || !it.effect) return;
    const effect = it.effect;
    switch (effect.type) {
      case 'speed':
        player.speedItemTimer = effect.duration;
        player.speedItemMult = effect.multiplier;
        break;
      case 'invuln':
        player.invulnTimer = Math.max(player.invulnTimer, effect.duration);
        break;
      case 'heal':
        player.hp = Math.min(player.maxHp, player.hp + effect.amount);
        break;
      case 'shield':
        player.shieldCharges = Math.min(2, player.shieldCharges + effect.amount);
        break;
      case 'smoke':
        this.smokeClouds.push({ x: player.px, y: player.py, timer: effect.duration, radius: effect.radius * TILE_SIZE / 2 });
        for (var sci = 0; sci < 8; sci++) {
          this.particles.push(new Particle(player.px, player.py, (Math.random()-0.5)*60, (Math.random()-0.5)*60, 0.6, '#888'));
        }
        break;
      case 'phase':
        var nearestWall = null;
        var nearestWallDist = Infinity;
        for (var wy = 0; wy < this.mapHeight; wy++) {
          for (var wx = 0; wx < this.mapWidth; wx++) {
            if (this.tiles[wy][wx] === 'O') {
              var wcx = wx * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE/2;
              var wcy = wy * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE/2;
              var wdx = wcx - player.px;
              var wdy = wcy - player.py;
              var wdist = Math.sqrt(wdx*wdx + wdy*wdy);
              if (wdist < nearestWallDist) { nearestWallDist = wdist; nearestWall = { tileX: wx, tileY: wy }; }
            }
          }
        }
        if (nearestWall) {
          this.phasedWalls.push({ tileX: nearestWall.tileX, tileY: nearestWall.tileY, timer: effect.duration });
          this.showMessage(player.name + ' phased a wall!');
        }
        break;
      case 'genes_hand':
        if (this.players.filter(function(p){return p.alive;}).length < 2 || this.playerCount < 2) {
          this.showMessage("Gene's Hand requires 2+ players!");
          player.addToInventory(itemKey);
          return;
        }
        var nearestToMonster = null;
        var nearestDist = Infinity;
        var secondNearest = null;
        var secondDist = Infinity;
        for (var mi = 0; mi < this.activeMonsters.length; mi++) {
          var mm = this.activeMonsters[mi];
          if (!mm.alive) continue;
          for (var pi = 0; pi < this.players.length; pi++) {
            var tp = this.players[pi];
            if (!tp.alive || tp === player) continue;
            var ddx = tp.px - mm.px;
            var ddy = tp.py - mm.py;
            var ddist = Math.sqrt(ddx*ddx + ddy*ddy);
            if (ddist < nearestDist) {
              secondNearest = nearestToMonster;
              secondDist = nearestDist;
              nearestToMonster = tp;
              nearestDist = ddist;
            } else if (ddist < secondDist) {
              secondNearest = tp;
              secondDist = ddist;
            }
          }
          break;
        }
        if (!nearestToMonster) { this.showMessage('No valid target!'); player.addToInventory(itemKey); return; }
        var teleportTarget = nearestToMonster;
        if (teleportTarget === player && secondNearest) teleportTarget = secondNearest;
        if (teleportTarget === player) { this.showMessage('No other player to teleport!'); player.addToInventory(itemKey); return; }
        var tpx = teleportTarget.px, tpy = teleportTarget.py;
        for (var si2 = 0; si2 < 10; si2++) this.particles.push(new Particle(tpx, tpy, (Math.random()-0.5)*100, (Math.random()-0.5)*100, 0.5, '#cc88ff'));
        teleportTarget.px = player.px;
        teleportTarget.py = player.py;
        teleportTarget.tileX = player.tileX;
        teleportTarget.tileY = player.tileY;
        for (var si3 = 0; si3 < 10; si3++) this.particles.push(new Particle(player.px, player.py, (Math.random()-0.5)*100, (Math.random()-0.5)*100, 0.5, '#cc88ff'));
        this.showMessage(teleportTarget.name + " was pulled by Gene's Hand!");
        break;
      case 'healing_aura':
        if (this.players.filter(function(p){return p.alive;}).length < 2 || this.playerCount < 2) {
          this.showMessage('Healing Aura requires 2+ players!');
          player.addToInventory(itemKey);
          return;
        }
        this.activeAuras.push({ player: player, timer: effect.duration, radius: 1.5 * TILE_SIZE, healRate: effect.healPerSec });
        player.healingAuraSlow = true;
        player.speed = player.baseSpeed * (1 - effect.slowAmount);
        this.showMessage(player.name + ' activated Healing Aura!');
        break;
    }
  }

  buyFromShop(player, shopData) {
    if (!shopData || shopData.sold) return;
    if (!player.hasSpace()) { this.showMessage(player.name + "'s inventory is full."); return; }
    if (!this.debugMode) {
      if (this.currency < shopData.price) { this.showMessage('Not enough money (' + this.currency + ' coins).'); return; }
      this.currency -= shopData.price;
    }
    if (player.playerClass === 'luckyspender' && player.countEmptySlots() >= 2 && Math.random() < 0.85) {
      player.addToInventory(shopData.itemKey);
      player.addToInventory(shopData.itemKey);
      const slotIdx = player.inventory.indexOf(shopData.itemKey);
      if (slotIdx >= 0) player.duplicatedSlots.add(slotIdx);
      const slotIdx2 = player.inventory.lastIndexOf(shopData.itemKey);
      if (slotIdx2 >= 0 && slotIdx2 !== slotIdx) player.duplicatedSlots.add(slotIdx2);
      if (!this.debugMode) shopData.sold = true;
      this.showMessage(player.name + (this.debugMode ? ' got 2x ' : ' bought 2x ') + Items[shopData.itemKey].name + (this.debugMode ? ' (FREE!)' : ' (lucky!)'));
    } else if (player.luckyCharmActive) {
      player.addToInventory(shopData.itemKey);
      var lcName = Items[shopData.itemKey].name;
      if (player.hasSpace()) {
        player.addToInventory(shopData.itemKey);
        this.showMessage(player.name + ' bought 2x ' + lcName + ' (Lucky Charm!)');
      } else {
        this.showMessage(player.name + ' bought ' + lcName + ' (Lucky Charm, no space for 2nd!)');
      }
      player.luckyCharmActive = false;
      if (!this.debugMode) shopData.sold = true;
    } else {
      player.addToInventory(shopData.itemKey);
      if (!this.debugMode) shopData.sold = true;
      this.showMessage(player.name + (this.debugMode ? ' got ' : ' bought ') + Items[shopData.itemKey].name + (this.debugMode ? ' (FREE!)' : ''));
    }
  }

  sellItem(player) {
    const item = player.getEquippedItem();
    if (!item) { this.showMessage(player.name + ' has no item to sell.'); return; }
    if (player.playerClass === 'luckyspender' && player.duplicatedSlots.has(player.selectedSlot)) {
      this.showMessage('Item duplicado nao pode ser vendido!');
      return;
    }
    const price = Items[item].price;
    this.currency += price;
    player.removeEquipped();
    this.showMessage(player.name + ' sold ' + Items[item].name + ' for ' + price + ' coins.');
  }

  initShops() {
    this.shopItems = [];
    var keys = Object.keys(Items);
    if (this.playerCount < 2) {
      keys = keys.filter(function(k) { return k !== 'genes_hand' && k !== 'healing_aura'; });
    }
    for (let y = 0; y < this.mapHeight; y++) {
      for (let x = 0; x < this.mapWidth; x++) {
        if (this.tiles[y][x] !== 'S') continue;
        var rarity = weightedRandomRarity();
        var eligible = keys.filter(function(k) { return Items[k].rarity === rarity; });
        if (eligible.length === 0) eligible = keys;
        var key = eligible[Math.floor(Math.random() * eligible.length)];
        this.shopItems.push({
          x, y, itemKey: key, price: Items[key].price, sold: false,
          worldX: x * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2,
          worldY: y * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2
        });
      }
    }
  }

  restockShops() {
    for (const shop of this.shopItems) {
      shop.sold = false;
      const keys = Object.keys(Items);
      const key = keys[Math.floor(Math.random() * keys.length)];
      shop.itemKey = key;
      shop.price = Items[key].price;
    }
  }

  updateTraps(dt) {
    // Handle noise maker (taunt) traps: timer + monster reach detection
    for (let i = this.traps.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const t = this.traps[i];
      if (!t.active || !t.tauntDuration) continue;
      t.tauntTimer -= dt;
      let removeTrap = false;
      if (t.tauntTimer <= 0) {
        removeTrap = true;
      } else if (t.tauntedMonster && t.tauntedMonster.alive) {
        const dx = t.tauntedMonster.px - t.x;
        const dy = t.tauntedMonster.py - t.y;
        if (Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy) < TILE_SIZE * 0.4) {
          removeTrap = true;
        }
      } else if (t.tauntedMonster && !t.tauntedMonster.alive) {
        removeTrap = true;
      }
      if (removeTrap) {
        if (t.tauntedMonster && t.tauntedMonster.alive && t.tauntedMonster.state === 'taunted') {
          t.tauntedMonster.state = t.tauntedMonster.preTauntState || 'patrol';
          t.tauntedMonster.tauntTimer = 0;
          t.tauntedMonster.tauntTarget = null;
          t.tauntedMonster.path = null;
        }
        this.traps.splice(i, 1);
      }
    }
    // Handle lifetime-based traps (banana peel, etc.)
    for (let i = this.traps.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const t = this.traps[i];
      if (!t.active || !t.lifetime) continue;
      t.timer -= dt;
      if (t.timer <= 0) { this.traps.splice(i, 1); }
    }
    // Handle stun traps: monster collision
    for (const m of this.activeMonsters) {
      if (!m.alive || m.stunTimer > 0) continue;
      for (let i = this.traps.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        const t = this.traps[i];
        if (!t.active || t.tauntDuration || t.lifetime) continue;
        const dx = m.px - t.x;
        const dy = m.py - t.y;
        if (Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy) < TILE_SIZE * 0.5) {
          if (m.type === 'hoarder' && m.shieldActive) {
            m.shieldActive = false;
            t.active = false;
            this.traps.splice(i, 1);
            this.showMessage('Hoarder shield absorbed the trap!');
            break;
          }
          m.stunTimer = m.type === 'monstrosity' ? t.stun * 0.65 : t.stun;
          if (m.type === 'berserker') m.berserkerBonus += 15;
          t.active = false;
          this.traps.splice(i, 1);
          if (t.item === 'banana_peel') {
            this.showMessage('Banana Peel triggered! Monster stunned ' + (m.type === 'monstrosity' ? (t.stun * 0.65).toFixed(1) : t.stun) + 's');
          } else {
            this.showMessage(Items[t.item].name + ' triggered! Monster stunned ' + (m.type === 'monstrosity' ? (t.stun * 0.65).toFixed(1) : t.stun) + 's');
          }
          break;
        }
      }
    }
    // Handle banana peel collision with monsters (separate from stun traps)
    for (const m of this.activeMonsters) {
      if (!m.alive || m.stunTimer > 0) continue;
      for (let i = this.traps.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        const t = this.traps[i];
        if (!t.active || !t.lifetime) continue;
        const dx = m.px - t.x;
        const dy = m.py - t.y;
        if (Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy) < TILE_SIZE * 0.5) {
          m.stunTimer = m.type === 'monstrosity' ? t.stun * 0.65 : t.stun;
          this.traps.splice(i, 1);
          this.showMessage('Banana Peel triggered! Monster stunned ' + (m.type === 'monstrosity' ? (t.stun * 0.65).toFixed(1) : t.stun) + 's');
          break;
        }
      }
    }
  }

  updateRatEntities(dt) {
    for (let ri = this.ratEntities.length - 1; ri >= 0; ri--) {
      const rato = this.ratEntities[ri];
      if (!rato.alive) continue;
      if (!rato.kingRef.alive) { rato.alive = false; continue; }
      if (rato.targetTrap && !this.traps.includes(rato.targetTrap)) {
        rato.targetTrap = null;
        rato.devourTimer = 0;
        rato.path = null;
      }
      if (this.traps.length === 0 && !rato.targetTrap) {
        const dx = rato.kingRef.px - rato.px;
        const dy = rato.kingRef.py - rato.py;
        const dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
        if (dist < TILE_SIZE * 0.5) {
          this.ratEntities.splice(ri, 1);
          continue;
        }
        if (!rato.path || rato.path.length === 0) {
          const stx = Math.floor(rato.px / TILE_SIZE);
          const sty = Math.floor(rato.py / TILE_SIZE);
          const gtx = Math.floor(rato.kingRef.px / TILE_SIZE);
          const gty = Math.floor(rato.kingRef.py / TILE_SIZE);
          rato.path = this.findPath(stx, sty, gtx, gty);
        }
        if (rato.path && rato.path.length > 0) {
          const next = rato.path[0];
          const tx = next.x * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2;
          const ty = next.y * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2;
          const dx2 = tx - rato.px; const dy2 = ty - rato.py;
          const ddist = Math.sqrt(dx2 * dx2 + dy2 * dy2);
          const ratSpeed = 50;
          if (ddist < 4) { rato.path.shift(); }
          else if (ddist > 0.001) {
            rato.px += (dx2 / ddist) * ratSpeed * dt;
            rato.py += (dy2 / ddist) * ratSpeed * dt;
          }
        }
        rato.tileX = Math.floor(rato.px / TILE_SIZE);
        rato.tileY = Math.floor(rato.py / TILE_SIZE);
        continue;
      }
      if (rato.devourTimer > 0) {
        rato.devourTimer -= dt;
        if (rato.devourTimer <= 0) {
          const idx = this.traps.indexOf(rato.targetTrap);
          if (idx >= 0) this.traps.splice(idx, 1);
          rato.targetTrap = null;
          rato.devourTimer = 0;
          rato.path = null;
          for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
            this.particles.push(new Particle(rato.px, rato.py,
              (Math.random() - 0.5) * 50, (Math.random() - 0.5) * 50, 0.4, '#886644'));
          }
          this.showMessage('A rat devoured a trap!');
        }
        continue;
      }
      if (!rato.targetTrap) {
        if (this.traps.length > 0) {
          let nearest = null; let nearestDist = Infinity;
          for (const t of this.traps) {
            const otherRat = this.ratEntities.find(r =>
              r !== rato && r.alive && r.targetTrap === t);
            if (otherRat) continue;
            const dx = t.x - rato.px; const dy = t.y - rato.py;
            const dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
            if (dist < nearestDist) { nearestDist = dist; nearest = t; }
          }
          rato.targetTrap = nearest;
          rato.path = null;
        }
      }
      if (rato.targetTrap) {
        const dx = rato.targetTrap.x - rato.px;
        const dy = rato.targetTrap.y - rato.py;
        const dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
        if (dist < TILE_SIZE * 0.4) {
          rato.devourTimer = 35 * 0.35;
          rato.path = null;
        } else {
          if (!rato.path) {
            const stx = Math.floor(rato.px / TILE_SIZE);
            const sty = Math.floor(rato.py / TILE_SIZE);
            const gtx = Math.floor(rato.targetTrap.x / TILE_SIZE);
            const gty = Math.floor(rato.targetTrap.y / TILE_SIZE);
            rato.path = this.findPath(stx, sty, gtx, gty);
          }
          if (rato.path && rato.path.length > 0) {
            const next = rato.path[0];
            const tx = next.x * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2;
            const ty = next.y * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2;
            const dx2 = tx - rato.px; const dy2 = ty - rato.py;
            const ddist = Math.sqrt(dx2 * dx2 + dy2 * dy2);
            const ratSpeed = 50;
            if (ddist < 4) { rato.path.shift(); }
            else if (ddist > 0.001) {
              rato.px += (dx2 / ddist) * ratSpeed * dt;
              rato.py += (dy2 / ddist) * ratSpeed * dt;
            }
          }
        }
        rato.tileX = Math.floor(rato.px / TILE_SIZE);
        rato.tileY = Math.floor(rato.py / TILE_SIZE);
      }
    }
  }

  renderTraps(ctx) {
    for (const t of this.traps) {
      if (!t.active) continue;
      if (!this.isPointVisible(t.x, t.y)) continue;
      const sx = t.x - this.camX;
      const sy = t.y - this.camY;
      if (sx < -50 || sx > this.canvas.width + 50 || sy < -50 || sy > this.canvas.height + 50) continue;
      // Draw circular area for all traps
      ctx.fillStyle = t.trapColor;
      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.35;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(sx, sy, t.trapRadius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.4)';
      ctx.lineWidth = 0.5;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(sx, sy, t.trapRadius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.stroke();
      // Crosshair marker
      ctx.strokeStyle = '#fff';
      ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(sx - 3, sy);
      ctx.lineTo(sx + 3, sy);
      ctx.moveTo(sx, sy - 3);
      ctx.lineTo(sx, sy + 3);
      ctx.stroke();
      // Icon in center
      if (t.item === 'banana_peel') {
        drawBananaPeel(ctx, sx, sy, t.rotation, Math.min(t.trapRadius * 1.5, 28));
      } else {
        const iconFn = ItemIcons[t.item];
        if (iconFn) iconFn(ctx, sx, sy, Math.min(t.trapRadius * 1.5, 28));
      }
    }
  }

  renderShops(ctx) {
    if (this.activeMonsters.some(m => m.alive) && !this.debugMode) return;
    let nearestDist = Infinity;
    let nearestShop = null;
    for (const shop of this.shopItems) {
      if (shop.sold) continue;
      for (const p of this.players) {
        if (!p.alive) continue;
        const pdx = p.px - shop.worldX;
        const pdy = p.py - shop.worldY;
        const dist = Math.sqrt(pdx * pdx + pdy * pdy);
        if (dist < TILE_SIZE * 1.5 && dist < nearestDist) { nearestDist = dist; nearestShop = shop; }
      }
    }
    for (const shop of this.shopItems) {
      if (shop.sold) continue;
      const sx = shop.worldX - this.camX;
      const sy = shop.worldY - this.camY;
      if (sx < -50 || sx > this.canvas.width + 50 || sy < -50 || sy > this.canvas.height + 50) continue;
      const it = Items[shop.itemKey];
      const rarityName = Rarities[it.rarity] ? Rarities[it.rarity].name : '';
      // Pedestal
      ctx.fillStyle = '#3a3030';
      ctx.fillRect(sx - 10, sy - 10, 20, 20);
      ctx.strokeStyle = '#888';
      ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      ctx.strokeRect(sx - 10, sy - 10, 20, 20);
      // Item icon
      const iconFn = ItemIcons[it.icon];
      if (iconFn) {
        iconFn(ctx, sx, sy, 22);
      } else {
        ctx.fillStyle = it.color;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(sx, sy, 8, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
      }
      // Price
      ctx.fillStyle = '#ff0';
      ctx.font = '9px "Courier New", monospace';
      ctx.fillText(it.price + 'g', sx - 8, sy - 14);
      if (shop !== nearestShop) continue;
      // Name + price + rarity tile to the right (only nearest shop)
      const signSx = sx + 20;
      const signSy = sy - TILE_SIZE * 0.6;
      const signW = TILE_SIZE * 2.4;
      const signH = TILE_SIZE * 1.2;
      if (signSx > -50 && signSx < this.canvas.width + 50 && signSy > -50 && signSy < this.canvas.height + 50) {
        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.75)';
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(signSx + 3, signSy);
        ctx.lineTo(signSx + signW - 3, signSy);
        ctx.quadraticCurveTo(signSx + signW, signSy, signSx + signW, signSy + 3);
        ctx.lineTo(signSx + signW, signSy + signH - 3);
        ctx.quadraticCurveTo(signSx + signW, signSy + signH, signSx + signW - 3, signSy + signH);
        ctx.lineTo(signSx + 3, signSy + signH);
        ctx.quadraticCurveTo(signSx, signSy + signH, signSx, signSy + signH - 3);
        ctx.lineTo(signSx, signSy + 3);
        ctx.quadraticCurveTo(signSx, signSy, signSx + 3, signSy);
        ctx.closePath();
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.3)';
        ctx.lineWidth = 1;
        ctx.stroke();
        ctx.fillStyle = it.color;
        ctx.font = 'bold 9px "Courier New", monospace';
        ctx.textAlign = 'center';
        ctx.fillText(it.name, signSx + signW / 2, signSy + signH * 0.38);
        ctx.fillStyle = '#ff0';
        ctx.font = '8px "Courier New", monospace';
        ctx.fillText(it.price + 'g [' + rarityName + ']', signSx + signW / 2, signSy + signH * 0.72);
        ctx.textAlign = 'start';
      }
    }
  }

  renderTrashBins(ctx) {
    if (this.activeMonsters.some(m => m.alive) && !this.debugMode) return;
    for (let y = 0; y < this.mapHeight; y++) {
      for (let x = 0; x < this.mapWidth; x++) {
        if (this.tiles[y][x] !== 'T') continue;
        const sx = x * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2 - this.camX;
        const sy = y * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2 - this.camY;
        if (sx < -50 || sx > this.canvas.width + 50 || sy < -50 || sy > this.canvas.height + 50) continue;
        ctx.fillStyle = '#555';
        ctx.fillRect(sx - 8, sy - 10, 16, 20);
        ctx.strokeStyle = '#888';
        ctx.lineWidth = 1;
        ctx.strokeRect(sx - 8, sy - 10, 16, 20);
        ctx.fillStyle = '#aaa';
        ctx.font = '9px "Courier New", monospace';
        ctx.textAlign = 'center';
        ctx.fillText('$', sx, sy + 3);
        ctx.textAlign = 'start';
      }
    }
  }

  showMessage(text, duration) {
    this.messageText = text;
    this.messageTimer = text ? (duration || 2.5) : 0;
    const el = document.getElementById('message');
    el.textContent = text;
    if (text) el.classList.add('show');
    else el.classList.remove('show');
  }

  showSummonDesc(text) {
    const el = document.getElementById('summon-desc');
    el.textContent = text;
    if (text) el.classList.add('show');
    else el.classList.remove('show');
  }

  hideSummonDesc() {
    const el = document.getElementById('summon-desc');
    el.classList.remove('show');
  }

  openMapEditor() {
    const panel = document.getElementById('map-editor-panel');
    panel.classList.add('open');
    const ta = document.getElementById('map-editor-textarea');
    ta.value = this.tiles.map(row => row.join('')).join('\n');
    updatePreview();
    document.getElementById('settings-panel').classList.remove('open');
  }

  closeMapEditor() {
    document.getElementById('map-editor-panel').classList.remove('open');
  }

  applyMapFromEditor() {
    const ta = document.getElementById('map-editor-textarea');
    const mapData = parseMap(ta.value);
    if (mapData) {
      this.loadMap(mapData);
      this.closeMapEditor();
      this.showMessage('Map applied!');
    } else {
      this.showMessage('Invalid map!');
    }
  }

  resetDefaultMap() {
    const ta = document.getElementById('map-editor-textarea');
    ta.value = DEFAULT_MAP.join('\n');
    updatePreview();
  }
