  // ============================================================
  // RENDERING
  // ============================================================
  render() {
    const ctx = this.ctx;
    const W = this.canvas.width, H = this.canvas.height;
    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);
    // Apply camera scale
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(W / 2, H / 2);
    ctx.scale(this.camScale, this.camScale);
    ctx.translate(-W / 2, -H / 2);
    // Background
    ctx.fillStyle = '#111';
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
    // Draw tiles
    this.renderTiles(ctx);
    // Draw artifacts on floor
    this.renderArtifacts(ctx);
    // Draw roof overlay
    this.renderRoof(ctx);
    // Raycast lightmap (replaces old fog)
    const lightmap = this.buildLightmap();
    ctx.drawImage(lightmap, 0, 0);
    this.renderArtifactLabels(ctx);
    // Doors on top of lightmap (always lit when near player)
    this.renderDoors(ctx);
    // Traps on top of doors (with visibility check)
    this.renderTraps(ctx);
    // Zones (Mighty Tinkerer)
    for (var zi2 = 0; zi2 < this.activeZones.length; zi2++) {
      var zone = this.activeZones[zi2];
      var zsx = zone.x - this.camX, zsy = zone.y - this.camY;
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(100,150,255,0.3)';
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(zsx, zsy, zone.radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(100,150,255,0.5)';
      ctx.lineWidth = 2;
      ctx.stroke();
    }
    // Shops and trash bins on top of lightmap
    this.renderShops(ctx);
    this.renderTrashBins(ctx);
    // Draw entities on top
    for (const c of (this.activeClones || [])) {
      if (!c.alive) continue;
      const sx = c.px - this.camX, sy = c.py - this.camY;
      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.3;
      ctx.fillStyle = c.playerColor;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(sx, sy, TILE_SIZE * 0.35, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.strokeStyle = '#fff';
      ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      ctx.setLineDash([3, 3]);
      ctx.stroke();
      ctx.setLineDash([]);
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
    }
    for (const p of this.players) p.render(ctx, this);
    for (const m of this.activeMonsters) m.render(ctx, this);
    // Render chests
    this.renderChests(ctx);
    // Render contaminated tiles (Acrid)
    this.renderContaminatedTiles(ctx);
    // Render smoke clouds
    for (const sc of this.smokeClouds) {
      var scx = sc.x - this.camX, scy = sc.y - this.camY;
      var grad = ctx.createRadialGradient(scx, scy, 0, scx, scy, sc.radius);
      grad.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(128,128,128,0.4)');
      grad.addColorStop(0.7, 'rgba(128,128,128,0.3)');
      grad.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(128,128,128,0)');
      ctx.fillStyle = grad;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(scx, scy, sc.radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
    }
    // Render phased walls
    for (const pw of this.phasedWalls) {
      var pwx = pw.tileX * TILE_SIZE - this.camX;
      var pwy = pw.tileY * TILE_SIZE - this.camY;
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(100,180,255,0.4)';
      ctx.fillRect(pwx, pwy, TILE_SIZE, TILE_SIZE);
      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(150,200,255,0.7)';
      ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      ctx.strokeRect(pwx, pwy, TILE_SIZE, TILE_SIZE);
    }
    // Render active bombs
    for (const b of this.activeBombs) {
      var bx = (b.tileX + 0.5) * TILE_SIZE - this.camX;
      var by = (b.tileY + 0.5) * TILE_SIZE - this.camY;
      var bgrad = ctx.createRadialGradient(bx, by, 0, bx, by, TILE_SIZE * 0.4);
      bgrad.addColorStop(0, '#111');
      bgrad.addColorStop(0.7, '#333');
      bgrad.addColorStop(1, '#555');
      ctx.fillStyle = bgrad;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(bx, by, TILE_SIZE * 0.35, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.strokeStyle = '#888';
      ctx.lineWidth = 0.5;
      ctx.stroke();
      var sparkAlpha = 0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(Date.now() * 0.015);
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,150,0,' + sparkAlpha + ')';
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(bx, by - TILE_SIZE * 0.25, 2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,200,0,0.6)';
      ctx.lineWidth = 0.5;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(bx, by - TILE_SIZE * 0.28);
      ctx.lineTo(bx, by - TILE_SIZE * 0.15);
      ctx.stroke();
    }
    // Render healing aura effects
    for (const au of this.activeAuras) {
      var aux = au.player.px - this.camX, auy = au.player.py - this.camY;
      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(68,255,68,0.3)';
      ctx.lineWidth = 2;
      ctx.setLineDash([4, 4]);
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(aux, auy, au.radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.stroke();
      ctx.setLineDash([]);
      for (var pli = 0; pli < 5; pli++) {
        var ang = (pli / 5) * Math.PI * 2;
        var px = aux + Math.cos(ang) * au.radius * 0.8;
        var py = auy + Math.sin(ang) * au.radius * 0.8;
        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(68,255,68,0.6)';
        ctx.fillText('+', px - 3, py + 4);
      }
    }
    for (const rato of (this.ratEntities || [])) {
      if (!rato.alive) continue;
      const rsx = rato.px - this.camX;
      const rsy = rato.py - this.camY;
      if (rsx < -50 || rsx > this.canvas.width + 50 || rsy < -50 || rsy > this.canvas.height + 50) continue;
      ctx.fillStyle = '#664422';
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(rsx, rsy, 4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.strokeStyle = '#442200';
      ctx.lineWidth = 0.5;
      ctx.stroke();
      if (rato.path && rato.path.length > 0 && rato.path[0]) {
        const nx = rato.path[0].x * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2;
        const ny = rato.path[0].y * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2;
        const ndx = nx - rato.px; const ndy = ny - rato.py;
        const ndist = Math.sqrt(ndx * ndx + ndy * ndy);
        if (ndist > 0.001) {
          ctx.strokeStyle = '#442200';
          ctx.lineWidth = 0.5;
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.moveTo(rsx + 2, rsy);
          ctx.quadraticCurveTo(rsx - 3, rsy + 2, rsx - 5, rsy + 4);
          ctx.stroke();
        }
      }
      if (rato.devourTimer > 0) {
        const progress = 1 - rato.devourTimer / 35;
        ctx.strokeStyle = '#ff8800';
        ctx.lineWidth = 1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(rsx, rsy, 7, -Math.PI / 2, -Math.PI / 2 + Math.PI * 2 * progress);
        ctx.stroke();
      }
    }
    // Locked door padlock
    if (this.lockedDoor) {
      for (var lti = 0; lti < this.lockedDoor.tiles.length; lti++) {
        var lt = this.lockedDoor.tiles[lti];
        var lx = lt.tileX * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2 - this.camX;
        var ly = lt.tileY * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2 - this.camY;
        drawLock(ctx, lx, ly, TILE_SIZE * 0.6);
      }
    }
    // Priest menus
    for (var pr = 0; pr < this.players.length; pr++) {
      var prp = this.players[pr];
      if (prp.playerClass === 'helpfulpriest' && prp.menuOpen && prp.alive) {
        drawPriestMenu(ctx, prp, this);
      }
    }
    this.renderParticles(ctx);
    // Interaction progress circle (canvas)
    this.renderInteractionCircle(ctx);
    this.renderUseProgressCircle(ctx);
    // Night overlay
    this.renderNight(ctx);
    // Draw walls (Annoying Looper)
    for (const w of (this.activeWalls || [])) {
      for (const t of w.tiles) {
        const wx = t.x * TILE_SIZE - this.camX;
        const wy = t.y * TILE_SIZE - this.camY;
        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(100,200,255,0.3)';
        ctx.fillRect(wx, wy, TILE_SIZE, TILE_SIZE);
        ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(100,200,255,0.7)';
        ctx.lineWidth = 1;
        ctx.setLineDash([4, 4]);
        ctx.strokeRect(wx, wy, TILE_SIZE, TILE_SIZE);
        ctx.setLineDash([]);
      }
    }
    // Debug
    if (this.debugMode) this.renderDebug(ctx);
    ctx.restore();
    // Message (DOM)
    this.renderMessage(ctx);
    // Interaction bar (DOM)
    this.renderInteractionBar();
  }

  renderTiles(ctx) {
    for (let y = 0; y < this.mapHeight; y++) {
      for (let x = 0; x < this.mapWidth; x++) {
        const tile = this.tiles[y][x];
        const sx = x * TILE_SIZE - this.camX;
        const sy = y * TILE_SIZE - this.camY;
        if (sx + TILE_SIZE < -50 || sx > this.canvas.width + 50 || sy + TILE_SIZE < -50 || sy > this.canvas.height + 50) continue;
        const v = (this.tileVariation[y] && this.tileVariation[y][x]) || 0.5;
        const v2 = (this.tileVariation2[y] && this.tileVariation2[y][x]) || 0.5;
        const v3 = (this.tileVariation3[y] && this.tileVariation3[y][x]) || 0.5;

        let color = '#111';
        switch (tile) {
          case 'O': color = WALL_COLOR; break;
          case 'C': color = INTERIOR_COLOR; break;
          case 'G': color = GRASS_COLOR; break;
          case 'd': {
            const door = this.doorEntities.find(d => d.tileX === x && d.tileY === y);
            if (door && door.hasGroup()) color = DOOR_CLOSED_COLOR;
            else color = (door && door.open) ? DOOR_OPEN_COLOR : DOOR_CLOSED_COLOR;
            break;
          }
          case 'A': color = ALTAR_COLOR; break;
          case 'P': case 'M': color = INTERIOR_COLOR; break;
          case 'F': case 'U': case 'm': {
            const resolved = this.baseTileResolved[y] ? this.baseTileResolved[y][x] : 'C';
            color = (resolved === 'G') ? GRASS_COLOR : INTERIOR_COLOR;
            break;
          }
          case 'W': color = FURNITURE_COLOR; break;
          case 'S': case 'T': color = INTERIOR_COLOR; break;
        }

        const effTile = (tile === 'F' || tile === 'U' || tile === 'm')
          ? (this.baseTileResolved[y] ? this.baseTileResolved[y][x] : 'C')
          : tile;
        const mask = (this.tileEdgeMask[y] && this.tileEdgeMask[y][x]) || 0;

        // ============================================================
        // TILE-SPECIFIC PROCEDURAL TEXTURES
        // ============================================================

        if (tile === 'O') {
          const wallR = 92 + (v - 0.5) * 20 | 0;
          const wallG = 58 + (v - 0.5) * 14 | 0;
          const wallB = 30 + (v - 0.5) * 10 | 0;
          ctx.fillStyle = 'rgb(' + wallR + ',' + wallG + ',' + wallB + ')';
          ctx.fillRect(sx, sy, TILE_SIZE, TILE_SIZE);
          for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
            const my = sy + 6 + i * 10 + (v2 * 4) | 0;
            if (my < sy + TILE_SIZE) {
              ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,' + (0.06 + i * 0.02) + ')';
              ctx.fillRect(sx, my, TILE_SIZE, 1);
            }
          }
          const mortarY = sy + TILE_SIZE - 2;
          ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.12)';
          ctx.fillRect(sx, mortarY, TILE_SIZE, 2);
          ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,' + (0.04 + v3 * 0.06) + ')';
          ctx.fillRect(sx, sy, 2, TILE_SIZE);
          ctx.fillRect(sx + TILE_SIZE - 2, sy, 2, TILE_SIZE);
          const spotCount = 1 + ((v2 * 10) | 0) % 2;
          for (let s = 0; s < spotCount; s++) {
            const spx = sx + 4 + ((Game.tileHash(x * 13 + s, y * 7) * (TILE_SIZE - 8)) | 0);
            const spy = sy + 4 + ((Game.tileHash(x * 17 + s, y * 11) * (TILE_SIZE - 8)) | 0);
            ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,' + (0.04 + v3 * 0.08) + ')';
            ctx.fillRect(spx, spy, 2, 2);
          }
        }

        else if (tile === 'd') {
          const door = this.doorEntities.find(d => d.tileX === x && d.tileY === y);
          if (!(door && door.hasGroup())) {
            const open = door && door.open;
            const anim = door && door.animating;
            if (open && !anim) {
              ctx.fillStyle = INTERIOR_COLOR;
              ctx.fillRect(sx, sy, TILE_SIZE, TILE_SIZE);
              const frameR = 139 + (v - 0.5) * 12 | 0;
              const frameG = 105 + (v - 0.5) * 10 | 0;
              const frameB = 20 + (v - 0.5) * 6 | 0;
              ctx.fillStyle = 'rgb(' + frameR + ',' + frameG + ',' + frameB + ')';
              ctx.fillRect(sx, sy, TILE_SIZE, 3);
              ctx.fillRect(sx, sy + TILE_SIZE - 3, TILE_SIZE, 3);
              ctx.fillRect(sx, sy, 3, TILE_SIZE);
              ctx.fillRect(sx + TILE_SIZE - 3, sy, 3, TILE_SIZE);
            } else if (anim) {
              const progress = door.animProgress;
              ctx.fillStyle = INTERIOR_COLOR;
              ctx.fillRect(sx, sy, TILE_SIZE, TILE_SIZE);
              const frameR = 139 + (v - 0.5) * 12 | 0;
              const frameG = 105 + (v - 0.5) * 10 | 0;
              const frameB = 20 + (v - 0.5) * 6 | 0;
              ctx.fillStyle = 'rgb(' + frameR + ',' + frameG + ',' + frameB + ')';
              ctx.fillRect(sx, sy, TILE_SIZE, 3);
              ctx.fillRect(sx, sy + TILE_SIZE - 3, TILE_SIZE, 3);
              ctx.fillRect(sx, sy, 3, TILE_SIZE);
              ctx.fillRect(sx + TILE_SIZE - 3, sy, 3, TILE_SIZE);
              ctx.fillStyle = '#8b6914';
              ctx.fillRect(sx + TILE_SIZE * 0.1, sy + TILE_SIZE * 0.1, TILE_SIZE * 0.8 * progress, TILE_SIZE * 0.8);
            } else {
              const doorR = 139 + (v - 0.5) * 16 | 0;
              const doorG = 105 + (v - 0.5) * 12 | 0;
              const doorB = 20 + (v - 0.5) * 8 | 0;
              ctx.fillStyle = 'rgb(' + doorR + ',' + doorG + ',' + doorB + ')';
              ctx.fillRect(sx, sy, TILE_SIZE, TILE_SIZE);
              for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
                const lx = sx + 6 + i * 6;
                ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.06)';
                ctx.fillRect(lx, sy + TILE_SIZE * 0.1, 2, TILE_SIZE * 0.8);
              }
              const handleX = sx + TILE_SIZE * 0.75;
              const handleY = sy + TILE_SIZE * 0.5;
              const hg = ctx.createLinearGradient(handleX - 3, handleY, handleX + 3, handleY);
              hg.addColorStop(0, '#a08040');
              hg.addColorStop(0.4, '#ffd700');
              hg.addColorStop(0.6, '#ffd700');
              hg.addColorStop(1, '#806020');
              ctx.fillStyle = hg;
              ctx.beginPath();
              ctx.arc(handleX, handleY, 3, 0, Math.PI * 2);
              ctx.fill();
            }
          } else {
            ctx.fillStyle = color;
            ctx.fillRect(sx, sy, TILE_SIZE, TILE_SIZE);
          }
        }

        else if (tile === 'A') {
          const ar = 255 - (v2 - 0.5) * 30 | 0;
          const ag = 68 + (v2 - 0.5) * 20 | 0;
          const ab = 68 + (v2 - 0.5) * 20 | 0;
          ctx.fillStyle = 'rgb(' + ar + ',' + ag + ',' + ab + ')';
          ctx.fillRect(sx + TILE_SIZE * 0.15, sy + TILE_SIZE * 0.15, TILE_SIZE * 0.7, TILE_SIZE * 0.7);
          ctx.strokeStyle = '#aa2222';
          ctx.lineWidth = 2;
          ctx.strokeRect(sx + TILE_SIZE * 0.15, sy + TILE_SIZE * 0.15, TILE_SIZE * 0.7, TILE_SIZE * 0.7);
          ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.15)';
          ctx.lineWidth = 1;
          ctx.beginPath();
          const cxA = sx + TILE_SIZE * 0.5;
          const cyA = sy + TILE_SIZE * 0.5;
          ctx.moveTo(cxA - 8, cyA);
          ctx.lineTo(cxA + 8, cyA);
          ctx.moveTo(cxA, cyA - 8);
          ctx.lineTo(cxA, cyA + 8);
          ctx.stroke();
          const glow = 0.35 + 0.15 * Math.sin(Date.now() / 1000 * 3 + x + y);
          ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,0,0,' + glow + ')';
          ctx.fillRect(sx + TILE_SIZE * 0.05, sy + TILE_SIZE * 0.05, TILE_SIZE * 0.9, TILE_SIZE * 0.9);
        }

        else if (tile === 'W') {
          const fr = 102 + (v - 0.5) * 20 | 0;
          const fg = 48 + (v - 0.5) * 14 | 0;
          const fb = 48 + (v - 0.5) * 10 | 0;
          ctx.fillStyle = 'rgb(' + fr + ',' + fg + ',' + fb + ')';
          ctx.fillRect(sx + TILE_SIZE * 0.15, sy + TILE_SIZE * 0.15, TILE_SIZE * 0.7, TILE_SIZE * 0.7);
          for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
            const vy = sy + TILE_SIZE * 0.25 + i * (TILE_SIZE * 0.2);
            ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.05)';
            ctx.fillRect(sx + TILE_SIZE * 0.2, vy, TILE_SIZE * 0.6, 1);
          }
          ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.10)';
          ctx.fillRect(sx + TILE_SIZE * 0.15, sy + TILE_SIZE * 0.85, TILE_SIZE * 0.7, 3);
          ctx.fillRect(sx + TILE_SIZE * 0.85, sy + TILE_SIZE * 0.15, 3, TILE_SIZE * 0.7);
        }

        else {
          ctx.fillStyle = color;
          ctx.fillRect(sx, sy, TILE_SIZE, TILE_SIZE);
        }

        // ============================================================
        // FLOOR AUTOTILING (C, P, M, S, T + F/U/m resolved as C)
        // ============================================================
        if (effTile === 'C' || tile === 'P' || tile === 'M' || tile === 'S' || tile === 'T') {
          const orient = (v2 > 0.5);
          ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.04)';
          if (orient) {
            const plankOff = (v * 6) | 0;
            for (let py = plankOff; py < TILE_SIZE; py += 6) {
              ctx.fillRect(sx, sy + py, TILE_SIZE, 1);
            }
          } else {
            const plankOff = (v * 6) | 0;
            for (let px = plankOff; px < TILE_SIZE; px += 6) {
              ctx.fillRect(sx + px, sy, 1, TILE_SIZE);
            }
          }
          const knotCount = 1 + ((v3 * 4) | 0) % 2;
          for (let k = 0; k < knotCount; k++) {
            const kx = sx + 8 + ((Game.tileHash(x * 19 + k, y * 23) * (TILE_SIZE - 16)) | 0);
            const ky = sy + 8 + ((Game.tileHash(x * 29 + k, y * 31) * (TILE_SIZE - 16)) | 0);
            ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.07)';
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.arc(kx, ky, 1.5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            ctx.fill();
          }
          if (mask) {
            ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.06)';
            if (mask & 1) ctx.fillRect(sx, sy, TILE_SIZE, 2);
            if (mask & 2) ctx.fillRect(sx, sy + TILE_SIZE - 2, TILE_SIZE, 2);
            if (mask & 4) ctx.fillRect(sx, sy, 2, TILE_SIZE);
            if (mask & 8) ctx.fillRect(sx + TILE_SIZE - 2, sy, 2, TILE_SIZE);
          }
        }

        // ============================================================
        // GRASS AUTOTILING (G tiles + F/U/m resolved as G)
        // ============================================================
        if (effTile === 'G') {
          const r = 58 + (v - 0.5) * 24 | 0;
          const g = 122 + (v - 0.5) * 28 | 0;
          const b = 42 + (v - 0.5) * 14 | 0;
          ctx.fillStyle = 'rgb(' + r + ',' + g + ',' + b + ')';
          ctx.fillRect(sx, sy, TILE_SIZE, TILE_SIZE);
          ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.02)';
          const detailCount = 3 + ((v * 10) | 0) % 4;
          for (let i = 0; i < detailCount; i++) {
            const dx = sx + 4 + ((Game.tileHash(x * 3 + i, y * 3) * (TILE_SIZE - 8)) | 0);
            const dy = sy + 4 + ((Game.tileHash(x * 5 + i, y * 5 + 1) * (TILE_SIZE - 8)) | 0);
            ctx.fillRect(dx, dy, 2, 2);
          }
          const clumpY = (v2 > 0.5) ? sy : sy + TILE_SIZE * 0.6;
          for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
            const cx = sx + 3 + i * 7 + (v3 * 3) | 0;
            ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.03)';
            ctx.fillRect(cx, clumpY, 1.5, TILE_SIZE * 0.35);
          }
          const flowerChance = v3;
          if (flowerChance < 0.15) {
            const fx = sx + 5 + ((Game.tileHash(x * 41, y * 37) * (TILE_SIZE - 10)) | 0);
            const fy = sy + 5 + ((Game.tileHash(x * 53, y * 43) * (TILE_SIZE - 10)) | 0);
            const flowerColors = ['#ffee88','#ffaa55','#ff99cc','#ffffff','#ffdd44'];
            const fc = flowerColors[((v2 * flowerColors.length) | 0) % flowerColors.length];
            ctx.fillStyle = fc;
            ctx.fillRect(fx, fy, 2, 2);
          }
          if (mask) {
            ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.04)';
            if (mask & 1) ctx.fillRect(sx, sy, TILE_SIZE, 2);
            if (mask & 2) ctx.fillRect(sx, sy + TILE_SIZE - 2, TILE_SIZE, 2);
            if (mask & 4) ctx.fillRect(sx, sy, 2, TILE_SIZE);
            if (mask & 8) ctx.fillRect(sx + TILE_SIZE - 2, sy, 2, TILE_SIZE);
          }
        }

        if (tile === 'F') {
          ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,255,255,0.03)';
          ctx.fillRect(sx + 6, sy + 6, TILE_SIZE - 12, TILE_SIZE - 12);
        }

        if (this.debugMode) {
          ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.1)';
          ctx.lineWidth = 0.5;
          ctx.strokeRect(sx, sy, TILE_SIZE, TILE_SIZE);
          if (tile === 'G' || tile === 'C') {
            ctx.fillStyle = '#0f0';
            ctx.font = '7px monospace';
            const m = (this.tileEdgeMask[y] && this.tileEdgeMask[y][x]) || 0;
            ctx.fillText(m.toString(16), sx + 2, sy + 10);
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }

  renderArtifacts(ctx) {
    for (const art of this.artifactEntities) {
      if (art.collected) continue;
      art.render(ctx, art.tileX, art.tileY, TILE_SIZE, this.camX, this.camY);
    }
  }

  renderArtifactLabels(ctx) {
    ctx.font = '24px "Courier New", monospace';
    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
    ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
    ctx.lineWidth = 3;
    for (const art of this.artifactEntities) {
      if (art.collected) continue;
      const cx = art.tileX * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2 - this.camX;
      const cy = art.tileY * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2 - this.camY - TILE_SIZE * 0.5;
      ctx.strokeStyle = '#000';
      ctx.strokeText(art.displayName || 'Artifact', cx, cy);
      ctx.fillStyle = '#fff';
      ctx.fillText(art.displayName || 'Artifact', cx, cy);
    }
    ctx.textAlign = 'start';
    ctx.textBaseline = 'alphabetic';
    ctx.lineWidth = 1;
  }

  renderRoof(ctx) {
    if (this.roofAlpha <= 0.01) return;
    const alpha = this.roofAlpha;
    ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(30,20,15,${alpha * 0.85})`;
    for (let y = 0; y < this.mapHeight; y++) {
      for (let x = 0; x < this.mapWidth; x++) {
        const rawTile = this.tiles[y][x];
        const effectiveTile = this.baseTileResolved[y] ? this.baseTileResolved[y][x] : rawTile;
        if (effectiveTile === 'G' || effectiveTile === 'O') continue; // Exterior
        const sx = x * TILE_SIZE - this.camX;
        const sy = y * TILE_SIZE - this.camY;
        // Check if near open door
        let nearOpenDoor = false;
        for (let di = 0; di < this.doorEntities.length; di++) {
          const door = this.doorEntities[di];
          if (!door.open && !door.animating) continue;
          if (door.hasGroup()) {
            // For grouped doors, check if tile is near the group bounding box
            const members = door.groupMembers;
            let gMinX = Infinity, gMaxX = -Infinity, gMinY = Infinity, gMaxY = -Infinity;
            for (let gi = 0; gi < members.length; gi++) {
              if (members[gi].tileX < gMinX) gMinX = members[gi].tileX;
              if (members[gi].tileX > gMaxX) gMaxX = members[gi].tileX;
              if (members[gi].tileY < gMinY) gMinY = members[gi].tileY;
              if (members[gi].tileY > gMaxY) gMaxY = members[gi].tileY;
            }
            if (x >= gMinX - 2 && x <= gMaxX + 2 && y >= gMinY - 2 && y <= gMaxY + 2 && door.open) nearOpenDoor = true;
          } else {
            const dx = door.tileX - x;
            const dy = door.tileY - y;
            if (Math.abs(dx) <= 2 && Math.abs(dy) <= 2 && door.open) nearOpenDoor = true;
          }
          if (nearOpenDoor) break;
        }
        if (nearOpenDoor) {
          ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(30,20,15,${alpha * 0.3})`;
        } else {
          ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(30,20,15,${alpha * 0.85})`;
        }
        ctx.fillRect(sx, sy, TILE_SIZE, TILE_SIZE);
      }
    }
  }

  renderParticles(ctx) {
    for (const p of this.particles) {
      p.render(ctx, this.camX, this.camY);
    }
  }

  renderFog(ctx) {
    for (let y = 0; y < this.mapHeight; y++) {
      for (let x = 0; x < this.mapWidth; x++) {
        const vis = this.visibilityGrid[y][x];
        if (vis >= 2) continue;
        const sx = x * TILE_SIZE - this.camX;
        const sy = y * TILE_SIZE - this.camY;
        if (vis === 1) {
          ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.6)';
        } else {
          ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.92)';
        }
        ctx.fillRect(sx, sy, TILE_SIZE, TILE_SIZE);
      }
    }
  }

  renderDoors(ctx) {
    const ts = TILE_SIZE;
    const doorRange = 160;
    const renderedGroups = new Set();
    for (let di = 0; di < this.doorEntities.length; di++) {
      const door = this.doorEntities[di];
      // Grouped doors: render entire group once using the first door in the group
      if (door.groupMembers) {
        if (renderedGroups.has(door)) continue;
        for (let gi = 0; gi < door.groupMembers.length; gi++) {
          renderedGroups.add(door.groupMembers[gi]);
        }
        // Compute group bounding box
        let minX = Infinity, minY = Infinity, maxX = -Infinity, maxY = -Infinity;
        for (let gi = 0; gi < door.groupMembers.length; gi++) {
          const gm = door.groupMembers[gi];
          if (gm.tileX < minX) minX = gm.tileX;
          if (gm.tileY < minY) minY = gm.tileY;
          if (gm.tileX > maxX) maxX = gm.tileX;
          if (gm.tileY > maxY) maxY = gm.tileY;
        }
        const gw = maxX - minX + 1;
        const gh = maxY - minY + 1;
        const gsx = minX * ts - this.camX;
        const gsy = minY * ts - this.camY;
        const gsw = gw * ts;
        const gsh = gh * ts;
        if (gsx + gsw < -50 || gsx > this.canvas.width + 50 || gsy + gsh < -50 || gsy > this.canvas.height + 50) continue;
        // Proximity check using group center
        const gcx = minX * ts + gsw / 2;
        const gcy = minY * ts + gsh / 2;
        let nearGroup = false;
        for (let pi = 0; pi < this.players.length; pi++) {
          const pl = this.players[pi];
          if (!pl.alive) continue;
          const pdx = pl.px - gcx;
          const pdy = pl.py - gcy;
          if (Math.sqrt(pdx * pdx + pdy * pdy) < doorRange + Math.max(gsw, gsh) * 0.5) { nearGroup = true; break; }
        }
        if (!nearGroup) continue;
        // Fill background for all tiles in the group
        ctx.fillStyle = INTERIOR_COLOR;
        ctx.fillRect(gsx, gsy, gsw, gsh);
        // Determine primary door state (use first door's state - all sync via toggle)
        const primary = door.groupMembers[0];
        const anyClosed = primary.open === false && primary.animating === false;
        const anyAnimating = primary.animating;
        const progress = primary.animProgress;
        const isHorizontal = gw > gh;
        if (anyAnimating) {
          ctx.fillStyle = DOOR_CLOSED_COLOR;
          if (isHorizontal) {
            // Horizontal group: door slides up (height shrinks)
            const doorH = gsh * 0.7 * progress;
            const doorY = gsy + gsh * 0.15;
            ctx.fillRect(gsx + gsw * 0.075, doorY, gsw * 0.85, doorH);
          } else {
            // Vertical group: door slides to the side (width shrinks)
            const doorW = gsw * 0.7 * progress;
            const doorX = gsx + gsw * 0.15;
            ctx.fillRect(doorX, gsy + gsh * 0.075, doorW, gsh * 0.85);
          }
        } else if (anyClosed) {
          ctx.fillStyle = DOOR_CLOSED_COLOR;
          if (isHorizontal) {
            const doorH = gsh * 0.7;
            const doorY = gsy + gsh * 0.15;
            ctx.fillRect(gsx + gsw * 0.075, doorY, gsw * 0.85, doorH);
          } else {
            const doorW = gsw * 0.7;
            const doorX = gsx + gsw * 0.15;
            ctx.fillRect(doorX, gsy + gsh * 0.075, doorW, gsh * 0.85);
          }
        }
        // Door handle (only when closed)
        if (anyClosed) {
          ctx.fillStyle = '#ffd700';
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.arc(gcx - this.camX, gcy - this.camY, 3, 0, Math.PI * 2);
          ctx.fill();
        }
        continue;
      }
      // Single door (no group) - original rendering
      const sx = door.tileX * ts - this.camX;
      const sy = door.tileY * ts - this.camY;
      if (sx + ts < -50 || sx > this.canvas.width + 50 || sy + ts < -50 || sy > this.canvas.height + 50) continue;
      const dx = door.tileX * ts + ts / 2;
      const dy = door.tileY * ts + ts / 2;
      let near = false;
      for (let pi = 0; pi < this.players.length; pi++) {
        const pl = this.players[pi];
        if (!pl.alive) continue;
        const pdx = pl.px - dx;
        const pdy = pl.py - dy;
        if (Math.sqrt(pdx * pdx + pdy * pdy) < doorRange) { near = true; break; }
      }
      if (!near) continue;
      ctx.fillStyle = INTERIOR_COLOR;
      ctx.fillRect(sx, sy, ts, ts);
      if (door.animating) {
        ctx.fillStyle = DOOR_CLOSED_COLOR;
        ctx.fillRect(sx + ts * 0.1, sy + ts * 0.1, ts * 0.8 * door.animProgress, ts * 0.8);
      } else if (!door.open) {
        ctx.fillStyle = DOOR_CLOSED_COLOR;
        ctx.fillRect(sx + ts * 0.1, sy + ts * 0.1, ts * 0.8, ts * 0.8);
      }
    }
  }

  renderChests(ctx) {
    for (const ch of this.chests) {
      var visible = false;
      if (this.debugMode) { visible = true; }
      else {
        for (const pl of this.players) {
          if (!pl.alive) continue;
          if (this.visibilityGrid[ch.tileY] && this.visibilityGrid[ch.tileY][ch.tileX] >= 2) { visible = true; break; }
          var cdx2 = (ch.tileX + 0.5) * TILE_SIZE - pl.px;
          var cdy2 = (ch.tileY + 0.5) * TILE_SIZE - pl.py;
          if (Math.sqrt(cdx2*cdx2 + cdy2*cdy2) < TILE_SIZE * 5) { visible = true; break; }
        }
      }
      if (!visible) continue;
      var cx = ch.tileX * TILE_SIZE - this.camX;
      var cy = ch.tileY * TILE_SIZE - this.camY;
      var chestSize = TILE_SIZE * 0.9;
      var bx = cx + (TILE_SIZE - chestSize) / 2;
      var by = cy + (TILE_SIZE - chestSize) / 2;
      var w = chestSize, h = chestSize * 0.75;
      var colors = this.getChestColors(ch.colorVariant);
      // Base
      ctx.fillStyle = colors.trim;
      ctx.fillRect(bx + w * 0.04, by + h * 0.50, w * 0.92, h * 0.30);
      // Lower body
      ctx.fillStyle = colors.body;
      ctx.fillRect(bx + w * 0.08, by + h * 0.40, w * 0.84, h * 0.22);
      // Upper body
      ctx.fillStyle = colors.upperBody;
      ctx.fillRect(bx + w * 0.06, by + h * 0.20, w * 0.88, h * 0.20);
      // Trim
      ctx.fillStyle = colors.trim;
      ctx.fillRect(bx + w * 0.04, by + h * 0.16, w * 0.92, h * 0.06);
      // Lid front
      ctx.fillStyle = colors.lid;
      ctx.fillRect(bx + w * 0.05, by + h * 0.04, w * 0.90, h * 0.14);
      // Top lid
      if (ch.opened) {
        ctx.fillStyle = colors.lid;
        ctx.fillRect(bx - w * 0.08, by - h * 0.18, w * 0.50, h * 0.22);
        ctx.fillStyle = '#111';
        ctx.fillRect(bx + w * 0.08, by + h * 0.04, w * 0.84, h * 0.12);
      } else {
        ctx.fillStyle = colors.lid;
        ctx.fillRect(bx + w * 0.04, by - h * 0.04, w * 0.92, h * 0.10);
      }
      // Lock
      ctx.fillStyle = colors.lock;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(bx + w / 2, by + h * 0.32, w * 0.08, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.strokeStyle = '#000';
      ctx.lineWidth = 0.3;
      ctx.stroke();
      // Keyhole
      ctx.fillStyle = '#111';
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(bx + w / 2, by + h * 0.34, w * 0.025, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
    }
  }

  getChestColors(variant) {
    switch (variant) {
      case 'classic': return { lid: '#8B5E3C', body: '#6B3E1C', upperBody: '#7B4E2C', trim: '#A07040', lock: '#FFD700' };
      case 'patati': return { lid: '#2266CC', body: '#FFDD00', upperBody: '#FFCC00', trim: '#FF9900', lock: '#CC2200' };
      case 'patata': return { lid: '#CC2222', body: '#FFFFFF', upperBody: '#EEEEFF', trim: '#FF6600', lock: '#FFD700' };
      case 'clown': return { lid: '#FF4488', body: '#44FF88', upperBody: '#66FF99', trim: '#FFAA00', lock: '#8844FF' };
      case 'red': return { lid: '#CC1111', body: '#880000', upperBody: '#990000', trim: '#FF3333', lock: '#FFD700' };
      case 'random': return {
        lid: '#' + Math.floor(Math.random()*16777215).toString(16).padStart(6,'0'),
        body: '#' + Math.floor(Math.random()*16777215).toString(16).padStart(6,'0'),
        upperBody: '#' + Math.floor(Math.random()*16777215).toString(16).padStart(6,'0'),
        trim: '#' + Math.floor(Math.random()*16777215).toString(16).padStart(6,'0'),
        lock: '#FFD700'
      };
      default: return { lid: '#8B5E3C', body: '#6B3E1C', upperBody: '#7B4E2C', trim: '#A07040', lock: '#FFD700' };
    }
  }

  renderContaminatedTiles(ctx) {
    for (const ct of this.contaminatedTiles) {
      var ctsx = ct.tileX * TILE_SIZE - this.camX;
      var ctsy = ct.tileY * TILE_SIZE - this.camY;
      if (ctsx + TILE_SIZE < -50 || ctsx > this.canvas.width + 50 || ctsy + TILE_SIZE < -50 || ctsy > this.canvas.height + 50) continue;
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,255,68,0.25)';
      ctx.fillRect(ctsx, ctsy, TILE_SIZE, TILE_SIZE);
      // Spawn contaminated particles
      if (Math.random() < 0.3) {
        this.contaminatedParticles.push({ x: ct.tileX * TILE_SIZE + Math.random() * TILE_SIZE, y: ct.tileY * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE * 0.8, vy: 15 + Math.random() * 25, life: 0.8 + Math.random() * 0.4 });
      }
    }
    for (const cp of this.contaminatedParticles) {
      var cpx = cp.x - this.camX, cpy = cp.y - this.camY;
      var alpha = Math.min(1, cp.life * 2);
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,255,68,' + (alpha * 0.5) + ')';
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(cpx, cpy, 2 + cp.life * 2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
    }
  }

  renderInteractionCircle(ctx) {
    if (!this.interactDisplay) return;
    const data = this.interactDisplay;
    const type = data.type;
    if (type !== 'artifact' && type !== 'altar' && type !== 'shop' && type !== 'trash') return;
    if (this.interactDuration <= 0) return;
    let interactingPlayer = null;
    for (const p of this.players) {
      if (p.interacting && p.interactTarget && p.interactType === type) {
        interactingPlayer = p; break;
      }
    }
    if (!interactingPlayer) return;
    const target = data.target;
    let worldX, worldY;
    if (type === 'artifact') {
      worldX = target.tileX * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2;
      worldY = target.tileY * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2;
    } else if (type === 'altar') {
      worldX = target.centerX;
      worldY = target.centerY;
    } else if (type === 'shop' || type === 'trash') {
      worldX = target.x * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2;
      worldY = target.y * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2;
    } else {
      worldX = target.worldX || (target.x * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2);
      worldY = target.worldY || (target.y * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2);
    }
    const sx = worldX - this.camX;
    const sy = worldY - this.camY;
    if (sx < -50 || sx > this.canvas.width + 50 || sy < -50 || sy > this.canvas.height + 50) return;
    const circleRadius = TILE_SIZE * 0.4;
    const lineWidth = circleRadius * 2 * 0.15;
    const progress = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, interactingPlayer.interactTimer / this.interactDuration));
    const startAngle = -Math.PI / 2;
    const endAngle = startAngle + Math.PI * 2 * progress;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(sx, sy, circleRadius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.strokeStyle = '#333';
    ctx.lineWidth = lineWidth + 2;
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(sx, sy, circleRadius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.strokeStyle = '#555';
    ctx.lineWidth = lineWidth;
    ctx.stroke();
    if (progress > 0) {
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(sx, sy, circleRadius, startAngle, endAngle);
      ctx.strokeStyle = Game.progressColor(progress);
      ctx.lineWidth = lineWidth;
      ctx.lineCap = 'round';
      ctx.stroke();
      ctx.lineCap = 'butt';
    }
  }

  renderUseProgressCircle(ctx) {
    const ts = TILE_SIZE;
    for (const p of this.players) {
      if (!p.alive || p.useTimer <= 0) continue;
      const worldX = p.px + ts;
      const worldY = p.py;
      const sx = worldX - this.camX;
      const sy = worldY - this.camY;
      if (sx < -50 || sx > this.canvas.width + 50 || sy < -50 || sy > this.canvas.height + 50) continue;
      const circleRadius = ts * 0.4;
      const lineWidth = circleRadius * 2 * 0.15;
      const progress = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, p.useTimer / 1));
      const startAngle = -Math.PI / 2;
      const endAngle = startAngle + Math.PI * 2 * progress;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(sx, sy, circleRadius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.2)';
      ctx.lineWidth = lineWidth + 2;
      ctx.stroke();
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(sx, sy, circleRadius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.5)';
      ctx.lineWidth = lineWidth;
      ctx.stroke();
      if (progress > 0) {
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(sx, sy, circleRadius, startAngle, endAngle);
        ctx.strokeStyle = '#fff';
        ctx.lineWidth = lineWidth;
        ctx.lineCap = 'round';
        ctx.stroke();
        ctx.lineCap = 'butt';
      }
    }
  }

  renderNight(ctx) {
    if (this.nightTransition <= 0) return;
    // Vignette
    const cx = this.canvas.width / 2;
    const cy = this.canvas.height / 2;
    const maxR = Math.sqrt(cx * cx + cy * cy);
    const gradient = ctx.createRadialGradient(cx, cy, maxR * 0.3, cx, cy, maxR);
    gradient.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
    gradient.addColorStop(0.5, `rgba(0,0,30,${this.nightTransition * 0.3})`);
    gradient.addColorStop(1, `rgba(0,0,0,${this.nightTransition * 0.7})`);
    ctx.fillStyle = gradient;
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, this.canvas.width, this.canvas.height);
  }

  renderDebug(ctx) {
    ctx.font = '10px monospace';
    ctx.fillStyle = '#0f0';
    // Waypoints
    for (const wp of this.waypoints) {
      const sx = wp.x * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2 - this.camX;
      const sy = wp.y * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2 - this.camY;
      ctx.strokeStyle = '#ff0';
      ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      ctx.strokeRect(sx - 4, sy - 4, 8, 8);
      ctx.fillText('U', sx + 6, sy - 6);
    }
    // Monster states
    for (const m of this.activeMonsters) {
      if (m.alive) {
        const mx = m.px - this.camX;
        const my = m.py - this.camY;
        ctx.fillStyle = '#f0f';
        ctx.fillText(m.type.toUpperCase() + ' ' + m.state.toUpperCase(), mx + 12, my - 12);
        m.renderVisionCone(ctx, this);
      }
    }
    // FPS
    ctx.fillText('FPS: ' + Math.round(1000 / (performance.now() - this.lastTime + 0.001)), 10, this.canvas.height - 10);
    // Player info
    let yOff = this.canvas.height - 45;
    for (const p of this.players) {
      const vr = this.playerVisionRange[p.playerIndex] || 0;
      ctx.fillText(p.name + ' Tile: ' + p.tileX + ',' + p.tileY + ' HP:' + p.hp + ' Class:' + p.playerClass + ' VisRng:' + (vr | 0) + ' Zone:' + this.getZoneName(p.tileX, p.tileY), 10, yOff);
      yOff -= 15;
    }
    // Raycast perf info
    const totalRays = this.visibilityPolygons.reduce((s, vp) => s + vp.points.length, 0);
    ctx.fillText('Rays: ' + totalRays + ' | Diff: ' + this.difficulty + ' | Rnd: ' + this.roundNumber + ' | Spd:+' + this.getSpeedBonusPercent() + '%', 10, this.canvas.height - 30);
    ctx.fillText('Traps: ' + this.traps.length + ' | Shops: ' + this.shopItems.filter(s => !s.sold).length + ' | Cash: ' + this.currency, 10, this.canvas.height - 15);
    // Random spawn points
    for (const rp of this.randomMonsterSpawnPoints) {
      const sx = rp.x * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2 - this.camX;
      const sy = rp.y * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2 - this.camY;
      ctx.strokeStyle = '#0f0';
      ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      ctx.strokeRect(sx - 6, sy - 6, 12, 12);
      ctx.fillStyle = '#0f0';
      ctx.fillText('m', sx + 8, sy + 4);
    }
    // Chest markers
    for (const ch of this.chests) {
      const csx = ch.tileX * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2 - this.camX;
      const csy = ch.tileY * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2 - this.camY;
      ctx.strokeStyle = '#ff8800';
      ctx.lineWidth = 2;
      ctx.strokeRect(csx - TILE_SIZE * 0.4, csy - TILE_SIZE * 0.3, TILE_SIZE * 0.8, TILE_SIZE * 0.6);
      ctx.fillStyle = '#ff8800';
      ctx.fillRect(csx - TILE_SIZE * 0.4, csy - TILE_SIZE * 0.3, TILE_SIZE * 0.8, TILE_SIZE * 0.6);
      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.15;
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
      ctx.fillStyle = '#fff';
      ctx.font = '10px monospace';
      ctx.fillText(ch.opened ? 'Chest(open)' : 'Chest', csx + 10, csy - 10);
      ctx.font = '10px monospace';
    }
    // Monster type info
    const mt = MonsterTypes[this.monsterSpawnType];
    if (mt) {
    ctx.fillText('Monster: ' + mt.name + ' | SpdMod: ' + (mt.speedMod || 1).toFixed(1) + 'x | RndSpawn: ' + mt.randomSpawn, 10, this.canvas.height - 60);

    // Player raycast rays (yellow)
    for (var vpi = 0; vpi < this.visibilityPolygons.length; vpi++) {
      var vp = this.visibilityPolygons[vpi];
      var pl = vp.player;
      if (!pl || !pl.alive) continue;
      var psx = pl.px - this.camX;
      var psy = pl.py - this.camY;

      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255, 255, 0, 0.25)';
      ctx.lineWidth = 0.5;

      // Desenhar linhas do jogador ate cada ponto do poligono
      // Apenas uma a cada 8 raios para nao poluir visualmente
      for (var ri = 0; ri < vp.points.length; ri += 8) {
        var pt = vp.points[ri];
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(psx, psy);
        ctx.lineTo(pt.x - this.camX, pt.y - this.camY);
        ctx.stroke();
      }

      // Pequeno ponto amarelo no final de cada raio desenhado
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255, 255, 0, 0.5)';
      for (var ri2 = 0; ri2 < vp.points.length; ri2 += 8) {
        var pt2 = vp.points[ri2];
        var epx = pt2.x - this.camX;
        var epy = pt2.y - this.camY;
        ctx.fillRect(epx - 1, epy - 1, 2, 2);
      }
    }
  }
  }

  renderMessage(ctx) {
    // message is DOM-based, nothing to render on canvas
  }

  renderInteractionBar() {
    // interaction bar is DOM-based
  }

  // ============================================================
  // SAVE/LOAD SYSTEM
  // ============================================================
  serializeGameState() {
    var gs = {
      roundNumber: this.roundNumber,
      summonCount: this.summonCount,
      currency: this.currency,
      collectedArtifacts: this.collectedArtifacts,
      artifactCount: this.artifactCount,
      isNight: this.isNight,
      nightTransition: this.nightTransition,
      roofAlpha: this.roofAlpha,
      hotbarSize: this.hotbarSize,
      hotbarLayout: this.hotbarLayout,
      hotbarGap: this.hotbarGap,
      monsterSpawnType: this.monsterSpawnType,
      pendingMonsterType: this.pendingMonsterType || null,
      selectedMonsterType: this.selectedMonsterType || null,
      debugSelectedMonster: this.debugSelectedMonster || null,
      summoning: this.summoning,
      summonDelay: this.summonDelay,
      lockedDoorCheckTimer: this.lockedDoorCheckTimer || 0
    };

    // Players
    gs.players = [];
    for (var i = 0; i < this.players.length; i++) {
      var p = this.players[i];
      var dup = [];
      if (p.duplicatedSlots) {
        p.duplicatedSlots.forEach(function(s) { dup.push(s); });
      }
      gs.players.push({
        index: p.playerIndex,
        px: p.px, py: p.py, tileX: p.tileX, tileY: p.tileY,
        hp: p.hp, alive: p.alive, inventory: p.inventory.slice(),
        selectedSlot: p.selectedSlot, duplicatedSlots: dup,
        speedItemTimer: p.speedItemTimer, speedItemMult: p.speedItemMult,
        invulnTimer: p.invulnTimer,
        invisible: p.invisible, invisibleTimer: p.invisibleTimer,
        abilityCooldown: p.abilityCooldown, abilityActive: p.abilityActive,
        abilityTimer: p.abilityTimer,
        timeTravelUsed: p.timeTravelUsed, wiredGirlUsed: p.wiredGirlUsed,
        rerrolerUsed: p.rerrolerUsed, helpfulpriestUsed: p.helpfulpriestUsed,
        phasing: p.phasing, phasingTimer: p.phasingTimer,
        commandYellMult: p.commandYellMult, commandYellTimer: p.commandYellTimer,
        menuOpen: p.menuOpen, interactTimer: p.interactTimer,
        interacting: p.interacting, lastDx: p.lastDx, lastDy: p.lastDy,
        name: p.name, playerClass: p.playerClass,
        shieldCharges: p.shieldCharges || 0, luckyCharmActive: p.luckyCharmActive || false,
        allyWarpUses: p.allyWarpUses || 0, stationaryTimer: p.stationaryTimer || 0,
        healingAuraSlow: p.healingAuraSlow || false,
        keybinds: {
          up: p.keybinds.up, down: p.keybinds.down,
          left: p.keybinds.left, right: p.keybinds.right,
          interact: p.keybinds.interact, ability: p.keybinds.ability,
          use: p.keybinds.use
        }
      });
    }

    // Active monsters
    gs.activeMonsters = [];
    for (var mi = 0; mi < this.activeMonsters.length; mi++) {
      var m = this.activeMonsters[mi];
      var lkpp = null;
      if (m.lastKnownPlayerPos) {
        lkpp = { x: m.lastKnownPlayerPos.x, y: m.lastKnownPlayerPos.y };
      }
      gs.activeMonsters.push({
        type: m.type, px: m.px, py: m.py, tileX: m.tileX, tileY: m.tileY,
        speed: m.speed, alive: m.alive, state: m.state,
        stunTimer: m.stunTimer, tauntTimer: m.tauntTimer,
        tauntTarget: m.tauntTarget ? { x: m.tauntTarget.x, y: m.tauntTarget.y } : null,
        preTauntState: m.preTauntState,
        currentWaypoint: m.currentWaypoint, waypointOrder: m.waypointOrder.slice(),
        facingAngle: m.facingAngle, chaseTimer: m.chaseTimer,
        searchTimer: m.searchTimer, lastKnownPlayerPos: lkpp,
        attackCooldown: m.attackCooldown,
        path: null, pathTarget: null, pathRecalc: m.pathRecalc,
        trapSeeking: m.trapSeeking, frozen: m.frozen,
        isTransformed: m.isTransformed, ghostTimer: m.ghostTimer,
        ghostVisible: m.ghostVisible, rushSpeedBonus: m.rushSpeedBonus,
        decoySpeedBoostTimer: m.decoySpeedBoostTimer,
        jesterPathRecalc: m.jesterPathRecalc,
        speedScaleBonus: m.speedScaleBonus || 0, speedScaleTimer: m.speedScaleTimer || 0,
        berserkerBonus: m.berserkerBonus || 0, shieldActive: m.shieldActive !== undefined ? m.shieldActive : true,
        bombTimer: m.bombTimer || 0, stolenItems: (m.stolenItems || []).slice()
      });
    }

    // Doors
    gs.doorEntities = [];
    for (var di = 0; di < this.doorEntities.length; di++) {
      var d = this.doorEntities[di];
      gs.doorEntities.push({
        tileX: d.tileX, tileY: d.tileY,
        open: d.open, animating: d.animating,
        animTimer: d.animTimer, animProgress: d.animProgress,
        debounceTimer: d.debounceTimer
      });
    }

    // Artifacts
    gs.artifactEntities = [];
    for (var ai = 0; ai < this.artifactEntities.length; ai++) {
      var art = this.artifactEntities[ai];
      gs.artifactEntities.push({
        tileX: art.tileX, tileY: art.tileY, collected: art.collected,
        isFake: art.isFake,
        displayName: art.displayName || 'Artifact',
        decoyRefIndex: art.decoyRef ? this.activeMonsters.indexOf(art.decoyRef) : -1
      });
    }

    // Traps
    gs.traps = [];
    for (var ti = 0; ti < this.traps.length; ti++) {
      var t = this.traps[ti];
      gs.traps.push({
        x: t.x, y: t.y, item: t.item, active: t.active,
        stun: t.stun, trapColor: t.trapColor, trapRadius: t.trapRadius,
        tauntDuration: t.tauntDuration || 0,
        tauntTimer: t.tauntTimer || 0,
        tauntedMonsterIndex: t.tauntedMonster ? this.activeMonsters.indexOf(t.tauntedMonster) : -1,
        lifetime: t.lifetime, timer: t.timer, rotation: t.rotation
      });
    }

    // Shop items
    gs.shopItems = [];
    for (var si = 0; si < this.shopItems.length; si++) {
      var sh = this.shopItems[si];
      gs.shopItems.push({
        x: sh.x, y: sh.y, itemKey: sh.itemKey, price: sh.price, sold: sh.sold
      });
    }

    // Clones
    gs.activeClones = [];
    for (var ci = 0; ci < this.activeClones.length; ci++) {
      var c = this.activeClones[ci];
      gs.activeClones.push({
        px: c.px, py: c.py, tileX: c.tileX, tileY: c.tileY,
        timer: c.timer, targetMonsterIndex: c.targetMonster ? this.activeMonsters.indexOf(c.targetMonster) : -1,
        playerIndex: c.playerIndex, path: null, playerColor: c.playerColor
      });
    }

    // Walls
    gs.activeWalls = [];
    for (var wi = 0; wi < this.activeWalls.length; wi++) {
      var w = this.activeWalls[wi];
      var wt = [];
      for (var wti = 0; wti < w.tiles.length; wti++) {
        wt.push({ x: w.tiles[wti].x, y: w.tiles[wti].y });
      }
      gs.activeWalls.push({ tiles: wt, timer: w.timer });
    }

    // Zones
    gs.activeZones = [];
    for (var zi = 0; zi < this.activeZones.length; zi++) {
      var z = this.activeZones[zi];
      gs.activeZones.push({
        x: z.x, y: z.y, radius: z.radius, timer: z.timer,
        ownerIndex: z.ownerIndex, bonusSpeed: z.bonusSpeed, monsterSpeed: z.monsterSpeed
      });
    }

    // Locked door
    gs.lockedDoor = null;
    if (this.lockedDoor) {
      var ldt = [];
      for (var ldi = 0; ldi < this.lockedDoor.tiles.length; ldi++) {
        var lt = this.lockedDoor.tiles[ldi];
        var doorIdx = -1;
        if (lt.doorRef) doorIdx = this.doorEntities.indexOf(lt.doorRef);
        ldt.push({ tileX: lt.tileX, tileY: lt.tileY, doorRefIndex: doorIdx });
      }
      gs.lockedDoor = { tiles: ldt, timer: this.lockedDoor.timer, lockedByPlayerIndex: this.lockedDoor.lockedByPlayerIndex };
    }

    // Rat entities
    gs.ratEntities = [];
    for (var ri = 0; ri < this.ratEntities.length; ri++) {
      var rat = this.ratEntities[ri];
      gs.ratEntities.push({
        px: rat.px, py: rat.py, tileX: rat.tileX, tileY: rat.tileY,
        targetTrap: null, devourTimer: rat.devourTimer, alive: rat.alive,
        kingRefIndex: rat.kingRef ? this.activeMonsters.indexOf(rat.kingRef) : -1
      });
    }

    // Expanded hotkey players
    gs.expandedHotkeyPlayers = [];
    if (this.expandedHotkeyPlayers) {
      this.expandedHotkeyPlayers.forEach(function(v) { gs.expandedHotkeyPlayers.push(v); });
    }

    return gs;
  }

  deserializeGameState(gs) {
    var self = this;
    // Restore global state
    this.roundNumber = gs.roundNumber || 0;
    this.summonCount = gs.summonCount || 0;
    this.currency = gs.currency || 0;
    this.collectedArtifacts = gs.collectedArtifacts || 0;
    this.artifactCount = gs.artifactCount || 3;
    this.isNight = gs.isNight || false;
    this.nightTransition = gs.nightTransition || 0;
    this.roofAlpha = gs.roofAlpha || 1;
    this.hotbarSize = gs.hotbarSize || 100;
    this.hotbarLayout = gs.hotbarLayout || 'horizontal';
    this.hotbarGap = gs.hotbarGap || 30;
    this.monsterSpawnType = gs.monsterSpawnType || 'normal';
    this.pendingMonsterType = gs.pendingMonsterType || null;
    this.selectedMonsterType = gs.selectedMonsterType || null;
    this.debugSelectedMonster = gs.debugSelectedMonster || null;
    this.summoning = gs.summoning || false;
    this.summonDelay = gs.summonDelay || 0;
    this.lockedDoorCheckTimer = gs.lockedDoorCheckTimer || 0;

    // Restore doors first (needed for pathfinding)
    this.doorEntities = [];
    for (var di = 0; di < (gs.doorEntities || []).length; di++) {
      var ds = gs.doorEntities[di];
      var door = new DoorEntity(ds.tileX, ds.tileY);
      door.open = ds.open;
      door.animating = ds.animating;
      door.animTimer = ds.animTimer;
      door.animProgress = ds.animProgress;
      door.debounceTimer = ds.debounceTimer;
      this.doorEntities.push(door);
    }

    // Rebuild door groups (same algorithm as loadMap)
    var doorByPos = {};
    for (var di2 = 0; di2 < this.doorEntities.length; di2++) {
      var d2 = this.doorEntities[di2];
      doorByPos[d2.tileX + ',' + d2.tileY] = d2;
    }
    var visited = {};
    for (var di3 = 0; di3 < this.doorEntities.length; di3++) {
      var startDoor = this.doorEntities[di3];
      var key = startDoor.tileX + ',' + startDoor.tileY;
      if (visited[key]) continue;
      var component = [];
      var queue = [startDoor];
      visited[key] = true;
      while (queue.length > 0) {
        var current = queue.shift();
        component.push(current);
        var dirs = [[0,-1],[0,1],[-1,0],[1,0]];
        for (var ddi = 0; ddi < dirs.length; ddi++) {
          var nx = current.tileX + dirs[ddi][0];
          var ny = current.tileY + dirs[ddi][1];
          var nkey = nx + ',' + ny;
          var neighbor = doorByPos[nkey];
          if (neighbor && !visited[nkey]) {
            visited[nkey] = true;
            queue.push(neighbor);
          }
        }
      }
      if (component.length >= 2) {
        for (var ci = 0; ci < component.length; ci++) {
          component[ci].groupMembers = component;
        }
      }
    }

    // Restore artifacts
    this.artifactEntities = [];
    for (var ai = 0; ai < (gs.artifactEntities || []).length; ai++) {
      var as = gs.artifactEntities[ai];
      var art = new ArtifactEntity(as.tileX, as.tileY);
      art.collected = as.collected;
      art.isFake = as.isFake || false;
      art.displayName = as.displayName || 'Artifact';
      this.artifactEntities.push(art);
    }

    // Restore traps
    this.traps = [];
    for (var ti = 0; ti < (gs.traps || []).length; ti++) {
      var ts = gs.traps[ti];
      this.traps.push({
        x: ts.x, y: ts.y, item: ts.item, active: ts.active,
        stun: ts.stun, trapColor: ts.trapColor, trapRadius: ts.trapRadius,
        tauntDuration: ts.tauntDuration || 0,
        tauntTimer: ts.tauntTimer || 0,
        tauntedMonster: null, // restored after monsters
        lifetime: ts.lifetime, timer: ts.timer, rotation: ts.rotation
      });
    }

    // Restore shop items
    this.shopItems = [];
    for (var si = 0; si < (gs.shopItems || []).length; si++) {
      var ss = gs.shopItems[si];
      this.shopItems.push({
        x: ss.x, y: ss.y, itemKey: ss.itemKey, price: ss.price, sold: ss.sold,
        worldX: ss.x * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2,
        worldY: ss.y * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2
      });
    }

    // Restore players
    var oldPlayers = this.players;
    this.players = [];
    for (var pi = 0; pi < (gs.players || []).length; pi++) {
      var ps = gs.players[pi];
      var p = new Player(ps.tileX || 0, ps.tileY || 0, ps.index, ps.name, ps.playerClass);
      p.px = ps.px; p.py = ps.py;
      p.tileX = ps.tileX; p.tileY = ps.tileY;
      p.hp = ps.hp; p.alive = ps.alive;
      p.inventory = ps.inventory ? ps.inventory.slice() : [null,null,null,null,null];
      p.selectedSlot = ps.selectedSlot || 0;
      p.duplicatedSlots = new Set(ps.duplicatedSlots || []);
      p.speedItemTimer = ps.speedItemTimer || 0;
      p.speedItemMult = ps.speedItemMult || 1;
      p.invulnTimer = ps.invulnTimer || 0;
      p.invisible = ps.invisible || false;
      p.invisibleTimer = ps.invisibleTimer || 0;
      p.abilityCooldown = ps.abilityCooldown || 0;
      p.abilityActive = ps.abilityActive || false;
      p.abilityTimer = ps.abilityTimer || 0;
      p.timeTravelUsed = ps.timeTravelUsed || false;
      p.wiredGirlUsed = ps.wiredGirlUsed || false;
      p.rerrolerUsed = ps.rerrolerUsed || false;
      p.helpfulpriestUsed = ps.helpfulpriestUsed || false;
      p.phasing = ps.phasing || false;
      p.phasingTimer = ps.phasingTimer || 0;
      p.commandYellMult = ps.commandYellMult || 0;
      p.commandYellTimer = ps.commandYellTimer || 0;
      p.menuOpen = ps.menuOpen || false;
      p.interactTimer = ps.interactTimer || 0;
      p.interacting = ps.interacting || false;
      p.lastDx = ps.lastDx || 0;
      p.lastDy = ps.lastDy || 0;
      p.shieldCharges = ps.shieldCharges || 0;
      p.luckyCharmActive = ps.luckyCharmActive || false;
      p.allyWarpUses = ps.allyWarpUses || 0;
      p.stationaryTimer = ps.stationaryTimer || 0;
      p.healingAuraSlow = ps.healingAuraSlow || false;
      if (ps.keybinds) {
        p.keybinds = {
          up: ps.keybinds.up, down: ps.keybinds.down,
          left: ps.keybinds.left, right: ps.keybinds.right,
          interact: ps.keybinds.interact, ability: ps.keybinds.ability,
          use: ps.keybinds.use
        };
      }
      p.initialSpawnDone = true;
      if (p.playerClass === 'scout' && p.abilityActive) p.speed = p.baseSpeed * 1.5;
      else p.speed = p.baseSpeed;
      this.players.push(p);
    }

    // Restore active monsters
    this.activeMonsters = [];
    for (var mi = 0; mi < (gs.activeMonsters || []).length; mi++) {
      var ms = gs.activeMonsters[mi];
      var m = new MonsterEntity(ms.tileX, ms.tileY, this, ms.type);
      m.px = ms.px; m.py = ms.py;
      m.tileX = ms.tileX; m.tileY = ms.tileY;
      m.speed = ms.speed; m.alive = ms.alive; m.state = ms.state;
      m.stunTimer = ms.stunTimer || 0;
      m.tauntTimer = ms.tauntTimer || 0;
      m.tauntTarget = ms.tauntTarget ? { x: ms.tauntTarget.x, y: ms.tauntTarget.y } : null;
      m.preTauntState = ms.preTauntState || 'patrol';
      m.currentWaypoint = ms.currentWaypoint || 0;
      m.waypointOrder = ms.waypointOrder ? ms.waypointOrder.slice() : [];
      m.facingAngle = ms.facingAngle || 0;
      m.chaseTimer = ms.chaseTimer || 0;
      m.searchTimer = ms.searchTimer || 0;
      m.lastKnownPlayerPos = ms.lastKnownPlayerPos ? { x: ms.lastKnownPlayerPos.x, y: ms.lastKnownPlayerPos.y } : null;
      m.attackCooldown = ms.attackCooldown || 0;
      m.pathRecalc = ms.pathRecalc || 0;
      m.trapSeeking = ms.trapSeeking || false;
      m.frozen = ms.frozen || false;
      m.isTransformed = ms.isTransformed || false;
      m.ghostTimer = ms.ghostTimer || 0;
      m.ghostVisible = ms.ghostVisible !== false;
      m.rushSpeedBonus = ms.rushSpeedBonus || 0;
      m.decoySpeedBoostTimer = ms.decoySpeedBoostTimer || 0;
      m.jesterPathRecalc = ms.jesterPathRecalc || 0;
      m.speedScaleBonus = ms.speedScaleBonus || 0;
      m.speedScaleTimer = ms.speedScaleTimer || 0;
      m.berserkerBonus = ms.berserkerBonus || 0;
      m.shieldActive = ms.shieldActive !== undefined ? ms.shieldActive : true;
      m.bombTimer = ms.bombTimer || 0;
      m.stolenItems = ms.stolenItems || [];
      m.path = null;
      m.pathTarget = null;
      this.activeMonsters.push(m);
    }

    // Restore cross-references
    // Artifact decoy refs
    for (var ai2 = 0; ai2 < this.artifactEntities.length; ai2++) {
      var as2 = gs.artifactEntities[ai2];
      if (as2.decoyRefIndex >= 0 && as2.decoyRefIndex < this.activeMonsters.length) {
        this.artifactEntities[ai2].decoyRef = this.activeMonsters[as2.decoyRefIndex];
      }
    }

    // Trap taunted monster refs
    for (var ti2 = 0; ti2 < this.traps.length; ti2++) {
      var ts2 = gs.traps[ti2];
      if (ts2.tauntedMonsterIndex >= 0 && ts2.tauntedMonsterIndex < this.activeMonsters.length) {
        this.traps[ti2].tauntedMonster = this.activeMonsters[ts2.tauntedMonsterIndex];
      }
    }

    // Restore clones with cross-refs
    this.activeClones = [];
    for (var ci2 = 0; ci2 < (gs.activeClones || []).length; ci2++) {
      var cs = gs.activeClones[ci2];
      var clone = {
        px: cs.px, py: cs.py, tileX: cs.tileX, tileY: cs.tileY,
        timer: cs.timer, targetMonster: cs.targetMonsterIndex >= 0 && cs.targetMonsterIndex < this.activeMonsters.length ? this.activeMonsters[cs.targetMonsterIndex] : null,
        playerIndex: cs.playerIndex, alive: true, path: null, playerColor: cs.playerColor
      };
      this.activeClones.push(clone);
    }

    // Restore walls
    this.activeWalls = [];
    for (var wi2 = 0; wi2 < (gs.activeWalls || []).length; wi2++) {
      var ws = gs.activeWalls[wi2];
      this.activeWalls.push({ tiles: ws.tiles, timer: ws.timer });
    }

    // Restore zones
    this.activeZones = [];
    for (var zi2 = 0; zi2 < (gs.activeZones || []).length; zi2++) {
      var zs = gs.activeZones[zi2];
      this.activeZones.push({
        x: zs.x, y: zs.y, radius: zs.radius, timer: zs.timer,
        ownerIndex: zs.ownerIndex, bonusSpeed: zs.bonusSpeed, monsterSpeed: zs.monsterSpeed
      });
    }

    // Restore locked door
    this.lockedDoor = null;
    if (gs.lockedDoor && gs.lockedDoor.tiles) {
      var ldt2 = [];
      for (var ldi2 = 0; ldi2 < gs.lockedDoor.tiles.length; ldi2++) {
        var lts = gs.lockedDoor.tiles[ldi2];
        var doorRef = lts.doorRefIndex >= 0 && lts.doorRefIndex < this.doorEntities.length ? this.doorEntities[lts.doorRefIndex] : null;
        ldt2.push({ doorRef: doorRef, tileX: lts.tileX, tileY: lts.tileY });
      }
      this.lockedDoor = { tiles: ldt2, timer: gs.lockedDoor.timer !== undefined ? gs.lockedDoor.timer : 2.0, lockedByPlayerIndex: gs.lockedDoor.lockedByPlayerIndex };
    }

    // Restore rat entities
    this.ratEntities = [];
    for (var ri2 = 0; ri2 < (gs.ratEntities || []).length; ri2++) {
      var rs = gs.ratEntities[ri2];
      this.ratEntities.push({
        px: rs.px, py: rs.py, tileX: rs.tileX, tileY: rs.tileY,
        targetTrap: null, devourTimer: rs.devourTimer, alive: rs.alive,
        kingRef: rs.kingRefIndex >= 0 && rs.kingRefIndex < this.activeMonsters.length ? this.activeMonsters[rs.kingRefIndex] : null,
        path: null, pathTarget: null
      });
    }

    // Restore expanded hotkey players
    this.expandedHotkeyPlayers = new Set();
    if (gs.expandedHotkeyPlayers) {
      for (var ei = 0; ei < gs.expandedHotkeyPlayers.length; ei++) {
        this.expandedHotkeyPlayers.add(gs.expandedHotkeyPlayers[ei]);
      }
    }

    // Reset interact display
    this.interactDisplay = null;
    this.interactDuration = 0;

    // Rebuild visibility
    this.updateCamera();
    this.updateVisionRanges(0.016);
  }

  saveGame(slotNum) {
    try {
      var config = {
        difficulty: this.difficulty,
        playerCount: this.playerCount,
        players: []
      };
      for (var i = 0; i < this.players.length; i++) {
        var p = this.players[i];
        config.players.push({
          index: i,
          name: this.playerNames[i] || p.name,
          class: p.playerClass,
          keybinds: {
            up: p.keybinds.up, down: p.keybinds.down,
            left: p.keybinds.left, right: p.keybinds.right,
            interact: p.keybinds.interact, ability: p.keybinds.ability,
            use: p.keybinds.use
          }
        });
      }
      var gameState = this.serializeGameState();
      var data = {
        version: 1,
        type: 'save',
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
        config: config,
        gameState: gameState
      };
      var json = JSON.stringify(data);
      localStorage.setItem('summongame_save_' + slotNum, json);
      return true;
    } catch(e) {
      console.error('Save failed:', e);
      return false;
    }
  }

  loadGame(slotNum) {
    try {
      var json = localStorage.getItem('summongame_save_' + slotNum);
      if (!json) return false;
      var data = JSON.parse(json);
      if (!data || data.type !== 'save') return false;

      // Load config
      if (data.config) {
        this.difficulty = data.config.difficulty || 'easy';
        this.playerCount = data.config.playerCount || 1;
        if (data.config.players) {
          for (var i = 0; i < data.config.players.length; i++) {
            var cp = data.config.players[i];
            this.playerNames[cp.index] = cp.name;
            this.playerClasses[cp.index] = cp.class;
          }
        }
      }

      // Load game state
      if (data.gameState) {
        this.deserializeGameState(data.gameState);
      }

      // Update UI
      updateHUD(this);
      updateAbilityHUD(this);
      updateHotbarHUD(this);
      updateMonsterSelector(this);
      updateItemSelector(this);
      return true;
    } catch(e) {
      console.error('Load failed:', e);
      return false;
    }
  }

  saveConfig(slotNum) {
    try {
      var players = [];
      for (var i = 0; i < this.playerCount; i++) {
        var kb = preGameKeybinds[i] || DEFAULT_KEYBINDS[i];
        // If in-game, use player keybinds
        if (this.players[i]) {
          kb = {
            up: this.players[i].keybinds.up, down: this.players[i].keybinds.down,
            left: this.players[i].keybinds.left, right: this.players[i].keybinds.right,
            interact: this.players[i].keybinds.interact,
            ability: this.players[i].keybinds.ability,
            use: this.players[i].keybinds.use
          };
        }
        players.push({
          index: i,
          name: this.playerNames[i] || ('P' + (i + 1)),
          class: this.playerClasses[i] || 'oracle',
          keybinds: kb
        });
      }
      var data = {
        version: 1,
        type: 'config',
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
        data: {
          difficulty: this.difficulty,
          playerCount: this.playerCount,
          players: players
        }
      };
      var json = JSON.stringify(data);
      localStorage.setItem('summongame_config_' + slotNum, json);
      return true;
    } catch(e) {
      console.error('Save config failed:', e);
      return false;
    }
  }

  loadConfig(slotNum) {
    try {
      var json = localStorage.getItem('summongame_config_' + slotNum);
      if (!json) return false;
      var data = JSON.parse(json);
      if (!data || data.type !== 'config') return false;
      var d = data.data;
      this.difficulty = d.difficulty || 'easy';
      this.playerCount = d.playerCount || 1;
      if (d.players) {
        for (var i = 0; i < d.players.length; i++) {
          var cp = d.players[i];
          this.playerNames[cp.index] = cp.name;
          this.playerClasses[cp.index] = cp.class;
          if (!preGameKeybinds[cp.index]) preGameKeybinds[cp.index] = {};
          if (cp.keybinds) {
            preGameKeybinds[cp.index].up = cp.keybinds.up;
            preGameKeybinds[cp.index].down = cp.keybinds.down;
            preGameKeybinds[cp.index].left = cp.keybinds.left;
            preGameKeybinds[cp.index].right = cp.keybinds.right;
            preGameKeybinds[cp.index].interact = cp.keybinds.interact;
            preGameKeybinds[cp.index].ability = cp.keybinds.ability;
            preGameKeybinds[cp.index].use = cp.keybinds.use;
          }
        }
      }
      // Refresh class selection UI
      this.showClassSelection();
      return true;
    } catch(e) {
      console.error('Load config failed:', e);
      return false;
    }
  }

  exportSave(slotNum, saveType) {
    var storageKey = saveType === 'save' ? 'summongame_save_' + slotNum : 'summongame_config_' + slotNum;
    var json = localStorage.getItem(storageKey);
    if (!json) { this.showMessage('Slot ' + slotNum + ' is empty!'); return; }
    var now = new Date();
    var ts = now.getFullYear() +
      ('0' + (now.getMonth() + 1)).slice(-2) +
      ('0' + now.getDate()).slice(-2) + '_' +
      ('0' + now.getHours()).slice(-2) +
      ('0' + now.getMinutes()).slice(-2) +
      ('0' + now.getSeconds()).slice(-2);
    var fn = 'SummonGame_' + (saveType === 'save' ? 'Save' : 'Config') + '_Slot' + slotNum + '_' + ts + '.json';
    var blob = new Blob([json], { type: 'application/json' });
    var a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    a.download = fn;
    a.click();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href);
    if (saveType === 'save') {
      this.showMessage('Game exported to ' + fn);
    } else {
      this.showMessage('Config exported to ' + fn);
    }
  }

  importSave(slotNum, saveType, file) {
    var self = this;
    var reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = function(e) {
      try {
        var json = e.target.result;
        var data = JSON.parse(json);
        if (!data || !data.version) { self.showMessage('Invalid save file!'); return; }
        var storageKey = saveType === 'save' ? 'summongame_save_' + slotNum : 'summongame_config_' + slotNum;
        localStorage.setItem(storageKey, json);
        if (saveType === 'save') {
          self.loadGame(slotNum);
          self.showMessage('Save imported and loaded!');
        } else {
          self.loadConfig(slotNum);
          self.showMessage('Config imported!');
        }
      } catch(e2) {
        self.showMessage('Failed to import file: ' + e2.message);
      }
    };
    reader.readAsText(file);
  }

  showSaveSlots(saveType) {
    var self = this;
    var box = document.getElementById('save-load-box');
    var title = saveType === 'save' ? 'Save Game' : 'Save Configuration';
    var storagePrefix = saveType === 'save' ? 'summongame_save_' : 'summongame_config_';
    var html = '<h3>' + title + '</h3>';
    for (var i = 1; i <= 3; i++) {
      var json = localStorage.getItem(storagePrefix + i);
      var label = 'Slot ' + i + ' - ';
      var hasSave = false;
      if (json) {
        try {
          var d = JSON.parse(json);
          hasSave = true;
          if (d.type === 'save' && d.gameState) {
            label += 'Round ' + (d.gameState.roundNumber || 0) + ' - ' + (d.config ? d.config.difficulty : '?') + ' - ' + (d.config ? d.config.playerCount : '?') + 'P';
          } else if (d.type === 'config' && d.data) {
            label += d.data.difficulty + ' - ' + d.data.playerCount + 'P';
          }
          if (d.timestamp) {
            label += ' - ' + d.timestamp.substring(0, 16).replace('T', ' ');
          }
        } catch(e) { label += 'Corrupted'; }
      } else {
        label += 'Empty';
      }
      html += '<button class="' + (hasSave ? 'has-save' : '') + '" onclick="window._saveToSlot(' + i + ',\'' + saveType + '\')">' + label + '</button>';
    }
      html += '<button class="file-btn" onclick="window._exportSlotFile(\'' + saveType + '\')">Export as File</button>';
    html += '<button class="cancel-btn" onclick="window._closeSavePanel()">Cancel</button>';
    box.innerHTML = html;
    document.getElementById('save-load-subpanel').classList.add('open');
    this.paused = true;
    document.getElementById('pause-overlay').classList.add('active');
  }

  showLoadSlots(saveType) {
    var self = this;
    var box = document.getElementById('save-load-box');
    var title = saveType === 'save' ? 'Load Game' : 'Load Configuration';
    var storagePrefix = saveType === 'save' ? 'summongame_save_' : 'summongame_config_';
    var html = '<h3>' + title + '</h3>';
    for (var i = 1; i <= 3; i++) {
      var json = localStorage.getItem(storagePrefix + i);
      var label = 'Slot ' + i + ' - ';
      var hasSave = false;
      if (json) {
        try {
          var d = JSON.parse(json);
          hasSave = true;
          if (d.type === 'save' && d.gameState) {
            label += 'Round ' + (d.gameState.roundNumber || 0) + ' - ' + (d.config ? d.config.difficulty : '?') + ' - ' + (d.config ? d.config.playerCount : '?') + 'P';
          } else if (d.type === 'config' && d.data) {
            label += d.data.difficulty + ' - ' + d.data.playerCount + 'P';
          }
          if (d.timestamp) {
            label += ' - ' + d.timestamp.substring(0, 16).replace('T', ' ');
          }
        } catch(e) { label += 'Corrupted'; }
      } else {
        label += 'Empty';
      }
      html += '<button class="' + (hasSave ? 'has-save' : '') + '" onclick="window._loadFromSlot(' + i + ',\'' + saveType + '\')">' + label + '</button>';
    }
      html += '<button class="file-btn" onclick="window._importSlotFile(\'' + saveType + '\')">Import from File</button>';
    html += '<button class="cancel-btn" onclick="window._closeSavePanel()">Cancel</button>';
    box.innerHTML = html;
    document.getElementById('save-load-subpanel').classList.add('open');
    this.paused = true;
    document.getElementById('pause-overlay').classList.add('active');
  }

}

// ============================================================
// SAVE/LOAD GLOBAL HELPERS
// ============================================================
window._saveToSlot = function(slotNum, saveType) {
  if (saveType === 'save') {
    if (game.saveGame(slotNum)) {
      game.showMessage('Game saved to Slot ' + slotNum + '!');
    } else {
      game.showMessage('Failed to save game!');
    }
  } else {
    if (game.saveConfig(slotNum)) {
      game.showMessage('Config saved to Slot ' + slotNum + '!');
    } else {
      game.showMessage('Failed to save config!');
    }
  }
  window._closeSavePanel();
};

window._loadFromSlot = function(slotNum, saveType) {
  if (saveType === 'save') {
    if (game.loadGame(slotNum)) {
      game.showMessage('Game loaded from Slot ' + slotNum + '!');
    } else {
      game.showMessage('Failed to load game from Slot ' + slotNum + '!');
    }
  } else {
    if (game.loadConfig(slotNum)) {
      game.showMessage('Config loaded from Slot ' + slotNum + '!');
    } else {
      game.showMessage('Failed to load config from Slot ' + slotNum + '!');
    }
  }
  window._closeSavePanel();
};

window._exportSlotFile = function(saveType) {
  var storagePrefix = saveType === 'save' ? 'summongame_save_' : 'summongame_config_';
  for (var i = 1; i <= 3; i++) {
    if (localStorage.getItem(storagePrefix + i)) {
      game.exportSave(i, saveType);
      return;
    }
  }
  game.showMessage('No saves to export!');
};

window._importSlotFile = function(saveType) {
  var fileInput = document.getElementById('save-file-input');
  fileInput._saveType = saveType;
  fileInput.click();
};

window._closeSavePanel = function() {
  document.getElementById('save-load-subpanel').classList.remove('open');
  document.getElementById('pause-overlay').classList.remove('active');
  game.paused = false;
};

function drawLock(ctx, x, y, size) {
  ctx.fillStyle = '#aaa';
  ctx.fillRect(x - size * 0.2, y - size * 0.05, size * 0.4, size * 0.35);
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#666'; ctx.lineWidth = 1;
  ctx.strokeRect(x - size * 0.2, y - size * 0.05, size * 0.4, size * 0.35);
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#888'; ctx.lineWidth = size * 0.15;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x, y - size * 0.1, size * 0.25, Math.PI, 0); ctx.stroke();
  ctx.fillStyle = '#333';
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x, y + size * 0.1, size * 0.07, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
}

function drawPriestMenu(ctx, player, game) {
  var baseX = player.px - game.camX + TILE_SIZE;
  var baseY = player.py - game.camY - TILE_SIZE / 2;
  var slotW = TILE_SIZE, slotH = TILE_SIZE;
  var totalW = game.playerCount * slotW;
  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.6)';
  ctx.fillRect(baseX, baseY, totalW, slotH);
  ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.2)'; ctx.lineWidth = 1;
  ctx.strokeRect(baseX, baseY, totalW, slotH);
  var order = [player.playerIndex];
  for (var i = 0; i < game.playerCount; i++) { if (i !== player.playerIndex) order.push(i); }
  for (var oi = 0; oi < order.length; oi++) {
    var realIdx = order[oi];
    var sx = baseX + oi * slotW, sy = baseY;
    var cx = sx + slotW / 2, cy = sy + slotH / 2 - 4;
    ctx.fillStyle = ['#4488ff','#ff8844','#44ff44','#ff44ff'][realIdx] || '#ccc';
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx, cy, TILE_SIZE * 0.3, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
    ctx.font = '8px "Courier New", monospace'; ctx.fillStyle = '#fff'; ctx.textAlign = 'center';
    ctx.fillText(game.players[realIdx] ? game.players[realIdx].name : 'P' + (realIdx + 1), cx, sy + slotH - 4);
    if (oi === player.menuSelectedIndex) {
      ctx.strokeStyle = '#00ff00'; ctx.lineWidth = TILE_SIZE * 0.1;
      ctx.strokeRect(sx + 2, sy + 2, slotW - 4, slotH - 4);
    }
    if (game.players[realIdx] && !game.players[realIdx].alive) {
      var xsz = TILE_SIZE * 0.7, xcx = sx + slotW / 2, xcy = sy + slotH / 2;
      ctx.strokeStyle = '#ff0000'; ctx.lineWidth = 3;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(xcx - xsz / 2, xcy - xsz / 2); ctx.lineTo(xcx + xsz / 2, xcy + xsz / 2);
      ctx.moveTo(xcx + xsz / 2, xcy - xsz / 2); ctx.lineTo(xcx - xsz / 2, xcy + xsz / 2);
      ctx.stroke();
    }
    if (player.menuUseTimer > 0 && oi === player.menuSelectedIndex && !player.menuUseTapPending) {
      var pct = player.menuUseTimer / 1.0;
      ctx.strokeStyle = '#fff'; ctx.lineWidth = 2;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(cx, cy, TILE_SIZE * 0.4, -Math.PI / 2, -Math.PI / 2 + Math.PI * 2 * pct);
      ctx.stroke();
    }
  }
  ctx.textAlign = 'start';
}

function drawBananaPeel(ctx, x, y, rotation, size) {
  size = size || 22;
  ctx.save();
  ctx.translate(x, y);
  ctx.rotate(rotation);
  var ow = size * 0.9, oh = size * 0.35;
  var bg = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, -oh, 0, oh);
  bg.addColorStop(0, '#e8c840'); bg.addColorStop(1, '#c8a020');
  ctx.fillStyle = bg;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(0, 0, ow / 2, oh / 2, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#a08020'; ctx.lineWidth = 0.8;
  ctx.stroke();
  ctx.fillStyle = '#fffde0';
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(0, oh * 0.15, ow * 0.35, oh * 0.35, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#c8a020'; ctx.lineWidth = 0.5;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, -oh * 0.8); ctx.lineTo(0, oh * 0.8); ctx.stroke();
  ctx.fillStyle = '#6b4c00';
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-ow * 0.35, -oh * 0.7); ctx.lineTo(ow * 0.35, -oh * 0.7);
  ctx.lineTo(0, -oh * 0.3); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-ow * 0.35, oh * 0.7); ctx.lineTo(ow * 0.35, oh * 0.7);
  ctx.lineTo(0, oh * 0.3); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
  for (var b = 0; b < 3; b++) {
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(139,108,32,0.4)';
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse((b - 1) * ow * 0.2, oh * 0.05, size * 0.06, size * 0.04, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
  }
  ctx.restore();
}

const keys = {};
const keysJustPressed = {};
window.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
  const k = e.key.toLowerCase();
  if (rebindingPlayer !== null && rebindingAction !== null) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (k === 'escape') { cancelRebinding(); return; }
    finalizeRebinding(k);
    return;
  }
  if (!keys[k]) keysJustPressed[k] = true;
  keys[k] = true;
  if (e.key === 'F1') {
    e.preventDefault();
    game.debugMode = !game.debugMode;
    updateMonsterSelector(game);
    updateItemSelector(game);
  }
  if (game && game.players) {
    for (const p of game.players) {
      if (!p.alive) continue;
      if (k === p.keybinds.ability) { activateAbility(game, p); }
      if (k === p.keybinds.interact && !p.interacting && p.interactTarget) {
        if (p.interactType !== 'door') { p.interacting = true; p.interactTimer = 0; }
      }
    }
  }
});
window.addEventListener('keyup', e => {
  const k = e.key.toLowerCase();
  keys[k] = false;
  if (game && game.players) {
    for (const p of game.players) {
      if (k === p.keybinds.interact) { p.interacting = false; p.interactTimer = 0; }
    }
  }
});

function ejectPlayerFromWall(player, game) {
  var tx = player.tileX, ty = player.tileY;
  var dirs = [[0, -1], [0, 1], [-1, 0], [1, 0]];
  if (player.lastDx < 0) dirs = [[-1, 0], [1, 0], [0, -1], [0, 1]];
  else if (player.lastDx > 0) dirs = [[1, 0], [-1, 0], [0, -1], [0, 1]];
  else if (player.lastDy < 0) dirs = [[0, -1], [0, 1], [-1, 0], [1, 0]];
  else if (player.lastDy > 0) dirs = [[0, 1], [0, -1], [-1, 0], [1, 0]];
  for (var r = 1; r <= 5; r++) {
    for (var di = 0; di < dirs.length; di++) {
      var cx = tx + dirs[di][0] * r, cy = ty + dirs[di][1] * r;
      if (cx < 0 || cy < 0 || cx >= game.mapWidth || cy >= game.mapHeight) continue;
      var tile = game.tiles[cy][cx];
      if (tile !== 'O' && tile !== 'W') {
        player.px = cx * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2;
        player.py = cy * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2;
        player.tileX = cx; player.tileY = cy;
        return;
      }
    }
  }
}

function applyPriestEffect(game, priest) {
  var order = [priest.playerIndex];
  for (var i = 0; i < game.playerCount; i++) { if (i !== priest.playerIndex) order.push(i); }
  var target = game.players[order[priest.menuSelectedIndex]];
  if (!target) return;
  if (target.alive) {
    target.hp = Math.min(target.maxHp, target.hp + 40);
    game.showMessage(priest.name + ' healed ' + target.name + ' for 40 HP!');
    for (var i = 0; i < 6; i++) game.particles.push(new Particle(target.px, target.py, (Math.random()-0.5)*50, (Math.random()-0.5)*50, 0.5, '#00ff00'));
  } else {
    target.alive = true;
    target.hp = target.maxHp;
    target.invulnTimer = 3;
    game.showMessage(priest.name + ' revived ' + target.name + '!');
    for (var i = 0; i < 8; i++) game.particles.push(new Particle(target.px, target.py, (Math.random()-0.5)*60, (Math.random()-0.5)*60, 0.5, '#ffdd00'));
  }
}

function notifyRushMonsters(game) {
  for (const m of game.activeMonsters) {
    if (m.type === 'rush' && m.alive) {
      m.rushSpeedBonus += 20;
      m.speed += 20;
      for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
        game.particles.push(new Particle(
          m.px, m.py,
