          (Math.random() - 0.5) * 40, (Math.random() - 0.5) * 40,
          0.3, '#ffaa00'
        ));
      }
    }
  }
}

function activateAbility(game, player) {
  const p = player;
  const cls = PlayerClasses[p.playerClass];
  if (!cls) return;
  if (p.playerClass === 'helpfulpriest') return;
  if (p.playerClass === 'lockerguy') {
    var nearestDoor = null, nearestDist = Infinity;
    for (var di = 0; di < game.doorEntities.length; di++) {
      var door = game.doorEntities[di];
      var dx = door.tileX + 0.5 - p.px / TILE_SIZE;
      var dy = door.tileY + 0.5 - p.py / TILE_SIZE;
      var dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy) * TILE_SIZE;
      if (dist < TILE_SIZE * 1.5 && dist < nearestDist) { nearestDist = dist; nearestDoor = door; }
    }
    if (!nearestDoor) { game.showMessage('No door nearby!'); return; }
    if (nearestDoor.open) { nearestDoor.toggle(); }
    if (game.lockedDoor && game.lockedDoor.tiles.some(function(t) { return t.doorRef === nearestDoor; })) {
      if (game.lockedDoor.timer > 1.0) { game.showMessage('Lock still reinforced. Wait 1 second.'); return; }
      for (var ldi = 0; ldi < game.lockedDoor.tiles.length; ldi++) {
        var lt = game.lockedDoor.tiles[ldi];
        if (lt.doorRef) lt.doorRef.lockCooldown = 1.0;
      }
      game.lockedDoor = null;
      game.showMessage(p.name + ' removed the lock.');
      return;
    }
    if (game.lockedDoor) { game.showMessage('A door is already locked! Remove it first.'); return; }
    if (nearestDoor.lockCooldown > 0) { game.showMessage('This door was recently unlocked!'); return; }
    var lockedTiles = [];
    if (nearestDoor.groupMembers && nearestDoor.groupMembers.length > 0) {
      for (var gi = 0; gi < nearestDoor.groupMembers.length; gi++) {
        var member = nearestDoor.groupMembers[gi];
        lockedTiles.push({ doorRef: member, tileX: member.tileX, tileY: member.tileY });
      }
    } else {
      lockedTiles.push({ doorRef: nearestDoor, tileX: nearestDoor.tileX, tileY: nearestDoor.tileY });
    }
    game.lockedDoor = { tiles: lockedTiles, timer: 2.0, lockedByPlayerIndex: p.playerIndex };
    game.showMessage(p.name + ' locked the door! (2s)');
    return;
  }
  if (p.playerClass === 'allywarp') {
    if (p.allyWarpUses <= 0) { game.showMessage('No Ally Warp uses remaining this summon!'); return; }
    if (p.abilityCooldown > 0) return;
    var farthestAlly = null, farthestDist = 0;
    for (var ai = 0; ai < game.players.length; ai++) {
      var ally = game.players[ai];
      if (!ally.alive || ally === p) continue;
      var adx = (ally.px - p.px) / TILE_SIZE;
      var ady = (ally.py - p.py) / TILE_SIZE;
      var adist = Math.sqrt(adx * adx + ady * ady);
      if (adist > farthestDist) { farthestDist = adist; farthestAlly = ally; }
    }
    if (!farthestAlly) { game.showMessage('No ally available!'); return; }
    if (farthestDist < 15) { game.showMessage('No ally is far enough (need 15+ tiles, current: ' + (farthestDist|0) + ')!'); return; }
    for (var si = 0; si < 10; si++) game.particles.push(new Particle(p.px, p.py, (Math.random()-0.5)*100, (Math.random()-0.5)*100, 0.4, '#4488ff'));
    p.px = farthestAlly.px;
    p.py = farthestAlly.py;
    p.tileX = farthestAlly.tileX;
    p.tileY = farthestAlly.tileY;
    for (var si2 = 0; si2 < 10; si2++) game.particles.push(new Particle(p.px, p.py, (Math.random()-0.5)*100, (Math.random()-0.5)*100, 0.4, '#ffdd00'));
    p.allyWarpUses--;
    p.abilityCooldown = 5;
    game.showMessage(p.name + ' warped to ' + farthestAlly.name + '! (' + p.allyWarpUses + ' left)');
    notifyRushMonsters(game);
    return;
  }
  if (p.playerClass === 'bumdude') {
    if (p.abilityCooldown > 0) return;
    var nearestTrap = null, nearestTrapDist = Infinity;
    for (var ti = 0; ti < game.traps.length; ti++) {
      var trap = game.traps[ti];
      if (!trap.active) continue;
      var tdx = trap.x - p.px;
      var tdy = trap.y - p.py;
      var tdist = Math.sqrt(tdx * tdx + tdy * tdy);
      if (tdist < TILE_SIZE * 1.5 && tdist < nearestTrapDist) { nearestTrapDist = tdist; nearestTrap = trap; }
    }
    if (!nearestTrap) { game.showMessage('No trap nearby!'); return; }
    if (!p.hasSpace()) { game.showMessage('Inventory is full!'); return; }
    p.addToInventory(nearestTrap.item);
    var trapIdx = game.traps.indexOf(nearestTrap);
    if (trapIdx >= 0) game.traps.splice(trapIdx, 1);
    p.abilityCooldown = 1;
    game.showMessage(p.name + ' picked up ' + Items[nearestTrap.item].name);
    return;
  }
  if (cls.cooldown === 0) return;
  if (p.abilityCooldown > 0 || p.abilityActive) return;
  if (p.playerClass !== 'rerroler' && game.activeMonsters.every(function(m) { return !m.alive; })) { game.showMessage('Ability only usable when monster is spawned!'); return; }
  if (p.playerClass === 'timetraveler') {
    if (p.timeTravelUsed) return;
    p.timeTravelUsed = true;
    p.abilityCooldown = 9999;
    var count = 0;
    for (var j = 0; j < game.players.length; j++) {
      var op = game.players[j];
      if (!op.alive) continue;
      if (op.playerClass !== 'timetraveler') { op.abilityCooldown = 0; count++; }
    }
    game.showMessage(p.name + ' resetou ' + count + ' cooldown(s)!');
    notifyRushMonsters(game);
    return;
  }
  if (p.playerClass === 'redhacker') {
    var existing = false;
    for (var k = 0; k < game.activeClones.length; k++) {
      if (game.activeClones[k].playerIndex === p.playerIndex) { existing = true; break; }
    }
    if (existing) return;
    var tgt = null, minD = Infinity;
    for (var mi = 0; mi < game.activeMonsters.length; mi++) {
      var mm = game.activeMonsters[mi];
      if (!mm.alive) continue;
      var dxm = mm.px - p.px, dym = mm.py - p.py;
      var dm = Math.sqrt(dxm * dxm + dym * dym);
      if (dm < minD) { minD = dm; tgt = mm; }
    }
    if (!tgt) return;
    game.activeClones.push({ playerIndex: p.playerIndex, px: p.px, py: p.py, tileX: p.tileX, tileY: p.tileY, alive: true, timer: 8, targetMonster: tgt, playerColor: ['#4488ff','#ff8844','#44ff44','#ff44ff'][p.playerIndex] || '#ccc', path: null });
    notifyRushMonsters(game);
    return;
  }
  if (p.playerClass === 'mightytinkerer') {
    game.activeZones = game.activeZones.filter(function(z) { return z.ownerIndex !== p.playerIndex; });
    game.activeZones.push({ x: p.px, y: p.py, radius: 3.5 * TILE_SIZE, timer: 6, ownerIndex: p.playerIndex, bonusSpeed: 30, monsterSpeed: 60 });
    p.abilityCooldown = 45;
    game.showMessage(p.name + ' created Engineering Zone!');
    notifyRushMonsters(game);
    return;
  }
  if (p.playerClass === 'wiredgirl') {
    if (p.wiredGirlUsed) { game.showMessage('Already used this summon!'); return; }
    if (game.activeMonsters.every(function(m) { return !m.alive; })) { game.showMessage('Ability only usable when monster is spawned!'); return; }
    var uncollected = game.artifactEntities.filter(function(a) { return !a.collected; });
    if (uncollected.length === 0) { game.showMessage('No uncollected artifacts to teleport to!'); return; }
    var target = uncollected[Math.floor(Math.random() * uncollected.length)];
    for (var si = 0; si < 10; si++) game.particles.push(new Particle(p.px, p.py, (Math.random()-0.5)*100, (Math.random()-0.5)*100, 0.4, '#4488ff'));
    p.px = target.tileX * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2;
    p.py = target.tileY * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE / 2;
    p.tileX = target.tileX; p.tileY = target.tileY;
    for (var si2 = 0; si2 < 10; si2++) game.particles.push(new Particle(p.px, p.py, (Math.random()-0.5)*100, (Math.random()-0.5)*100, 0.4, '#ffdd00'));
    p.wiredGirlUsed = true;
    p.abilityCooldown = 9999;
    game.showMessage(p.name + ' teleported to artifact!');
    notifyRushMonsters(game);
    return;
  }
  if (p.playerClass === 'untitledgeneralist') {
    for (var oi = 0; oi < game.players.length; oi++) {
      var op2 = game.players[oi];
      if (!op2.alive) continue;
      op2.invulnTimer += 2;
      for (var pi2 = 0; pi2 < 6; pi2++) game.particles.push(new Particle(op2.px, op2.py, (Math.random()-0.5)*60, (Math.random()-0.5)*60, 0.5, '#ffdd00'));
    }
    p.abilityCooldown = 36;
    game.showMessage(p.name + ' granted 2s invulnerability to all!');
    notifyRushMonsters(game);
    return;
  }
  if (p.playerClass === 'clown') {
    game.traps.push({ x: p.px, y: p.py, item: 'banana_peel', active: true, stun: 4, trapColor: '#e8c840', trapRadius: 12, lifetime: 15, timer: 15, rotation: Math.random() * Math.PI * 2, tauntDuration: 0, tauntTimer: 0, tauntedMonster: null });
    p.abilityCooldown = 30;
    game.showMessage(p.name + ' slipped a banana peel!');
    notifyRushMonsters(game);
    return;
  }
  if (p.playerClass === 'rerroler') {
    if (p.rerrolerUsed) { game.showMessage('Already rerolled this summon!'); return; }
    if (!game.activeMonsters.every(function(m) { return !m.alive; })) { game.showMessage('Only usable when monster is not spawned!'); return; }
    var toReroll = game.shopItems.filter(function(s) { return !s.sold; });
    if (toReroll.length === 0) { game.showMessage('No shops to reroll!'); return; }
    var itemKeys = Object.keys(Items);
    for (var si3 = 0; si3 < toReroll.length; si3++) {
      var newItem = itemKeys[Math.floor(Math.random() * itemKeys.length)];
      toReroll[si3].itemKey = newItem;
      toReroll[si3].price = Items[newItem].price;
    }
    p.rerrolerUsed = true;
    p.abilityCooldown = 9999;
    game.showMessage(p.name + ' rerolled ' + toReroll.length + ' shops!');
    notifyRushMonsters(game);
    return;
  }
  p.abilityActive = true;
  p.abilityTimer = cls.duration;
  p.abilityCooldown = cls.cooldown + cls.duration;
  if (p.playerClass === 'scout') { p.speed = p.baseSpeed * 1.5; }
  if (p.playerClass === 'commandyeller') {
    for (var ji = 0; ji < game.players.length; ji++) {
      var op3 = game.players[ji];
      if (!op3.alive) continue;
      op3.commandYellMult = 0.25;
      op3.commandYellTimer = 7;
    }
  }
  if (p.playerClass === 'dyingguy') {
    p.invisible = true;
    p.invisibleTimer = 8;
    p.abilityCooldown = 20;
    for (var mi2 = 0; mi2 < game.activeMonsters.length; mi2++) {
      var m2 = game.activeMonsters[mi2];
      if (m2.state === 'chase' || m2.state === 'search') {
        m2.state = 'patrol';
        m2.path = null;
        m2.chaseTimer = 0;
        m2.searchTimer = 0;
      }
    }
    notifyRushMonsters(game);
    return;
  }
  if (p.playerClass === 'annoyinglooper') {
    var orientation = Math.abs(p.lastDx) > Math.abs(p.lastDy) ? 'vertical' : 'horizontal';
    var wallTiles = [];
    var cx = p.tileX, cy = p.tileY;
    if (orientation === 'horizontal') { wallTiles.push({ x: cx - 1, y: cy }, { x: cx, y: cy }, { x: cx + 1, y: cy }); }
    else { wallTiles.push({ x: cx, y: cy - 1 }, { x: cx, y: cy }, { x: cx, y: cy + 1 }); }
    game.activeWalls.push({ px: p.px, py: p.py, tileX: cx, tileY: cy, orientation: orientation, timer: 5, duration: 5, tiles: wallTiles });
  }
  if (p.playerClass === 'vex') {
    p.phasing = true;
    p.phasingTimer = 1.5;
  }
  notifyRushMonsters(game);
}

// ============================================================
// UI HANDLERS
// ============================================================
function updateHUD(game) {
  const hud = document.getElementById('hud');
  const night = game.nightTransition > 0.3 ? 'NIGHT' : 'DAY';
  const mt = game.monsterSpawnType;
  const mtName = MonsterTypes[mt] ? MonsterTypes[mt].name : 'None';
  let html = '';
  for (const p of game.players) {
    const clsName = PlayerClasses[p.playerClass] ? PlayerClasses[p.playerClass].name : 'Delivery Guy';
    html += '<span style="color:' + (['#4488ff','#ff8844','#44ff44','#ff44ff'][p.playerIndex] || '#fff') + '">' + p.name + '</span>: ';
    html += 'HP:' + Math.round(p.hp) + '/' + p.maxHp + ' ';
    html += clsName + ' ';
    html += (p.alive ? '' : 'DEAD ') + '<br>';
  }
  html += 'Monster: ' + mtName + '<br>';
  html += 'Difficulty: ' + game.difficulty + ' | Round: ' + game.roundNumber + '<br>';
  html += 'Artefatos: ' + game.collectedArtifacts + '/' + game.artifactCount + '<br>';
  html += 'Speed Bonus: +' + game.getSpeedBonusPercent() + '%<br>';
  html += 'Coins: ' + game.currency + '<br>';
  html += 'Estado: ' + night;
  hud.innerHTML = html;
}

function updateAbilityHUD(game) {
  const hud = document.getElementById('ability-hud');
  let html = '';
  let anyAbility = false;
  for (const p of game.players) {
    if (!p.alive) continue;
    const cls = PlayerClasses[p.playerClass];
    if (!cls) continue;
    if (cls.cooldown === 0 && p.playerClass !== 'lockerguy' && p.playerClass !== 'helpfulpriest') continue;
    anyAbility = true;
    const color = ['#4488ff','#ff8844','#44ff44','#ff44ff'][p.playerIndex] || '#ccc';
    html += '<div style="text-align:left; margin-bottom:4px;">';
    html += '<span style="color:' + color + '">' + p.name + '</span> ';
    html += cls.abilityName + ' [' + p.keybinds.ability.toUpperCase() + '] ';
    if (p.abilityActive) {
      const pct2 = (p.abilityTimer / cls.duration) * 100;
      html += '<span style="color:#ff0">Active ' + p.abilityTimer.toFixed(1) + 's</span>';
    } else if ((p.playerClass === 'timetraveler' && p.timeTravelUsed) || (p.playerClass === 'wiredgirl' && p.wiredGirlUsed) || (p.playerClass === 'rerroler' && p.rerrolerUsed) || (p.playerClass === 'helpfulpriest' && p.helpfulpriestUsed)) {
      html += '<span style="color:#f66">Used</span>';
    } else if (p.abilityCooldown > 0) {
      html += '<span style="color:#f66">CD ' + p.abilityCooldown.toFixed(1) + 's</span>';
    } else {
      html += '<span style="color:#6f6">Ready</span>';
    }
    html += '</div>';
  }
  if (!anyAbility) {
    hud.style.display = 'none';
    return;
  }
  hud.style.display = 'block';
  hud.innerHTML = html;
}

function updateHotbarHUD(game) {
  const container = document.getElementById('hotbar-hud');
  container.classList.toggle('vertical', game.hotbarLayout === 'vertical');
  container.style.gap = (game.hotbarLayout === 'vertical' ? Math.floor(game.hotbarGap / 2) : game.hotbarGap) + 'px';
  const abilityHud = document.getElementById('ability-hud');
  if (game.hotbarLayout === 'vertical') {
    abilityHud.style.bottom = 'auto';
    abilityHud.style.top = '50px';
    abilityHud.style.left = '10px';
  } else {
    abilityHud.style.bottom = '10px';
    abilityHud.style.top = 'auto';
    abilityHud.style.left = '10px';
  }
  const alivePlayers = game.players.filter(p => p.alive);
  const numPlayers = alivePlayers.length;
  if (numPlayers === 0) { container.innerHTML = ''; return; }
  const isVertical = game.hotbarLayout === 'vertical';
  const slotSize = isVertical ? 36 : Math.max(18, Math.min(80, Math.floor(55 * game.hotbarSize / 100)));
  const fontSizeSlot = isVertical ? 20 : Math.max(10, Math.min(40, Math.floor(28 * game.hotbarSize / 100)));
  const fontSizeName = isVertical ? 16 : Math.max(8, Math.min(30, Math.floor(23 * game.hotbarSize / 100)));
  const barWidth = isVertical ? 40 : Math.max(20, Math.min(80, Math.floor(50 * game.hotbarSize / 100)));
  const barHeight = isVertical ? 6 : Math.max(4, Math.min(14, Math.floor(10 * game.hotbarSize / 100)));
  const slotGap = isVertical ? 4 : 8;

  function getInteractionText(p) {
    if (!p.interactTarget) return null;
    const key = p.keybinds.interact.toUpperCase();
    if (p.interacting && p.interactType !== 'door') {
      if (p.interactType === 'altar') return 'Invocando no Altar\u2026';
      if (p.interactType === 'artifact') return 'Coletando ' + (p.interactTarget.displayName || 'Artefato') + '\u2026';
      if (p.interactType === 'shop') return game.debugMode ? 'Pegando ' + Items[p.interactTarget.itemKey].name + ' FREE\u2026' : 'Comprando ' + Items[p.interactTarget.itemKey].name + '\u2026';
      if (p.interactType === 'trash') return 'Vendendo item equipado\u2026';
    }
    if (p.interactType === 'door') return 'Pressione ' + key + ' para abrir/fechar a Porta';
    if (p.interactType === 'altar') return 'Pressione ' + key + ' para invocar no Altar';
    if (p.interactType === 'artifact') return 'Pressione ' + key + ' para coletar ' + (p.interactTarget.displayName || 'o Artefato');
    if (p.interactType === 'shop') return game.debugMode ? 'Pressione ' + key + ' para pegar ' + Items[p.interactTarget.itemKey].name + ' FREE' : 'Pressione ' + key + ' para comprar ' + Items[p.interactTarget.itemKey].name;
    if (p.interactType === 'trash') return 'Pressione ' + key + ' para vender o item equipado';
    return null;
  }

  let html = '';
  for (const p of alivePlayers) {
    const color = ['#4488ff','#ff8844','#44ff44','#ff44ff'][p.playerIndex] || '#ccc';
    const interactText = getInteractionText(p);

    if (isVertical) {
      html += '<div style="display:flex;flex-direction:row;gap:8px;align-items:center;width:100%;flex-shrink:0;margin-bottom:' + Math.floor(game.hotbarGap / 2) + 'px;">';
      html += '<div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:' + slotGap + 'px;align-items:center;">';
      html += '<span style="color:' + color + ';font-size:' + fontSizeName + 'px;min-width:' + (fontSizeName * 2.5) + 'px;text-align:center;">' + p.name.substring(0, 3) + '</span>';
      for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
        const item = p.inventory[i];
        const selected = i === p.selectedSlot;
        const it = item ? Items[item] : null;
        html += '<div style="width:' + slotSize + 'px;height:' + slotSize + 'px;background:' + (selected ? '#2a2a3e' : '#111') + ';border:1px solid ' + (selected ? '#4fc3f7' : '#333') + ';border-radius:' + Math.floor(slotSize * 0.15) + 'px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;flex-shrink:0;">';
        if (it) {
          html += '<canvas class="item-canvas" data-item="' + item + '" width="' + slotSize + '" height="' + slotSize + '" style="width:' + slotSize + 'px;height:' + slotSize + 'px;"></canvas>';
        } else {
          html += '<span style="color:#333;font-size:' + fontSizeSlot + 'px;">-</span>';
        }
        html += '</div>';
      }
      html += '</div>';
      if (interactText) {
        html += '<div style="background:rgba(0,0,0,0.7);border-radius:4px;padding:4px 8px;font-size:11px;font-family:\'Courier New\',monospace;color:#fff;pointer-events:none;white-space:nowrap;">' + interactText + '</div>';
      }
      html += '</div>';
    } else {
      html += '<div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:4px;align-items:center;' +
        'margin-right:' + game.hotbarGap + 'px;flex-shrink:0;">';
      if (interactText) {
        html += '<div style="background:rgba(0,0,0,0.7);border-radius:4px;padding:4px 8px;font-size:11px;font-family:\'Courier New\',monospace;color:#fff;pointer-events:none;white-space:nowrap;text-align:center;">' + interactText + '</div>';
      }
      html += '<div style="display:flex;gap:' + slotGap + 'px;align-items:center;">';
      html += '<span style="color:' + color + ';font-size:' + fontSizeName + 'px;min-width:' + (fontSizeName * 2.5) + 'px;text-align:center;">' + p.name.substring(0, 3) + '</span>';
      for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
        const item = p.inventory[i];
        const selected = i === p.selectedSlot;
        const it = item ? Items[item] : null;
        html += '<div style="width:' + slotSize + 'px;height:' + slotSize + 'px;background:' + (selected ? '#2a2a3e' : '#111') + ';border:1px solid ' + (selected ? '#4fc3f7' : '#333') + ';border-radius:' + Math.floor(slotSize * 0.15) + 'px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;flex-shrink:0;">';
        if (it) {
          html += '<canvas class="item-canvas" data-item="' + item + '" width="' + slotSize + '" height="' + slotSize + '" style="width:' + slotSize + 'px;height:' + slotSize + 'px;"></canvas>';
        } else {
          html += '<span style="color:#333;font-size:' + fontSizeSlot + 'px;">-</span>';
        }
        html += '</div>';
      }
      html += '</div>';
      html += '</div>';
    }
  }
  container.innerHTML = html;

  // Render ItemIcons on hotbar canvases
  var canvases = container.querySelectorAll('.item-canvas');
  for (var ci = 0; ci < canvases.length; ci++) {
    var cvs = canvases[ci];
    var itemKey = cvs.getAttribute('data-item');
    if (!itemKey) continue;
    var iconKey = Items[itemKey] ? Items[itemKey].icon : itemKey;
    var iconFn = ItemIcons[iconKey];
    if (!iconFn) continue;
    var ctx2 = cvs.getContext('2d');
    var cw = cvs.width;
    var ch = cvs.height;
    ctx2.clearRect(0, 0, cw, ch);
    var iconSize = Math.min(cw, ch) * 0.7;
    iconFn(ctx2, cw / 2, ch / 2, iconSize);
  }
}

function updateHotbarInGame(game) { updateHotbarHUD(game); }
updateHotbarHUD = updateHotbarHUD;

function updateMapEditor(game) {
  // Build legend
  const legend = document.getElementById('map-legend');
  let html = '';
  for (const [ch, label] of Object.entries(LEGEND_LABELS)) {
    const color = LEGEND_COLORS[ch];
    html += '<span style="color:' + color + '">' + ch + ' = ' + label + '</span>';
  }
  legend.innerHTML = html;
}

function updatePreview() {
  const ta = document.getElementById('map-editor-textarea');
  const pre = document.querySelector('#map-editor-preview pre');
  const text = ta.value;
  let html = '';
  for (const ch of text) {
    if (ch === '\n') { html += '\n'; continue; }
    if (ch === ' ') { html += ' '; continue; }
    const color = LEGEND_COLORS[ch] || '#ccc';
    html += '<span style="color:' + color + '">' + ch + '</span>';
  }
  pre.innerHTML = html;
}

// ============================================================
// HOTKEY CONFIG SYSTEM
// ============================================================
let rebindingPlayer = null;
let rebindingAction = null;

function buildHotkeyConfig(game) {
  const container = document.getElementById('hotkey-config');
  let html = '<h3>Key Bindings</h3>';
  for (let i = 0; i < game.playerCount; i++) {
    const p = game.players[i];
    if (!p) continue;
    const color = ['#4488ff','#ff8844','#44ff44','#ff44ff'][i] || '#ccc';
    const keysList = ['up','down','left','right','interact','ability','use']
      .map(a => p.keybinds[a].toUpperCase()).join(', ');
    const isExpanded = game.expandedHotkeyPlayers && game.expandedHotkeyPlayers.has(i);

    html += '<button class="player-hotkey-toggle' + (isExpanded ? ' expanded' : '') + '" data-player="' + i + '">';
    html += '<span style="color:' + color + ';font-weight:bold;">P' + (i + 1) + '</span>';
    html += ' (' + p.name + ') <span style="color:#666;font-size:11px;">[' + keysList + ']</span>';
    html += '</button>';

    if (isExpanded) {
      html += '<div class="player-hotkey-content">';
      html += '<h3 style="color:' + color + ';margin:0 0 6px;font-size:13px;">' + p.name + ' - Key Bindings</h3>';
      const actions = [
        { key: 'up', label: 'Move Up' },
        { key: 'down', label: 'Move Down' },
        { key: 'left', label: 'Move Left' },
        { key: 'right', label: 'Move Right' },
        { key: 'interact', label: 'Interact' },
        { key: 'ability', label: 'Ability' },
        { key: 'use', label: 'Use/Cycle Item' }
      ];
      for (const act of actions) {
        html += '<div class="hotkey-row">';
        html += '<span class="action">' + act.label + '</span>';
        html += '<button class="key-btn" data-player="' + i + '" data-action="' + act.key + '">' + p.keybinds[act.key].toUpperCase() + '</button>';
        html += '</div>';
      }
      html += '</div>';
    }
  }
  container.innerHTML = html;

  container.querySelectorAll('.player-hotkey-toggle').forEach(btn => {
    btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const pi = parseInt(btn.dataset.player);
      if (!game.expandedHotkeyPlayers) game.expandedHotkeyPlayers = new Set();
      if (game.expandedHotkeyPlayers.has(pi)) {
        game.expandedHotkeyPlayers.delete(pi);
      } else {
        game.expandedHotkeyPlayers.add(pi);
      }
      buildHotkeyConfig(game);
    });
  });

  container.querySelectorAll('.key-btn').forEach(btn => {
    btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const pi = parseInt(btn.dataset.player);
      const act = btn.dataset.action;
      rebindingPlayer = pi;
      rebindingAction = act;
      container.querySelectorAll('.key-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('waiting'));
      btn.classList.add('waiting');
      btn.textContent = '...';
    });
  });

  checkKeyConflicts();
}

function finalizeRebinding(keyName) {
  if (rebindingPlayer === null || rebindingAction === null) return false;
  const classSelectVisible = document.getElementById('class-select').style.display !== 'none';
  const p = game.players[rebindingPlayer];
  if (classSelectVisible) {
    // Class selection screen visible: update preGameKeybinds
    if (!preGameKeybinds[rebindingPlayer]) {
      preGameKeybinds[rebindingPlayer] = { ...DEFAULT_KEYBINDS[rebindingPlayer] };
    }
    preGameKeybinds[rebindingPlayer][rebindingAction] = keyName;
    // Update player keybinds too if they exist already
    if (p) {
      keys[p.keybinds[rebindingAction]] = false;
      p.keybinds[rebindingAction] = keyName;
    }
    // Update the waiting button text
    const waitingBtn = document.querySelector('.key-btn.waiting');
    if (waitingBtn) {
      waitingBtn.textContent = keyName.toUpperCase();
      waitingBtn.classList.remove('waiting');
    }
    rebindingPlayer = null;
    rebindingAction = null;
    checkKeyConflicts();
    return true;
  }
  if (!p) {
    rebindingPlayer = null; rebindingAction = null; return false;
  }
  keys[p.keybinds[rebindingAction]] = false;
  p.keybinds[rebindingAction] = keyName;
  rebindingPlayer = null;
  rebindingAction = null;
  buildHotkeyConfig(game);
  return true;
}

function cancelRebinding() {
  if (rebindingPlayer !== null) {
    const classSelectVisible = document.getElementById('class-select').style.display !== 'none';
    const isPreGame = classSelectVisible || !game || !game.players || !game.players[rebindingPlayer];
    if (isPreGame) {
      const waitingBtns = document.querySelectorAll('.key-btn.waiting');
      waitingBtns.forEach(btn => {
        const pi = parseInt(btn.dataset.player);
        const act = btn.dataset.action;
        const kb = preGameKeybinds[pi] || DEFAULT_KEYBINDS[pi];
        btn.textContent = (kb[act] || '').toUpperCase();
        btn.classList.remove('waiting');
      });
    }
    if (!classSelectVisible && game && game.players) {
      buildHotkeyConfig(game);
    }
  }
  rebindingPlayer = null;
  rebindingAction = null;
}

function checkKeyConflicts() {
  const btns = document.querySelectorAll('.key-btn:not(.waiting)');
  const keyMap = {};
  for (const btn of btns) {
    const key = btn.textContent.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!key || key === '...') continue;
    if (!keyMap[key]) keyMap[key] = [];
    keyMap[key].push(btn);
  }

  for (const btns of Object.values(keyMap)) {
    const playerGroups = {};
    for (const btn of btns) {
      const pi = btn.dataset.player;
      if (!playerGroups[pi]) playerGroups[pi] = [];
      playerGroups[pi].push(btn);
    }
    const uniquePlayers = Object.keys(playerGroups);
    if (uniquePlayers.length < 2) continue;

    const key = btns[0].textContent.trim().toUpperCase();
    for (const [pi, buttons] of Object.entries(playerGroups)) {
      const playerName = 'P' + (parseInt(pi) + 1);
      const conflicts = [];
      for (const [otherPi, otherBtns] of Object.entries(playerGroups)) {
        if (otherPi === pi) continue;
        const otherName = 'P' + (parseInt(otherPi) + 1);
        for (const ob of otherBtns) {
          const actionLabel = {
            'up': 'Move Up', 'down': 'Move Down',
            'left': 'Move Left', 'right': 'Move Right',
            'interact': 'Interact', 'ability': 'Ability',
            'use': 'Use/Cycle Item'
          }[ob.dataset.action] || ob.dataset.action;
          conflicts.push(otherName + ' usa ' + key + ' para ' + actionLabel);
        }
      }
      for (const btn of buttons) {
        btn.classList.add('conflict');
        btn.title = 'Conflito: ' + conflicts.join('; ');
      }
    }
  }

  for (const btn of btns) {
    const key = btn.textContent.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!key || key === '...') continue;
    const list = keyMap[key] || [];
    const playerSet = new Set(list.map(b => b.dataset.player));
    if (playerSet.size < 2) {
      btn.classList.remove('conflict');
      btn.title = '';
    }
  }
}

let game = null;

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  game = new Game();
  game.start();
  updateMapEditor(game);

  // Config save/load buttons
  document.getElementById('btn-save-config').addEventListener('click', () => {
    game.showSaveSlots('config');
  });
  document.getElementById('btn-load-config').addEventListener('click', () => {
    game.showLoadSlots('config');
  });

  // Settings button
  document.getElementById('settings-btn').addEventListener('click', () => {
    const panel = document.getElementById('settings-panel');
    panel.classList.toggle('open');
    if (panel.classList.contains('open')) {
      buildHotkeyConfig(game);
      document.getElementById('hotbar-size-value').textContent = game.hotbarSize;
      document.getElementById('btn-hotbar-layout').textContent = 'Layout: ' + (game.hotbarLayout === 'horizontal' ? 'Horizontal' : 'Vertical');
      document.getElementById('hotbar-gap-range').value = game.hotbarGap;
      document.getElementById('hotbar-gap-value').textContent = game.hotbarGap;
    }
  });

  document.getElementById('btn-close-settings').addEventListener('click', () => {
    document.getElementById('settings-panel').classList.remove('open');
    cancelRebinding();
  });

  document.getElementById('btn-restart').addEventListener('click', () => {
    game.restart();
    document.getElementById('settings-panel').classList.remove('open');
  });

  document.getElementById('btn-save-game').addEventListener('click', () => {
    document.getElementById('settings-panel').classList.remove('open');
    game.showSaveSlots('save');
  });

  document.getElementById('btn-load-game').addEventListener('click', () => {
    document.getElementById('settings-panel').classList.remove('open');
    game.showLoadSlots('save');
  });

  document.getElementById('btn-map-editor').addEventListener('click', () => {
    game.openMapEditor();
  });

  document.getElementById('btn-apply-map').addEventListener('click', () => {
    game.applyMapFromEditor();
  });

  document.getElementById('btn-reset-map').addEventListener('click', () => {
    game.resetDefaultMap();
  });

  document.getElementById('btn-cancel-map').addEventListener('click', () => {
    game.closeMapEditor();
  });

  document.getElementById('btn-hotbar-minus').addEventListener('click', () => {
    game.hotbarSize = Math.max(50, game.hotbarSize - 10);
    document.getElementById('hotbar-size-value').textContent = game.hotbarSize;
    updateHotbarHUD(game);
  });

  document.getElementById('btn-hotbar-plus').addEventListener('click', () => {
    game.hotbarSize = Math.min(200, game.hotbarSize + 10);
    document.getElementById('hotbar-size-value').textContent = game.hotbarSize;
    updateHotbarHUD(game);
  });

  document.getElementById('btn-hotbar-layout').addEventListener('click', () => {
    game.hotbarLayout = game.hotbarLayout === 'horizontal' ? 'vertical' : 'horizontal';
    const layoutBtn = document.getElementById('btn-hotbar-layout');
    layoutBtn.textContent = 'Layout: ' + (game.hotbarLayout === 'horizontal' ? 'Horizontal' : 'Vertical');
    updateHotbarHUD(game);
  });


  document.getElementById('hotbar-gap-range').addEventListener('input', () => {
    const val = parseInt(document.getElementById('hotbar-gap-range').value);
    game.hotbarGap = val;
    document.getElementById('hotbar-gap-value').textContent = val;
    updateHotbarHUD(game);
  });

  // Live preview update
  document.getElementById('map-editor-textarea').addEventListener('input', updatePreview);

  // Close panels on Escape
  window.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
    if (e.key === 'Escape') {
      document.getElementById('settings-panel').classList.remove('open');
      game.closeMapEditor();
      cancelRebinding();
      window._closeSavePanel();
    }
  });

  // File import handler
  document.getElementById('save-file-input').addEventListener('change', function() {
    if (this.files && this.files[0]) {
      var saveType = this._saveType || 'save';
      // Determine slot: find first empty or use slot 1
      var storagePrefix = saveType === 'save' ? 'summongame_save_' : 'summongame_config_';
      var targetSlot = 3;
      for (var s = 1; s <= 3; s++) {
        if (!localStorage.getItem(storagePrefix + s)) { targetSlot = s; break; }
      }
      game.importSave(targetSlot, saveType, this.files[0]);
      this.value = '';
      window._closeSavePanel();
    }
  });

  // Resize canvas display
  function resizeCanvas() {
    const container = document.getElementById('game-container');
    const maxW = container.clientWidth - 40;
    const maxH = container.clientHeight - 40;
    const canvas = document.getElementById('game');
    const scaleW = maxW / canvas.width;
    const scaleH = maxH / canvas.height;
    const scale = Math.min(scaleW, scaleH, 1.5);
    canvas.style.width = (canvas.width * scale) + 'px';
    canvas.style.height = (canvas.height * scale) + 'px';
  }
  window.addEventListener('resize', resizeCanvas);
  resizeCanvas();
});

function updateMonsterSelector(game) {
  var sel = document.getElementById('monster-selector');
  if (!sel) return;
  if (!game.debugMode) { sel.style.display = 'none'; return; }
  sel.style.display = 'block';
  var html = '<b>Monster Selector:</b><br>';
  var types = Object.keys(MonsterTypes);
  for (var ti = 0; ti < types.length; ti++) {
    var tkey = types[ti];
    var mt = MonsterTypes[tkey];
    var selClass = (game.selectedMonsterType === tkey) ? 'color:#0f0;' : 'color:#aaa;';
    html += '<span style="cursor:pointer;' + selClass + '" ';
    html += 'onclick="selectDebugMonster(\'' + tkey + '\')">';
    html += (game.selectedMonsterType === tkey ? '> ' : '  ') + mt.name + '</span><br>';
  }
  if (game.selectedMonsterType) {
    html += '<br><span style="cursor:pointer;color:#f66;" onclick="selectDebugMonster(null)">[Random]</span>';
  }
  sel.innerHTML = html;
}

function selectDebugMonster(typeKey) {
  game.debugSelectedMonster = typeKey;
  game.selectedMonsterType = typeKey;
  game.showMessage(typeKey ? 'Next summon: ' + MonsterTypes[typeKey].name : 'Next summon: Random');
  updateMonsterSelector(game);
}

function updateItemSelector(game) {
  var sel = document.getElementById('item-selector');
  if (!sel) return;
  if (!game.debugMode) { sel.style.display = 'none'; return; }
  sel.style.display = 'block';
  var html = '<b>Item Selector (give all):</b><br>';
  var keys = Object.keys(Items);
  for (var ki = 0; ki < keys.length; ki++) {
    var ikey = keys[ki];
    var it = Items[ikey];
    html += '<span style="cursor:pointer;color:#ff0;" ';
    html += 'onclick="giveDebugItem(\'' + ikey + '\')">';
    html += '  ' + it.name + ' ($' + it.price + ')</span><br>';
  }
  sel.innerHTML = html;
}

function giveDebugItem(itemKey) {
  if (!game || !game.debugMode) return;
  for (var pi = 0; pi < game.players.length; pi++) {
    var p = game.players[pi];
    if (!p.alive) continue;
    if (!p.hasSpace()) { game.showMessage(p.name + "'s inventory is full!"); continue; }
    p.addToInventory(itemKey);
  }
  game.showMessage('Gave ' + Items[itemKey].name + ' to all players!');
}
</script>
